  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在变分积分框架下，研究线性增长密度的正则性问题，特别是全平面上的Euler-Lagrange方程解是否为仿射函数（Bernstein性质），这是非线性椭圆方程和变分法中的经典问题。

Method: 通过对线性增长且严格凸的密度函数g施加定量积分条件（∫₀^∞ t g''(t) dt < ∞），结合全平面R²上整解的分析，证明Bernstein性质；当条件减弱时，利用部分性结论给出退化的Bernstein结果。

Result: 条件∫₀^∞ t g''(t) dt < ∞足以保证全平面上Euler-Lagrange方程的整解为仿射函数；在该条件被弱化的情况下，仍能得到部分Bernstein性质。

Conclusion: 积分条件∫₀^∞ t g''(t) dt < ∞是保证线性增长密度变分积分Bernstein性质的充分条件，且该条件是相对精致的——减弱后只能得到部分Bernstein结果，说明该条件在某种意义下是最优的。

Abstract: We consider entire solutions $u: \mathbb{R}^2 \rightarrow \mathbb{R}$ of the Euler-Lagrange equation associated to the variational integral $\int_Ω g(|\nabla u|)\,dx$ with a strictly convex density $g: [0,\infty)\rightarrow \mathbb{R}$ being of linear growth. We show that the condition $\int_{0}^{\infty} t\,g''(t)\,dt < \infty$ implies the Bernstein property, which means that $u$ must be an affine function. If this condition on g is weakened, we still have some partial Bernstein results.

</details>


### [97] [Local smoothing for rough wave equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01440)
*Jan Rozendaal,Robert Schippa*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有C^r系数波动方程的局部光滑估计，在二维和三维中，对C^{1,1}系数恢复了此前仅对光滑系数成立的尖峰L^p-L^q局部光滑界，并由此得到非平凡的L^p-L^p局部光滑估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有局部光滑估计主要针对光滑系数的波动方程，而实际应用中系数往往仅具有有限正则性（如C^r）。本文旨在将尖峰（sharp）的L^p-L^q局部光滑界从光滑系数推广到粗糙系数（特别是C^{1,1}）情形，弥合粗糙介质中波动方程估计的理论空白。

Method: 利用与粗糙波传播相关的双线性限制估计（bilinear restriction estimates），推导出具有端点导数损失的L^p-L^q解耦不等式（decoupling inequalities），进而证明局部光滑估计。具体处理了二维和三维情形，针对C^{1,1}系数建立了与光滑系数情形一致的尖峰估计。

Result: 在二维和三维中，对于C^{1,1}系数的波动方程，恢复了此前仅对光滑系数成立的尖峰L^p-L^q局部光滑界。该结果由更强的带端点导数损失的L^p-L^q解耦不等式推出，并作为推论获得了非平凡的L^p-L^p局部光滑估计。

Conclusion: 本文证明了粗糙系数（C^{1,1}）波动方程的局部光滑估计可以达到与光滑系数相同的最优阶，表明这类估计对系数正则性的要求可以显著放宽。同时，所建立的双线性限制估计与解耦不等式方法为后续研究更一般粗糙系数或更高维情形提供了技术框架。

Abstract: We prove local smoothing estimates for wave equations with $C^{r}$ coefficients, relying on bilinear restriction estimates associated with rough wave propagation. In two and three dimensions, for $C^{1,1}$ coefficients we recover the sharp $L^p$-$L^q$ local smoothing bounds previously established for smooth coefficients. These are implied by stronger $L^{p}$-$L^{q}$ decoupling inequalities with endpoint derivative loss. As a corollary, we obtain non-trivial $L^{p}$-$L^{p}$ local smoothing estimates as well.

</details>


### [98] [Fully Nonlinear Evolution equations: From Critical Integrability to Schauder Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01455)
*Junior da Silva Bessa,José Erivamberto L. Oliveira,Patrícia Renata Pereira Regis*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了完全非线性抛物方程在斜导数边界条件下的粘性解直到边界的尖锐正则性理论，通过紧致性结合多项式逼近的方法，根据源项积分性、边界数据光滑性和算子系数振荡性，分别建立了Log-Lipschitz连续性、最优C^{1+α'}边界估计和Schauder型估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究完全非线性抛物方程在斜导数边界条件下解的边界正则性，这是偏微分方程正则性理论中的核心问题。尽管Dirichlet和Neumann边界条件下的正则性理论已相当成熟，但斜导数边界条件（非切向边界条件）下的尖锐边界正则性结果仍然缺乏系统性研究，特别是在临界情形（f∈L^{n+2}）和BMO临界情形下的最优正则性。

Method: 采用紧致性方法（compactness method）结合多项式逼近（polynomial approximation）技术。根据源项f的积分性、边界数据g的光滑性以及算子F系数的振荡性分层建立正则性理论：在临界情形f∈L^{n+2}时获得对数Lipschitz连续性；当f∈L^p（p>n+2）时建立最优C^{1+α'}边界估计；在更高正则性水平下，通过对算子F和边界数据施加适当假设，证明Schauder型估计。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 临界情形f∈L^{n+2}下，解直到边界的对数Lipschitz连续性；(2) f∈L^p（p>n+2）时，最优的C^{1+α',(1+α')/2}边界估计；(3) 在更高正则性假设下，Schauder型估计；(4) 作为副产品，源项属于BMO空间这一临界情形下得到抛物C^{1,Log-Lip}正则性。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了完全非线性抛物方程在斜导数边界条件下粘性解在临界和超临界积分情形下的尖锐边界正则性，填补了该方向上边界正则性理论的空白，其嵌入的紧致性+多项式逼近方法为后续研究提供了有力的分析框架。

Abstract: We investigate the sharp regularity up to the boundary for viscosity solutions of fully nonlinear parabolic equations with oblique derivative boundary conditions given by \begin{equation*} \left\{ \begin{array}{rclcl} F(D^2u,x,t) - u_{t} &=& f(x,t) & \text{in} & Q_{1}^{+}, \\ β(x,t) \cdot Du &=& g(x,t) & \text{on} & Q_{1}^{*}. \end{array} \right. \end{equation*} The regularity theory is developed according to the integrability of the source term, the smoothness of the boundary data, and the oscillation of the coefficients of the operator \(F\), using a compactness method combined with polynomial approximation. In the borderline case \(f\in L^{n+2}\), we obtain Log-Lipschitz continuity of solutions up to the boundary. Under stronger integrability, specifically when \(f\in L^{p}\) for some \(p>n+2\), we establish optimal \(C^{1+α',\,\frac{1+α'}{2}}\) boundary estimates. At a higher regularity level, we prove Schauder-type estimates under appropriate assumptions on the operator \(F\) and the boundary data. As a byproduct, we obtain parabolic \(C^{1,\mathrm{Log\text{-}Lip}}\) regularity in the critical borderline case where the source term belongs to BMO space.

</details>


### [99] [Free Boundary Regularity for Non-Convex Fully Nonlinear Alt-Phillips Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01508)
*Alvis Zahl*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了完全不具有凸性假设的全非线性Alt-Phillips自由边界问题，建立了平坦自由边界的光滑性结果。该结果即使在γ=0的全非线性障碍问题中也是新的。方法基于部分Hodograph变换将自由边界转化为固定边界，并对由此产生的全非线性退化方程证明了新的Harnack不等式和Schauder估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alt-Phillips自由边界问题在全非线性框架下缺乏系统性研究，尤其是在不施加凸性假设的情况下，已有的正则性理论极为有限。通过将该问题转化为固定边界问题，并建立新的分析工具（Harnack不等式和Schauder估计），可以突破凸性限制并推广到更广泛的问题类别。

Method: 采用部分Hodograph变换将自由边界问题转化为固定边界问题，将原自由边界转换为固定边界，从而得到全非线性退化方程。随后对退化方程证明Harnack不等式以获得平坦度的改进（关键的正则性提升步骤），并建立相应的线性化方程的Schauder估计。

Result: 在不依赖F的凸性假设的条件下，证明了平坦自由边界的光滑性。该结果对γ∈(-1/3,1)成立，并且对于γ=0的全非线性障碍问题也是全新的。所建立的Harnack不等式和Schauder估计本身是新的分析结果，具有独立的学术价值。

Conclusion: 本文通过部分Hodograph变换和全新的分析工具，成功地在无凸性假设下建立了全非线性Alt-Phillips自由边界问题的平坦自由边界光滑性。这一方法论的创新为更广泛的全非线性自由边界问题的正则性理论奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the fully nonlinear Alt-Phillips free boundary problem \begin{equation} F(D^2u) = u^γχ_{\{u>0\}}, \end{equation} for $γ\in (-1/3,1)$ without any convexity assumption on $F$. We establish that flat free boundaries are smooth. This result is new even for the fully nonlinear obstacle problem when $γ= 0$.
  Our approach is based on a partial hodograph transform, which converts the free boundary into a fixed boundary and produces a fully nonlinear degenerate equation. For this equation we prove a Harnack inequality, yielding an improvement of flatness, and a Schauder estimate for the associated linearized equation. Both estimates appear to be new and are of independent interest.

</details>


### [100] [The Riemann Problem for a 3x3 Generalized Chaplygin Gas System with Variable Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01580)
*Thomas Allen,Ella Kim,Jonathan Nunes,Rita Xing,Charis Tsikkou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究带广义Chaplygin压力的3×3守恒律系统的Riemann问题，分析其波结构、分类Riemann解，并针对经典解不存在的奇异解（δ-激波）进行严格分析。通过Dafermos最大熵耗散准则研究解的适定性，并利用Dafermos正则化与球面爆裂技术构造孤立过压缩δ-激波的粘性剖面，证明其作为零粘性极限存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于压力依赖于额外输运变量的广义Chaplygin气体（p(ρ,v)=-A(v)/ρ^α），Riemann问题的波结构比经典情况更复杂。当经典解（激波、稀疏波、接触间断）不存在时，需要处理奇异解（δ-激波）。作者希望建立该系统下奇异解的严格数学理论基础，并通过粘性消失方法验证其物理合理性。

Method: 系统研究3×3守恒律系统的Riemann问题，分类解析解的结构；在经典Dirac delta框架下验证奇异解满足分布意义下的守恒律；与Nedeljkov的shadow-wave构造进行对比；应用Dafermos最大熵耗散准则研究解的适定性和物理相关性；通过Lax-Friedrichs数值模拟验证解析结果。对于孤立过压缩δ-激波，假设α为有理数时，采用Dafermos正则化结合球面爆裂技术，在三个方向坐标图中构造奇异拼接轨道（左外轨道、爆裂边界上的中轨道、右外轨道），并证明小粘性扰动下该拼接轨道可微扰为异宿轨道。

Result: 成功分类了该系统的Riemann解，确认了奇异解在分布意义下满足守恒律；与shadow-wave构造互补，给出了同一奇异解的两种等价描述。Dafermos最大熵耗散准则能够选择物理相关解。关于孤立过压缩δ-激波，证明了在足够小的正粘性下，奇异拼接轨道可扰动为异宿轨道，从而孤立过压缩δ-激波是自相似Dafermos粘性剖面的零粘性极限。

Conclusion: 该研究为带广义Chaplygin压力的3×3守恒律系统建立了完备的Riemann解理论，特别在奇异解方面取得了严格的分析结果。通过Dafermos粘性剖面方法，证明了孤立过压缩δ-激波具有物理可实现性（零粘性极限），为这类广义Chaplygin气体的复杂波结构提供了数学上自洽的框架。

Abstract: We consider the Riemann problem for a 3x3 system of conservation laws with generalized Chaplygin pressure $p(ρ,v)=-\frac{A(v)}{ρ^α}$, $0<α\leq 1$, where the pressure depends on an additional transported variable. We analyze the system's wave structure and classify the Riemann solutions. Whenever classical solutions consisting of shocks, rarefaction waves, and contact discontinuities fail to exist, singular solutions arise. We verify that these satisfy the conservation laws in the distributional sense within the classical Dirac delta framework, and compare them with Nedeljkov's shadow-wave construction, giving two complementary descriptions of the same singular solution. We further study admissibility via the Dafermos maximum entropy dissipation principle, with several examples showing how it selects the physically relevant solution. Lax-Friedrichs simulations illustrate the Riemann wave patterns and provide a comparison with the analytical results.
  To construct viscous profiles for the isolated overcompressive $δ$-shock, we assume $α\in\mathbb{Q}\cap(0,1]$ and apply the Dafermos regularization together with a spherical blow-up. Working in three directional charts, we construct the reduced singular concatenation consisting of the left outer orbit, the middle orbit on the blown-up boundary, and the right outer orbit. We then prove that, for sufficiently small positive viscosity, this singular concatenation perturbs to a heteroclinic orbit. Consequently, the isolated overcompressive $δ$-shock is realized as the zero-viscosity limit of a family of self-similar Dafermos viscous profiles.

</details>


### [101] [Asymptotic Flocking Behavior in Particle and Kinetic Systems with biological Memory Term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01627)
*Yishuo Wang,Yawei Wei*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究含生物记忆项的Cucker-Smale型群集模型，发现记忆使得群集现象更难实现：微观层面需更强的通信核下界和记忆偏差指数衰减条件；介观层面群集速率衰减变慢，通信强度参数β的可取范围变小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生物记忆对群集（flocking）行为的影响，验证"记忆使群集更困难"这一结论，并刻画在微观与介观两个层面上的具体条件变化。

Method: 在Cucker-Smale模型中引入记忆变量h(t)，其动力学为dh/dt=x(t)+v(t)-λh(t)，体现路径、速度依赖及指数衰减。分别在微观（粒子级）和介观（动力学层级）层面分析群集现象的条件与速率。

Result: 微观层面：群集成为条件性的，需要通信核ψ(t)具有多项式下界且记忆偏差H(t)指数衰减。介观层面：群集速率呈指数或代数衰减，通信强度参数β的可取范围缩小。

Conclusion: 记忆项的引入使群集条件更苛刻、收敛速率更慢、参数空间更窄，即记忆总体上阻碍了群集现象的形成。

Abstract: We investigate a Cucker-Smale type flocking model with a biological memory term and conclude that memory makes flocking harder. The model introduces $h(t)$ to describe memory, with dynamics $\frac{dh}{dt}=x(t)+v(t)-λh(t)$ reflecting path, velocity dependence and exponential decay. In microscopic level, the flocking phenomenon becomes conditional, requires communication kernel $ψ(t)$ has polynomial lower bound and memory deviation $H(t)$ decays exponentially. In mesoscopic level, flocking rates exhibit exponential or algebraic decay and the range of communication strength $β$ become smaller.

</details>


### [102] [Smooth Failure of Boundary Unique Continuation for Harmonic Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01683)
*Guolin Qin,Weicheng Zhan*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文对每个n≥3构造了一个在ℝⁿ₊上半空间中调和、在边界紧致无处稠密正测度子集上完全边界jet为零的非平凡C^∞函数，否定了Bourgain-Wolff在1990年遗留的边界唯一延拓问题的光滑情形，并证伪了Nadirashvili关于单位球边界奇异集的光滑猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bourgain和Wolff在1990年提出了边界唯一延拓问题，其光滑情形（即边界jet在正测度集上完全消失时函数是否恒为零）一直悬而未决。同时，Nadirashvili在1997年提出了关于单位球中边界奇异集的猜想。本文将这两个著名开放问题均予以否证，填补了该领域的长期空白。

Method: 构造的核心在二维进行，产生n=3的情形，然后通过圆柱提升得到更高维例子。证明的关键在于发现非局部椭圆方程的"隐藏灵活性原理"：微观修改可以对边界外部数据产生宏观控制。具体而言，通过跨尺度迭代的1/2-Laplacian定量校正机制，在正测度集上产生平坦的非局部Cauchy数据。此外，在三维情形通过Möbius-Kelvin变换将该反例从上半空间转移到单位球。

Result: 对每个n≥3，构造出了满足上述性质的非平凡调和函数；例外集在固定边界立方体中可占据任意大的比例。该结果否证了Bourgain-Wolff边界唯一延拓问题的光滑情形，推翻了Nadirashvili的单位球边界奇异集猜想，也驳斥了Logunov-Malinnikova和Lin记录的梯度-only版本。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了非局部椭圆方程的双重特性：长程相互作用既驱动唯一延拓的刚性，也能提供使刚性在光滑类别中失效的灵活性。这为理解调和函数边界行为与非局部算子之间的深层联系提供了新的视角。

Abstract: For every $n\geq 3$, we construct a nonconstant real-valued function $U\in C^\infty(\overline{\mathbb R^n_+})$, harmonic in the upper half-space, whose complete boundary jet vanishes on a compact nowhere dense subset of $\partial\mathbb R^n_+$ of positive $(n-1)$-dimensional measure. The essential construction takes place in two dimensions and yields the case $n=3$; higher-dimensional examples follow by cylindrical lifting. In every dimension, the exceptional set may occupy an arbitrarily large proportion of a fixed boundary cube. This resolves, in the negative, the smooth case of the boundary unique-continuation problem left open by Bourgain and Wolff in 1990 \cite[p.~260]{BourgainWolff1990}. In dimension three, a Möbius--Kelvin transfer gives the corresponding counterexample in the unit ball. It disproves Nadirashvili's smooth unit-ball conjecture on boundary singular sets \cite[Conjecture~4, p.~232]{Nadirashvili1997} and, a fortiori, disproves the gradient-only formulation subsequently recorded by Logunov and Malinnikova \cite[Section~7.4]{LogunovMalinnikova2020} and by Lin \cite[Conjecture~3, pp.~15--16]{Lin2020Current}.
  The proof uncovers a hidden flexibility principle for nonlocal elliptic equations: microscopic modifications can exert macroscopic control over exterior data. A quantitative correction mechanism for the half-Laplacian, iterated across scales, produces flat nonlocal Cauchy data on a set of positive measure. Thus nonlocality has a striking dual character: the same long-range interaction that drives unique-continuation rigidity can also furnish the flexibility through which that rigidity fails in the smooth category.

</details>


### [103] [Exponential mixing for Korteweg-de Vries equation with localized noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01863)
*Yuxuan Chen,Shengquan Xiang,Zhifei Zhang,Jia-Cheng Zhao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了在环面L²空间中，随机强迫且弱阻尼KdV方程的指数混合性。噪声有界、局部化、在高频段退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机强迫色散偏微分方程（如KdV方程）的遍历性与混合性，特别是如何将渐近紧致性、控制理论和遍历性联系起来。

Method: 结合一般概率框架、通过正态形变换实现的KdV非线性光滑性及其线性化分析，以及局部强迫的系统稳定性证明。

Result: 在L²(𝕋)中证明了随机强迫且弱阻尼KdV方程具有指数混合性质，尽管噪声是有界、局部化且高频退化的。

Conclusion: 该研究是连接渐近紧致性、控制理论与色散PDE随机强迫系统的遍历性和混合性研究系列工作的延续，证明了在相对苛刻的噪声条件下仍能获得指数混合性。

Abstract: We establish exponential mixing for the randomly forced and weakly damped KdV equation in $L^2(\mathbb{T})$. The noise is bounded, localized, and degenerate in high frequencies. Our proof relies on a general probabilistic framework in [11,33], nonlinear smoothing for KdV and its linearization via normal form transformation, and stabilization of the system by localized force. This paper continues a series of works connecting asymptotic compactness, control theory, and ergodicity and mixing for randomly forced dispersive PDEs.

</details>


### [104] [Generalized solution and Weak-Strong uniqueness for a barotropic Euler-Riesz system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01872)
*Nilasis Chaudhuri*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究环面上Euler-Riesz系统（可压缩Euler方程耦合Riesz核的非局部排斥力），通过Caffarelli-Silvestre延拓将非局部作用转化为带有额外变量的局部恒等式，引入全局时间的耗散解概念，并证明对所有β∈(0,2)和γ>1都成立的弱（测度值）-强唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euler-Riesz系统包含压力项和非局部排斥力的耦合，其非局部相互作用给数学分析带来困难。本文旨在通过将非局部项局部化，为大初始能量数据建立全局时间的解的概念，并研究其与强解的唯一性关系。

Method: 利用Riesz核K是分数阶拉普拉斯算子(-Δ)^{-β/2}的核这一关键事实，借助Caffarelli-Silvestre延拓将非局部力改写为一个局部应力张量的迹，在引入的一个额外变量中实现非局部相互作用的局部化表达。基于排斥核下总能量的强制性，定义了全局时间的耗散解，并通过适当调整相对能量的方法进行弱（测度值）-强唯一性证明。

Result: 对所有阶数β∈(0,2)和所有γ>1（独立地），在强解存在的任意时间区间上，具有相同初始数据的每个耗散解都与该强解完全一致，且所有缺陷消失。

Conclusion: 通过Caffarelli-Silvestre延拓实现了Euler-Riesz系统非局部项的局部化，建立了耗散解的概念并证明了弱-强唯一性，结果对β和γ的取值独立成立。

Abstract: We study the Euler--Riesz system on the torus $\mathbb T^d$, $d=2,3$: the compressible Euler equations with barotropic pressure $p(\varrho)=a\varrho^γ$ ($γ>1$, $a>0$), coupled to a repulsive nonlocal force ($\approx \varrho \nabla_x K \ast \varrho$) with Riesz kernel $K(x)\propto|x|^{β-d}$ of order $β\in(0,2)$. Since $K$ is the kernel of the inverse fractional Laplacian $(-Δ)^{-β/2}$, we recast the force through the Caffarelli--Silvestre extension as the trace of a local stress tensor, replacing the nonlocal interaction by a local identity in one extra variable. For a repulsive kernel the total energy is coercive, and we use this to introduce a notion of global-in-time \emph{dissipative solution} for arbitrarily large finite-energy data. Our main result is weak (measure-valued)--strong uniqueness, for every order $β\in(0,2)$ and every $γ>1$ independently: on any interval on which a strong solution exists, every dissipative solution with the same initial data coincides with it and all defects vanish. The proof rests on a suitable adaptation of relative energy.

</details>


### [105] [Unified theory for regularity persistence of vortex patch boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02033)
*Marc Magaña*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个统一的局部理论，研究二维主动标量方程中涡旋斑块边界Sobolev正则性的持续性，涵盖2D Euler方程、广义SQG方程（0<β<1）以及准地转浅水方程。在核函数的自然假设下，证明初始边界满足H^3正则性和弧弦条件时，轮廓动力学方程在C([0,T];H^3(T))中存在唯一局部解；在更强的核函数可积性条件下，还得到H^2解的局部存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要建立统一的局部理论来研究一类广泛的二维主动标量方程（包括2D Euler、广义SQG和准地转浅水方程）中涡旋斑块边界的Sobolev正则性持续性，此前这类结果分散在不同方程的特殊情形中，缺乏统一的处理框架。

Method: 将Sobolev能量估计应用于轮廓方程，结合对弧弦量的定量控制。对径向卷积核K(|x-y|)施加自然假设（光滑性、原点附近可积性、单调性和多项式增长），并在不同可积性条件下分别推导H^3和H^2解的局部存在性。

Result: 在核函数的自然假设下，证明初始边界属于H^3(T)且满足弧弦条件时，轮廓动力学方程存在唯一局部解于C([0,T];H^3(T))；在更强的可积性条件下，获得H^2局部解的存在性。

Conclusion: 建立了涵盖多类重要二维主动标量方程的涡旋斑块边界正则性持续性的统一局部理论，为理解这些方程在轮廓动力学层面的共性提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We establish a unified local theory for the persistence of Sobolev regularity of vortex patch boundaries in a family of two-dimensional active scalar equations with radial convolution kernels $K(|x-y|)$. The class includes the 2D Euler equation, the generalized SQG equation in the locally integrable range $0<β<1$, and the quasi-geostrophic shallow water equation. Under natural assumptions on $K$ (smoothness, integrability near the origin, monotonicity, and polynomial growth), we prove that if the initial boundary belongs to $H^3(\mathbb T)$ and satisfies the arc-chord condition, then the contour dynamics equation admits a unique local solution in $C([0,T];H^3(\mathbb T))$. Under a stronger integrability condition on the kernel, we also obtain local existence of $H^2$ solutions. The proof combines Sobolev energy estimates for the contour equation with quantitative control of the arc-chord quantity.

</details>


### [106] [Balance between degenerate elliptic operators and coercive Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02198)
*Isabeau Birindelli,Giulio Galise,Hitoshi Ishii*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一类全非线性退化椭圆方程在Dirichlet边界或边界爆破条件下的边值问题，探讨解的存在性、不存在性以及当γ趋于零时解的渐近行为，并揭示了算子的退化性与Hamiltonian的超线性增长之间的相互作用如何导致依赖于算子的不同现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一类具有退化性全非线性椭圆算子与超线性Hamiltonian相结合的边值问题，这类问题在遍历控制理论等领域有重要应用。理解算子的退化程度与Hamiltonian增长之间的竞争关系对解的定性行为的影响，是一个自然且具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 本文采用先验Lipschitz估计作为核心工具，结合上解/下解方法，对Dirichlet问题和边界爆破问题分别建立解的存在性与不存在性准则。通过分析不同指标i下算子的退化程度，揭示i=N与i<N时现象的差异。

Result: 研究表明：遍历二分律仅在i=N（即拉普拉斯算子情形）时成立；当i<N时出现新的现象——在温和条件下，即使在单点爆破解也不存在；对于Dirichlet齐次边界问题，需要对方程中的函数f施加尺寸条件才能保证解的存在性。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了解的存在性与解的渐近行为对算子指标i的强依赖性，系统地刻画了退化算子与超线性Hamiltonian之间主导权的竞争如何决定问题的可解性，为该类问题的进一步研究提供了完整的理论框架。

Abstract: For $p>1$, we consider the boundary value problem for fully nonlinear degenerate elliptic equations $-λ_i(D^2u)+|Du|^p+γu=f(x)$ in bounded domains with Dirichlet or boundary blow-up conditions; here $λ_i(D^2u)$ denotes the $i$-th eigenvalue of the Hessian. We study existence and nonexistence of solutions together with the asymptotic behaviour of the solutions when $γ$ goes to zero. A priori Lipschitz estimates play an important role. The interplay between the operator's degeneracy and the superlinear growth of the Hamiltonian gives rise to phenomena that are very different depending on which of the two terms dominates, e.g. the ergodic dichotomy takes place only when $i=N$, while new phenomena arise for $i<N$ in which case, under mild conditions, solutions that blow up even in just one point do not exist, and conditions on the size of $f$ must be imposed for the existence of solutions to the Dirichlet problem with homogeneous boundary condition.

</details>


### [107] [The narrow escape problem in arbitrary dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02212)
*Louis Carillo,Tony Lelièvre,Thomas Normand,Gabriel Stoltz,Urbain Vaes*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一般域中任意维数（≥2）的窄逃逸问题，采用拟平稳分布方法分析亚稳态，推导了孔洞尺寸趋近零时平均逃逸时间的渐近展开和逃逸位置的分布律，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 窄逃逸问题是研究熵亚稳态的典型范例，源于生物和化学系统的分析需求。现有研究多局限于特定域或维度，本文旨在一般域中、任意维数下系统性地研究该问题。

Method: 采用拟平稳分布（quasi-stationary distribution）方法处理亚稳态，推导平均逃逸时间的渐近展开式以及逃逸位置的概率分布律，在孔洞尺寸趋于零的极限下进行渐近分析，并利用专用蒙特卡洛数值方法进行验证。

Result: 获得了在任意维度（≥2）一般域中，孔洞尺寸趋零时平均逃逸时间的渐近展开以及逃逸位置的渐近分布律，并通过蒙特卡洛数值模拟验证了理论结果的正确性。

Conclusion: 拟平稳分布方法为一般域中的窄逃逸问题提供了统一的渐近分析框架，所得理论结果与数值模拟一致，为生物和化学系统中的亚稳态分析提供了有力工具。

Abstract: The narrow escape problem is a prototypical example for studying entropic metastability, motivated by the analysis of biological and chemical systems. The problem concerns the determination of the exit time and position of a Brownian particle trapped in a domain with a reflecting boundary pierced by narrow holes. Our goal is to investigate this problem in a general domain in any dimension (greater than or equal to two), using the quasi-stationary distribution approach to metastability. In particular, we derive the asymptotic expansion of the mean exit time and the law of the exit position in the limit where the hole sizes tend to zero. Our analytical predictions are illustrated by numerical simulations, using dedicated Monte Carlo techniques.

</details>


### [108] [Linear non-divergence parabolic equations in non-cylindrical space-time sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02221)
*Luan Hoang,Akif Ibragimov*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一般非圆柱形时空集合上的二阶非散度型线性抛物方程。对齐次问题，获得了解的指数、幂次及中间速率的衰减估计；对非齐次问题，给出了解在抛物边界数据和外力函数意义下的全时及渐近估计。分析依赖于一般时空集合的精细性质和一种自举Growth引理的迭代方案，其中时间步长不必恒定，可适应直径的增长率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有抛物方程理论通常假设空间域是圆柱形的（即固定时间截面不变），而实际应用中许多问题的空间区域随时间变化，且当时间趋于无穷时截面直径可能无界。作者旨在将经典解估计理论推广到更一般的非圆柱形时空集合。

Method: 利用一般时空集合的精细几何性质，发展了一种迭代方案以自举（bootstrap）Growth引理。该迭代的时间步长非恒定，而是根据截面直径的增长率自适应调整，从而在直径可能无界增长的情况下获得解的衰减估计。

Result: 对于齐次问题，在不同场景下分别获得了指数衰减、幂次衰减以及介于二者之间的中间衰减速率。对于非齐次问题，建立了基于抛物边界数据和外力函数的全时估计以及时间趋于无穷时的渐近估计。

Conclusion: 该工作将线性抛物方程解的估计理论从经典的圆柱形（或固定域）情形推广到截面直径随时间无界增长的非圆柱形时空区域，拓宽了该理论的适用范围。

Abstract: We study linear parabolic equations of the second order in non-divergence form in a general set which is non-cylindrical with respect to the spatial and time variables. The restriction of the set on any bounded time interval is bounded, but the spatial diameter of each fixed-time cross section can be unbounded as time tends to infinity. For homogeneous problems, we obtain exponential, power and other intermediate decaying rates for the solutions in different scenarios. For inhomogeneous problems, we obtain all-time and asymptotic, as time tend to infinity, estimates for the solutions in terms of the data on the parabolic boundary and forcing functions. The analysis requires subtle properties of general space-time sets and an iteration scheme to bootstrap the Growth Lemma. The time steps for such an iteration need not be constant and are adapted to the growth of the diameter.

</details>


### [109] [The critical exponent for the three-dimensional semilinear wave equation with strong damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02278)
*Wenhui Chen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文确定了带强阻尼的三维半线性波动方程的关键幂指数为p=7/3，解开了2014年提出的开放问题：p>7/3时小初值产生全局解，1<p≤7/3时存在任意小的光滑紧支撑数据导致有限时间爆破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决2014年提出的关于带强阻尼的三维半线性波动方程临界幂指数的开放问题，该问题此前尚未有定论。

Method: 利用完整速度基本解的正性结合降维公式得到正半直线核，替代强阻尼波动所不具备的有限传播性质；由此建立无需径向对称或初值逐点符号假设的非线性下界抛物迭代。在临界幂指数处，通过移动壳上的精细切片论证将边界对数增益转化为爆破所需的增长。

Result: 确定了临界幂指数为p_crit=7/3，并分别证明了次临界与临界情形（1<p≤7/3）的有限时间爆破以及超临界情形（p>7/3）的小初值全局解存在性。

Conclusion: 该结果完整刻画了带强阻尼的三维半线性波动方程幂非线性项的临界指标，为相关退化耗散波动系统的临界现象提供了新的方法论工具。

Abstract: In this manuscript, we determine the critical exponent for the three-dimensional semilinear wave equation with strong damping and thereby resolve an open problem posed in 2014. The threshold for the power nonlinearity $|u|^p$ is
  \begin{align*}
  p=p_{\mathrm{crit}}=\frac{7}{3}.
  \end{align*}
  Sufficiently small data generate global in-time solutions for $p>\frac{7}{3}$, whereas there exist arbitrarily small smooth compactly supported data whose solutions blow up in finite time for $1<p\leqslant\frac{7}{3}$. Positivity of the full velocity fundamental solution, combined with a dimension-descent formula, yields a positive half-line kernel and replaces the finite propagation property unavailable for strongly damped waves. This leads to a nonlinear lower-bound parabolic iteration without radial symmetry or pointwise sign assumptions on the initial data. At the critical power, a refined slicing argument on moving shells converts the borderline logarithmic gain into the growth required for blow-up.

</details>


### [110] [Equilibration versus Localization in a Diffusion-Relaxation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02307)
*Aseel AlNajjar,Hoyoun Kim,Athanasios Tzavaras*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类扩散-弛豫系统，严格刻画了从均衡化到局部化的转变条件。在扩散占优时证明解收敛于均匀平衡态（借助能量耗散结构和不变区域估计）；在有效扩散较弱时，通过几何奇异摄动理论构造自相似解（等价于自治动力系统中的异宿轨道），从而证明局部化现象的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散-弛豫系统中均衡化与局部化之间的转化虽已通过形式渐近分析和线性稳定性分析有所研究，但对非线性现象的严格理解仍然有限，尤其在高维情形。本文旨在填补这一空白，给出从均衡化到局部化转变的严格数学刻画。

Method: 在均衡化区间，利用能量耗散结构和不变区域估计建立向常数平衡态的收敛性。在局部化区间，研究自相似解，将其存在性问题转化为一个自治动力系统中的异宿轨道构造问题，并通过几何奇异摄动理论（GSPT）证明该异宿轨道的存在性。

Result: 在均衡化区间严格证明了解收敛于常数平衡态；在局部化区间，通过几何奇异摄动理论成功构造了对应于局部化解的自相似轮廓，从而实现了从均衡化到局部化转变的严格刻画。

Conclusion: 本文为扩散-弛豫系统中均衡化与局部化现象的转变提供了严格的非线性分析框架，该结果在高维情形下尤其具有重要意义。

Abstract: We consider a diffusion-relaxation system and investigate the conditions on parameters leading to equilibration versus localization. When the diffusion is dominant, solutions converge toward homogeneous equilibria. By contrast, when the effective diffusion is weak, localization emerges. Such behaviors have been studied for various models through formal asymptotic arguments and linearized stability analysis, but rigorous understanding of the associated nonlinear phenomena remains limited, particularly in higher dimensions. In the equilibration regime, we establish convergence toward constant equilibria by exploiting an energy dissipation structure and invariant-region estimates. In the localization regime, we study self-similar solutions and transform the problem of their existence into an autonomous dynamical system. The existence of self-similar profiles associated to localizing solutions is reduced to the construction of a heteroclinic orbit for an autonomous dynamical system. Their existence is obtained through an application of geometric singular perturbation theory. Our analysis provides a rigorous characterization of the transition from equilibration to localization.

</details>


### [111] [Strong ill-posedness for the MHD system in the supercritical regime: inviscid and viscous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02330)
*Zhenyu Wan,Weikui Ye,Zhaoyang Yin*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究三维不可压缩磁流体力学（MHD）方程在超临界Sobolev空间中的Cauchy问题，通过引入新颖的"Magnetic-solo ansatz"几何构造，在超临界Sobolev空间中建立了方程（无论是否含Laplacian耗散）的范数暴涨（norm inflation），揭示了系统在该正则性水平上的强不适定性。对于理想MHD系统，范数暴涨仅发生在磁场b的H^s范数（0<s<5/2）上，而速度场u的H^s范数保持一致有界，展示了由磁场单独驱动的非对称不适定性行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维不可压缩MHD方程在次临界Sobolev空间中已建立局部适定性，但超临界正则性区域在很大程度上仍是开放问题。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索超临界Sobolev空间中MHD方程的不适定性机理，特别是磁场在打破适定性中的独特作用。

Method: 引入一种新颖的几何构造方法，称为"Magnetic-solo ansatz"，通过该构造在超临界Sobolev空间中证明MHD方程（含与不含Laplacian耗散两种情形）的范数暴涨现象，从而实现强不适定性的证明。该方法的关键在于使范数暴涨仅发生磁场分量上，而速度场保持有界。

Result: 在超临界Sobolev空间中，不可压缩MHD方程（有无Laplacian耗散两种情形）均出现范数暴涨，表明系统在该正则性水平上的强不适定性。对于理想MHD系统，在0<s<5/2时，范数暴涨仅发生在磁场的H^s范数上，速度场的H^s范数保持一致有界，揭示了磁场驱动的非对称不适定性行为。

Conclusion: 研究表明超临界不适定性可以完全由磁场单独驱动，凸显了磁场在适定性崩溃中的本质作用。该工作填补了不可压缩MHD超临界正则性理论的重要空白，并揭示了流体动力学与磁动力学截然不同的控制机制。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with the Cauchy problem for the 3D incompressible magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) equations in supercritical Sobolev spaces. It is well known that the system is locally well-posed in subcritical Sobolev spaces, whereas the supercritical regime remains largely open. In this work, we establish norm inflation for the incompressible MHD equations, both with and without Laplacian dissipation, in supercritical Sobolev spaces, thereby revealing strong ill-posedness of the system at this regularity level. A distinctive feature of our approach is the introduction of a novel geometric construction, termed the ``Magnetic-solo ansatz'', through which, for the ideal MHD system, norm inflation occurs exclusively in the magnetic field $b$ in $H^s$ with $0<s<\frac{5}{2}$, while the $H^s$-norm of the velocity field $u$ remains uniformly bounded. This asymmetric behavior shows that supercritical ill-posedness can be driven exclusively by the magnetic field, highlighting its essential role in the breakdown of well-posedness. Our findings fill a significant gap in the supercritical regularity theory for incompressible MHD and shed light on the distinct mechanisms governing the fluid and magnetic dynamics.

</details>


### [112] [On countable subsets of solutions of nonlinear higher-order ODEs and elliptic PDEs with indefinite operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02532)
*Pablo Alvarez-Caudevilla,Jonathan D. Ev ans,Victor A. Galaktionov*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过拼接/匹配论证方法，研究了来自反应扩散、薄膜和动力系统理论的高阶非线性ODE及具不定非强制算子的椭圆PDE，证明了它们在无穷远处边界条件F(∞)=0下存在可数无穷多个解，并建立了与两条周期轨道相关的两族基本正主导解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于具有非奇非线性（如F^4=-F+F^2等）的高阶非线性ODE和椭圆PDE，传统的Lusternik-Schnirel'man（L-S）亏格/范畴变分理论无法适用，因为相关的泛函是非奇的。因此需要发展新的方法（拼接/匹配论证）来证明可数无穷多解的存在性。

Method: 采用拼接（gluing）/匹配（matching）论证方法：将来自两条周期轨道Γ_max和Γ_min的任意有限样本通过指数衰减尾部拼接在一起，构造出具有任意复杂度的同宿轨解。该方法适用于经典和拟线性退化ODE（四阶和六阶），以及相关的多调和椭圆方程（如Δ²F=-F+F², Δ²F=-F-Δ(F²-F), Δ³F=F-F²等）。

Result: 证明了上述高阶非线性ODE（包括具有奇非线性、非光滑非线性以及Hamilton系统的方程）和椭圆PDE（径向情形）至少存在两个基本的可数解族F_1,2，它们与周期轨道Γ_max/min相联系。通过拼接这些周期轨道上的任意有限样本，可得到R^4中可数无限个任意复杂度的同宿轨解。

Conclusion: 本文建立的拼接/匹配论证为处理L-S变分理论不适用的非奇非线性高阶方程提供了有效工具，证明了可数无穷多解的存在性，并揭示了解的复杂结构（任意复杂度的拼接模式）。

Abstract: Countable subsets of solutions of higher-order nonlinear ODEs and elliptic PDEs with indefinite non-coercive operators from the reaction-diffusion, thin film and dynamical system (DS) theories are obtained via a gluing/matching argument. In particular we study some classic and quasilinear degenerate ODEs in $\mathbb{R}$, with boundary conditions at infinity $F(\infty)=0$, with non-odd nonlinearities such as
  $$ \begin{matrix} F^{(4)} =-F+F^2,\, \,\, F^{(4)}=-F+F^2{\rm e}^{F-1}, \, (|F''|F'')''=-F + F^2, \\ F^{(4)} =-|F|F+F^2, \,\,\,F^{(4)} =-F^3+F^4, \,\,\, F^{(6)}=F-F^2, \end{matrix} $$ etc, as well as equations with odd and non-smooth nonlinearities like
  $$F^{(4)}=-F+F^3, \,F^{(4)}=-F-(|F|F-F)'', \,\, F^{(4)}=- \frac F{\sqrt{|F|}}-(F^3-F)'',\,\mbox{etc.}$$ Some of these ODEs are Hamiltonian and were studied in detail in the DS theory. On the basis of nonlinear operators, elliptic PDEs and variational theory, related polyharmonic elliptic equations in $\mathbb{R}^N$, $F(\infty)=0$, such as $$Δ^2 F=-F+F^2, \quad Δ^2 F=-F -Δ(F^2-F), \quad Δ^3 F=F-F^2, \quad \mbox{etc.};$$ are also shown to admit countable families of solutions. For such equations with non-odd functionals associated Lusternik--Schnirel'man (L--S) genus/category variational theory guaranteeing existence of a sequence of critical points does not apply. These ODEs and elliptic PDEs (e.g., in the radial setting) are shown to admit at least two basic countable families ${\mathcal F}_{1,2}$ of positively dominant solutions connected with two periodic orbits $Γ_{\rm max/min}$. Patterns obtained by gluing together via exponentially decaying tails of arbitrary finite samples from $Γ$'s form a countable subset of homoclinics in $\mathbb{R}^4$ of an arbitrary complexity.

</details>


### [113] [Failure of Fixed-Profile Modified Scattering at the Pure $L^2$ Endpoint for the 1D Defocusing Cubic NLS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02534)
*Xi Chen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了一维聚焦三次非线性薛定谔方程（NLS）中标准固定轮廓修正散射（modified-scattering）ansatz在无权重L²端点处失效。作者构造了一个实值初值q_* ∈ L¹ ∩ ∩H^k（但xq_* ∉ L²），使得校正后的傅里叶轮廓没有强L²极限，且初值的L²范数可任意指定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 修正散射理论在L²端点处的有效性是一个关键问题。此前的工作表明固定轮廓ansatz在加权空间或非端点情形下成立，但在无权重L²端点是否成立一直悬而未决。该问题的解答对整个修正散射理论的适用范围有根本性影响。

Method: 构造方法包括：(1) 归纳选取互不相交的光滑鼓包之和作为初值；(2) 利用Zakharov-Shabat传递矩阵的精确合成，在对数透射系数中引入高频振荡；(3) 借助Deift-Zhou相位中的单侧对数算子，将大小为ε_n的插入放大log X_n倍；(4) 选取ε_n log X_n = κ以保证连续光滑渐近轮廓之间的均匀分离，尽管部分数据在L¹∩L²中收敛。

Result: 存在实值初值q_* ∈ L¹ ∩ ∩_{k≥0} H^k，xq_* ∉ L²，使得校正傅里叶轮廓在L²中无强极限。该障碍是特定于单一时间无关轮廓的，但自适应或尺度依赖的重新正规化仍可能有效。

Conclusion: 标准固定轮廓修正散射ansatz在无权重L²端点失败，这揭示了该端点处的本质困难。然而，该反例并不排除自适应或尺度依赖的重正规化方案，为后续研究留下了开放的路径。

Abstract: We prove that the standard fixed-profile modified-scattering ansatz fails at the unweighted $L^2$ endpoint for the one-dimensional defocusing cubic nonlinear Schrödinger equation. More precisely, there exists a real-valued datum $$q_* \in L^1(\mathbb{R}) \cap \bigcap_{k \geq 0} H^k(\mathbb{R}), \quad x q_* \notin L^2(\mathbb{R})$$ for which the corrected Fourier profile has no strong $L^2$ limit. The $L^2$ norm of the datum may be prescribed arbitrarily. The construction is an inductively chosen sum of disjoint smooth bumps. Exact composition of the Zakharov--Shabat transfer matrices inserts a high-frequency oscillation into the logarithm of the transmission coefficient, while the one-sided logarithmic operator in the Deift--Zhou phase amplifies an insertion of size $ε_n$ by a factor of order $\log X_n$. Choosing $ε_n\log X_n=κ$ produces a uniform separation between consecutive smooth asymptotic profiles even though the partial data converge in $L^1\cap L^2$. The obstruction is specific to a single time-independent profile and leaves open adaptive or scale-dependent renormalizations.

</details>


### [114] [On the regularity theory for mixed local and nonlocal weighted quasilinear elliptic equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02557)
*Sanjit Biswas,Prashanta Garain*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了带一般右端项、由Muckenhoupt A_p权重控制退化的混合局部与非局部p-Laplace方程，建立了系统的局部正则性理论，包括弱子解的局部有界性与下半连续性、弱超解的弱Harnack不等式、Harnack不等式以及弱解的局部Hölder连续性。这是该框架下首个系统性正则性理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合局部与非局部p-Laplace方程在建模中具有广泛应用，但现有正则性理论不适用于由Muckenhoupt权重驱动的退化加权框架，当局部和非局部算子同时退化时缺乏统一的处理工具。

Method: 将加权分析技术与De Giorgi-Nash-Moser迭代方法相结合，并适配到混合局部-非局部设置中，用于处理Muckenhoupt A_p权重下的一般右端项。

Result: 建立了完整的局部正则性理论：弱子解的局部有界性与下半连续性、弱超解的弱Harnack不等式、Harnack不等式及弱解的局部Hölder连续性。即使对p=2的齐次线性方程在w∈A_2的自然假设下，结果也是新的，实质性扩展了现有混合局部-非局部方程的正则性理论。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统建立了带Muckenhoupt权重的混合局部与非局部退化p-Laplace方程的正则性理论，将现有结果实质性地推广到退化加权框架，并涵盖一般右端项。

Abstract: We investigate a broad class of mixed local and nonlocal degenerate $p$-Laplace equations with general right-hand sides. The degeneracy is governed by Muckenhoupt $A_p$-weights, yielding a highly nonuniform elliptic framework in which both the local and nonlocal operators may degenerate simultaneously. We establish a comprehensive local regularity theory, including local boundedness and lower semicontinuity of weak subsolutions, weak Harnack inequalities for weak supersolutions, Harnack inequalities, and local Hölder continuity of weak solutions. Our approach combines weighted analytic techniques with the De Giorgi--Nash--Moser iteration method, adapted to the mixed local--nonlocal setting. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic regularity theory for mixed local and nonlocal equations with Muckenhoupt weights. In particular, our results are new even for homogeneous linear equations ($p=2$) under the natural assumption $w\in A_2$, and therefore substantially extend the existing regularity theory for mixed local--nonlocal equations to a degenerate weighted framework with general right-hand sides.

</details>


### [115] [Talbot effect for the periodic Benjamin--Ono equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02567)
*Xi Chen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有粗糙初值的周期Benjamin–Ono方程的Talbot效应。利用Gérard–Kappeler–Topalov关于Tao规范变换的光滑化定理，将奇异性分析简化为一个显式的二次规范轮廓。对于一般有界变差数据，获得了有理时间下的规范-Hardy表示；对于一类Talbot可容许数据（其规范轮廓仅有有限个边缘奇点），该表示退化为有限个对数核与单跳核之和加上连续余项。有限跳变、分段光滑的有界变差数据被证明是Talbot可容许的（包括方波情形）。在无理时间下，证明了在有限Diophantine型条件下解的连续性，并构造了Liouville障碍表明相应的单侧规范轮廓并非在所有无理时间都连续。所得结果与Alama Bronsard–Laurens的数值轮廓定性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Talbot效应描述了色散偏微分方程解在粗糙初值下的奇异结构演化，是孤立波之外的经典非线性色散现象。对于Benjamin–Ono方程，由于其非局部非线性结构与规范变换的复杂性，Talbot效应的严格数学分析一直非常困难。本文旨在通过Gérard–Kappeler–Topalov的光滑化定理，为周期Benjamin–Ono方程在粗糙初值下建立严格的Talbot效应结构描述，填补理论空白。

Method: 首先利用Gérard–Kappeler–Topalov关于Tao规范变换的光滑化定理，将方程解映射到规范变量，从而将奇异性分析简化为一个显式的二次规范轮廓。随后对有界变差初值建立有理时间下的规范-Hardy表示；引入Talbot可容许初值的概念，对只有有限个边缘奇点的规范轮廓给出有限对数核与单跳核的分解。最后利用有限Diophantine型条件证明无理时间的连续性，并构造Liouville型数说明非连续性的存在。

Result: 证明了有限跳变、分段光滑的有界变差数据（包括方波）均为Talbot可容许的，其有理时间的解可由有限个对数核与单跳核精确刻画（模连续余项）。在无理时间下，有限Diophantine型条件下解连续；同时构造了Liouville数障碍，说明在一般无理时间下单侧规范轮廓可能不连续。结果与Alama Bronsard–Laurens的数值模拟定性吻合。

Conclusion: 本文为周期Benjamin–Ono方程的Talbot效应提供了严格的数学结构描述，揭示了与KdV等方程类似但具有独特规范变换特征的奇异性结构。结果不仅统一了数值观察，还指出了无理时间下连续性要求的Diophantine本质，为后续研究更一般的色散方程Talbot效应提供了方法框架。

Abstract: We study the Talbot effect for the periodic Benjamin--Ono equation with rough initial data. Using the smoothing theorem of Gérard--Kappeler--Topalov for Tao's gauge transform, we reduce the singularity analysis to an explicit quadratic gauge profile. For general bounded-variation data we obtain a rational-time gauge-Hardy representation. For a natural subclass of Talbot-admissible data, whose initial gauge profile has only finitely many edge singularities, this representation becomes a finite sum of logarithmic kernels and one-jump kernels, up to a continuous remainder. We show that finite-jump, piecewise smooth BV data are Talbot-admissible; in particular, this applies to the square wave. At irrational times, we prove continuity under a finite Diophantine type condition, and we also exhibit a Liouville obstruction showing that the corresponding one-sided gauge profile need not be continuous at all irrational times. The results give a rigorous structural result that is qualitatively consistent with the numerical profiles of Alama Bronsard--Laurens.

</details>

### [116] [Eigensystems of Dirac operators with singular potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00865)
*Boris Mityagin,Petr Siegl*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究有界区间上受Dirichlet边界条件约束的一维Dirac算子，其势函数具有幂次衰减的Fourier系数。利用Paley-Zygmund定理，这类广泛势函数族包含既非可积函数也非测度的分布。论文定位了扰动算子的谱，并作为主要结果证明了其特征系统生成Riesz基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究更广泛一类势函数（包括非可积函数也非测度的分布）对一维Dirac算子谱性质的影响，特别是特征系统的基底性质。这类奇异势函数此前未被系统研究。

Method: 利用Paley-Zygmund定理刻画具有幂次衰减Fourier系数的势函数族，对Dirichlet边界条件下的一维Dirac算子进行谱定位分析，并进一步证明其本征函数系统构成Riesz基。

Result: 成功定位了扰动后Dirac算子的谱，并证明了该算子的特征系统生成一个Riesz基（主要结果）。

Conclusion: 对于这类包含奇异分布的宽泛势函数族，扰动后的一维Dirac算子仍具有良好的谱结构，其特征系统构成Riesz基，这为更一般奇异势下的谱理论提供了基础。

Abstract: We perturb one-dimensional Dirac operators on a bounded interval subject to Dirichlet boundary conditions by potentials with Fourier coefficients exhibiting power-decay. As a consequence of Paley-Zygmund theorem, this broad family of potentials comprises distributions that are neither integrable functions nor measures. We localize the spectrum of the perturbed operator and, as the main result, show that its eigensystem generates a Riesz basis.

</details>


### [117] [Capacity estimates and improved lower bounds for the inner radius of nodal domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01345)
*Philippe Charron*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文证明了对任意闭光滑流形(M,g)上的拉普拉斯特征函数，其结点域必然包含一个半径至少为c(g)λ^{-1/2}(log log λ)^{-1/2}（d=3）或c(g)λ^{-1/2}(log λ)^{-(d-3)/2}（d>3）的测地球，球心位于结点域内特征函数绝对值最大点处；同时构造了环面T^d上内半径达到o(λ^{-1/2})阶的结点域序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拉普拉斯特征函数结点域的内半径下界，这是谱几何中的经典问题。此前对结点域内半径的估计尚不精确，作者希望给出更优的通用下界，并探讨在环面上该下界是否可达到最优。

Method: 利用特征函数在结点域内最大值点的局部性质，结合几何流形上的测地球半径估计与特征函数的振荡理论，通过对数因子的精细处理来获得下界；对于环面的构造，则显式构建特征函数序列来展示结点的退化行为。

Result: 在d=3时给出包含log log λ因子修正的下界，d>3时给出包含log λ^{-(d-3)/2}因子的下界；并且在任意d≥3的环面上构造出内半径仅为o(λ^{-1/2})的结点域序列，证明下界在某种意义下接近最优。

Conclusion: 结点域内半径在大维数条件下具有对数阶修正的下界，且该估计在环面情形下显示出与最优阶之间的差距，揭示了结点域几何结构的精细性质。

Abstract: We show that for every closed, smooth manifold $(M,g)$ of dimension $d$, there exists $c(g)$ such that any nodal domain $Ω_λ$ of a Laplace eigenfunction with eigenvalue $λ$ contains a geodesic ball of radius at least $c(g) λ^{-1/2} \log\log(λ)^{-1/2}$ if $d=3$ and $c(g) λ^{-1/2} \log(λ)^{-\frac{d-3}{2}}$ if $d >3$. This ball is centered at any point at which the eigenfunction attains its maximum in absolute value within the nodal domain. Furthermore, we show that for any $d \geq 3$, there exist sequences of $λ$-nodal domains on $\mathbb{T}^d$ whose inner radius is of order $o(λ^{-1/2})$.

</details>


### [118] [Coarse nodal counts on sub-Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01446)
*Irene Silvestre-Roselló,Vukašin Stojisavljević*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究子拉普拉斯算子本征函数线性组合的节点集的粗拓扑性质，在紧致幂零流形（分层群的商空间）上证明了Courant定理和Bézout定理的粗版本，并推测这些结果可推广到一般闭等正则子黎曼流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将黎曼流形上关于本征函数节点集拓扑性质（如Courant定理、Bézout定理）的经典结果推广到子黎曼几何框架下，特别关注子拉普拉斯算子的本征函数线性组合。由于子黎曼几何中缺乏黎曼情形的常规工具，需要发展新的方法。

Method: 结合拓扑持久性理论与Hörmander向量场的各向异性Sobolev理论，推广了近期在黎曼情形下实施的思路，对幂零流形上的子拉普拉斯本征函数线性组合的节点集进行粗拓扑分析。

Result: 在紧致幂零流形（分层群的商空间）上证明了子拉普拉斯本征函数线性组合节点集的Courant定理与Bézout定理的粗版本。

Conclusion: 提出了将这些结果推广到一般闭等正则子黎曼流形的猜想，并概述了证明该猜想的纲领。

Abstract: We study coarse topology of nodal sets of linear combinations of eigenfunctions of sub-Laplacians. More precisely, we prove coarse versions of Courant's and Bézout's theorems for linear combinations of eigenfunctions of sub-Laplacians on compact nilmanifolds obtained as quotients of stratified groups. We conjecture the extensions of these results to general closed equiregular sub-Riemannian manifolds and outline a programme for proving them. The method we use combines topological persistence and anisotropic Sobolev theory of Hörmander vector fields, generalizing the ideas which have recently been implemented in the Riemannian case.

</details>


### [119] [A Weighted Discretization of Riemannian Manifolds with Lower Ricci Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02405)
*Aditya Tiwari*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文在具有Ricci曲率下界约束的紧致黎曼流形上，通过ε-离散化构造加权组合拉普拉斯算子，证明了加权图拉普拉斯与Laplace-Beltrami算子之间的谱比较定理，并将此结果应用于度量Gromov-Hausdorff收敛下的谱稳定性证明及Schoen-Wolpert-Yau不等式的恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在黎曼流形的离散化研究中，传统的谱比较通常依赖于注入半径（injectivity radius），限制了其应用范围。本文旨在建立只依赖于维数、曲率下界和离散化尺度ε的均匀谱比较结果，从而摆脱对注入半径的依赖，为度量Gromov-Hausdorff收敛下的谱分析提供更普适的工具。

Method: 在满足Ric≥-(n-1)κg的紧致连通n维黎曼流形M的ε-离散化上引入加权组合拉普拉斯算子，通过精心设计权重使得离散算子与连续Laplace-Beltrami算子的谱特征值可均匀比较，比较常数仅依赖于n、κ和ε。随后利用该谱比较定理分析度量Gromov-Hausdorff收敛下的谱稳定性，并应用于双曲曲面族的加权离散化。

Result: 证明了加权图拉普拉斯算子与Laplace-Beltrami算子的特征值是均匀可比较的，且比较常数仅依赖于维数n、曲率下界κ和离散化尺度ε，与注入半径无关。作为应用，获得了度量Gromov-Hausdorff收敛下的谱稳定性，并利用退化（pinching）亏格-2双曲曲面族上的加权离散化恢复了Schoen-Wolpert-Yau不等式。

Conclusion: 本文建立的与注入半径无关的谱比较定理为离散与连续谱理论之间的桥梁提供了更稳健的工具，在度量收敛理论和双曲曲面谱几何中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Let $(M,g)$ be a connected, compact, $n$-dimensional Riemannian manifold with $\operatorname{Ric}(M,g)\geq-(n-1)κg$. We introduce a weighted combinatorial Laplacian on $\varepsilon$-discretizations of $M$ and prove a spectral comparison theorem between the weighted graph Laplacian and the Laplace-Beltrami operator. More precisely, the eigenvalues of the two operators are uniformly comparable with constants depending only on $n,κ,\varepsilon$, independently of the injectivity radius. As an application, we prove spectral stability under measured Gromov-Hausdorff convergence. We also recover the Schoen-Wolpert-Yau inequality using the weighted discretization on families of pinching genus-$2$ hyperbolic surfaces.

</details>

### [120] [Value distributions for read-once polynomials on finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00081)
*Alexey Yashunsky,Dmitrii Tabalin*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域上只读一次（read-once）多项式函数输出值分布的几何性质，证明了所有这样的分布都落在由特定不等式定义的凸体B_k中，且该凸体对域加法与乘法的卷积运算是稳定的。文中给出了该凸体的精确体积公式及其指数增长速率，并在Lean 4中完成了全部定理的形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 只读一次多项式函数是一类自然的代数对象，其输出分布刻画了这类函数在随机输入下的行为。理解这些分布所构成的几何区域（凸体）有助于揭示有限域上代数表达式的基本统计性质，并可能为随机算法、密码学等相关领域提供理论支撑。

Method: 作者将所有只读一次函数输出分布构成的集合刻画为凸体B_k（由排序后的原子概率满足的不等式定义），并证明该凸体对两类场运算诱导的卷积运算闭合：加法结论推广到任意拟群（quasigroup）运算，乘法结论仅需吸收零元加上非零元素上的拟群结构。凸体的体积通过对一维积分精确计算，并分析其k次根的极限行为。

Result: 对所有k≥4，只读一次多项式函数的输出分布均包含于凸体B_k；该凸体在加法和乘法对应的卷积下保持稳定，且更一般地在拟群卷积下保持稳定。B_k是全维的，包含均匀分布和所有点质量；其归一化体积由一维积分精确给出，k次根收敛到0.2183305369...。上述全部结果（包括两个稳定性定理、精确体积公式及其指数收敛速率）已在Lean 4中形式化证明。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅给出了有限域上只读一次多项式输出分布的精确几何刻画，还通过形式化验证确保了数学结论的可靠性，为随机代数结构的研究提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Consider read-once polynomial functions over a finite field of order $k$, i.e., functions defined by expressions built from field addition, multiplication, and constants, in which every variable occurs at most once. Let $p=(p_1,\dots,p_k)$ be the distribution of the values of a read-once function on independent uniform inputs. We prove that for every $k\ge4$ all such distributions belong to a body $\mathcal{B}_k$, defined by the following relation on the sorted atoms $p_1^\downarrow\geq\cdots\geq p_k^\downarrow$ of the distribution: \[
  \mathcal{B}_k=\left\{p:p_k^\downarrow\ge
  \frac{1-p_2^\downarrow-(1-p_2^\downarrow)^k}{k-1}\right\}, \] or equivalently, $\mathcal{B}_k = \{ p \colon 1-p_2^\downarrow-(k-1)p_k^\downarrow\le(1-p_2^\downarrow)^k\}$.
  Our main theorem is that this body is stable under the convolutions corresponding to both field operations. More generally, convolution for any quasigroup operation on $k$ points preserves $\mathcal{B}_k$; the multiplicative conclusion needs only an absorbing zero and a quasigroup operation on the nonzero elements.
  The body is full-dimensional and contains the uniform law and every point mass; its normalized volume is given by an exact one-dimensional integral, and the $k$th root of that volume tends to $0.2183305369\ldots$. The complete development, including the two stability theorems, the exact volume formula, and its sharp exponential rate, has been formalized in Lean 4.

</details>


### [121] [On the Integer Domination Root Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00109)
*Saeid Alikhani,Max Griswold*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个33阶图G_33，其支配多项式D(G_33, x)在x=-4处有整数根，从而否证了"支配整数根猜想"（该猜想认为0和-2是任意图支配多项式的仅有的整数根）。文中给出了G_33的完整结构描述、精确的支配多项式及其有理因式分解，并阐述了通过转移矩阵和S-单位分支消消机制产生x=-4处非平凡零值的结构元件机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支配整数根猜想长期悬而未决，此前0和-2被认为是任意图支配多项式仅有的两个整数根。验证或推翻该猜想对图论中多项式根分布的研究具有重要意义，此工作旨在寻找并构造一个反例。

Method: 构造一个n=33的特定图G_33，利用转移矩阵（transfer matrix）和S-单位分支消消（S-unit branch cancellations）的结构元件机制，设计出在x=-4处产生零评估的图结构，并计算其精确的支配多项式及其有理因式分解。

Result: 成功构造出33阶反例图G_33，其支配多项式在x=-4处有整数根，即D(G_33, -4)=0，并给出了该图的完整结构描述、精确支配多项式表达式及精确的有理因式分解。

Conclusion: 该结果证伪了支配整数根猜想，表明除0和-2之外，-4也可以作为图的支配多项式的整数根，扩展了对支配多项式整数根分布的认识。

Abstract: The domination integer root conjecture asserted that $0$ and $-2$ are the only integer roots of the domination polynomial $D(G, x)$ for any graph $G$. In this paper, we document a counterexample of order $n = 33$ possessing an integer domination root at $x = -4$. We provide the complete structural description of the graph $G_{33}$, present its exact domination polynomial $D(G_{33}, x)$, and demonstrate its exact rational factorization. Furthermore, we outline the structural gadget mechanism involving transfer matrices and $S$-unit branch cancellations that gives rise to non-trivial zero evaluation at $x = -4$.

</details>


### [122] [Antimagic orientations of graphs with a dominating clique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00209)
*Abdulrahman Al-Taweel*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了每个含有支配团（dominating clique）的连通图都具有反魔定向（antimagic orientation），从而验证了Hefetz、Mütze和Schwartz提出的猜想在该图类上成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hefetz、Mütze和Schwartz提出了关于任意连通图都存在反魔定向的猜想，该猜想是一个重要的图论组合问题，本文旨在在具有支配团的图类上验证该猜想。

Method: 本文通过构造性的方法证明：对任意含有支配团的连通图，存在一种边的定向方式和双射标号方案（标签为1到m），使得每个顶点的定向顶点和（入弧标签减出弧标签）两两互不相同。

Result: 证明了每个具有支配团的连通图都存在反魔定向，即Hefetz-Mütze-Schwartz猜想在该图类上成立。

Conclusion: 该结果为反魔定向猜想提供了新的支持性证据，将猜想成立的范围扩展到所有包含支配团的连通图。

Abstract: A graph G with m edges has an antimagic orientation if its edges can be oriented and bijectively labelled 1, ..., m so that the oriented vertex sums, the labels on in-arcs minus the labels on out-arcs, are pairwise distinct. Hefetz, Mütze and Schwartz conjectured that every connected graph admits an antimagic orientation. We prove that this conjecture holds for every graph with a dominating clique.

</details>


### [123] [Identities involving the number of missing integers in partitions - combinatorial proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00278)
*Joselyne Aniceto,Cristina Ballantine*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文为Bhoria、Eyyunni和Santra关于分拆中缺失整数数量的研究结果提供了组合证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的研究建立了关于具有固定缺失整数数量的分拆（过分拆）的生成函数、恒等式和同余式，但其中许多结果是通过解析方法获得的，缺乏直接的组合解释。本文旨在为这些结果提供组合证明。

Method: 采用组合学的双射（bijection）和组合构造方法，为已有文献中关于分拆缺失整数数量的多个结果提供纯组合证明，而非解析证明。

Result: 成功为Bhoria等人论文中的若干结果（包括生成函数、恒等式等）提供了组合证明。

Conclusion: 该文通过提供组合证明，深化了对分拆中缺失整数问题的理解，展示了这些结果的组合本质。

Abstract: A missing integer in a partition $λ$ is a positive integer less than the largest part of $λ$ that does not appear as a part in $λ$. In the recent paper On the number of missing integers in partitions, Bhoria, Eyyunni, and Santra examined the number of partitions (overpartitions) of $n$ with a fixed number of missing integers and established generating functions, identities, and congruences for them. In this article, we provide combinatorial proofs for several of their results.

</details>


### [124] [On a conjecture of Han and Xiong for fractional Gaussian binomial coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00323)
*Ken Ono*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一个关于高斯二项式系数推广至正有理数情况下的支撑支配定理，验证了Han和Xiong关于整数迹在r=1/2处取最大值的猜想，并通过计算机计算验证了所有正有理数r且k≤200的情形。理论结果由AxiomProver在Lean中自主生成并验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Han和Xiong将高斯二项式系数从整数扩展到正有理数r，并猜想其整数迹（结果幂级数的整数指数部分）在r=1/2时系数wise最大。该猜想需要严格证明。

Method: 提出了一个支撑支配定理，通过显式的可整除性条件比较有理参数；将猜想归约到单位分数r=1/(2m)的情形（对每个固定k仅有限多个非平凡情形）；结合计算机计算验证k≤200的情况；理论结果由AxiomProver在Lean证明助手中自主产生并验证。

Result: 定理解决了所有r≥1/2时的猜想，并将完整猜想归约到单位分数情形。计算机计算验证了所有正有理数r和所有k≤200时的猜想成立。

Conclusion: 通过支撑支配定理和计算验证，完整解决了高斯二项式系数正整数迹最大化的猜想，并展示了自动定理证明在组合学猜想验证中的应用。

Abstract: Han and Xiong recently extended the Gaussian binomial coefficient $\genfrac{[}{]}{0pt}{}{r+k}{k}_{q}$ to positive rational $r$ and conjectured that its integer trace, the integer-exponent part of the resulting power series, is coefficientwise largest at $r=1/2$. We prove a support-dominance theorem comparing rational parameters under an explicit divisibility condition. It settles the conjecture for every $r\geq 1/2$ and reduces the full conjecture to the unit fractions $r=\frac{1}{2m}$, only finitely many of which are nontrivial for each fixed $k$. A computer computation then verifies the conjecture for every positive rational $r$ and every $k\leq 200$. The theoretical results were autonomously produced and verified in Lean by AxiomProver.

</details>


### [125] [Flexible Latin directed triple systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00329)
*Aleš Drápal,Andrew R. Kozlik,Terry S. Griggs*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究柔性Latin有向三元系（flexible Latin directed triple systems），这是拉丁有向三元系中满足柔性律的子类，与Steiner和Mendelsohn三元系形成的拟群不同，此类系统在柔性律下表现出独特性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Steiner三元系和Mendelsohn三元系通过定义运算可以形成满足柔性律的拟群，而一般有向三元系不总是形成拟群。作者在之前的工作中研究了非柔性的Latin有向三元系，本文旨在补充研究柔性的Latin有向三元系这一缺失情形。

Method: 通过将Latin有向三元系与拟群建立关联，利用柔性律x·(y·x)=(x·y)·x进行分类讨论，系统研究柔性Latin有向三元系的结构性质。

Result: 给出了柔性Latin有向三元系的结构刻画和分类结果，补充了之前关于非柔性Latin有向三元系的研究。

Conclusion: 柔性Latin有向三元系构成一个有别于Steiner和Mendelsohn三元系的独特有向三元系族，其拟群满足柔性律，本文完成了对Latin有向三元系在柔性律下的系统研究。

Abstract: It is well known that, given a Steiner triple system, a quasigroup can be formed by defining an operation $\cdot$ by the identities $x \cdot x = x$ and $x \cdot y = z$ where $z$ is the third point in the block containing the pair $\{x,y\}$. The same is true for a Mendelsohn triple system where the pair $(x,y)$ is considered to be ordered. But it is not true in general for directed triple systems. However directed triple systems which form quasigroups under this operation do exist and we call these Latin directed triple systems. The quasigroups associated with Steiner and Mendelsohn triple systems satisfy the flexible law $x \cdot (y \cdot x) = (x \cdot y) \cdot x$ but those associated with Latin directed triple systems need not. In a previous paper, [Discrete Mathematics 312 (2012), 597-607], we studied non-flexible Latin directed triple systems. In this paper we turn our attention to flexible Latin directed triple systems.

</details>


### [126] [Schubitopes are not Ehrhart positive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00377)
*Yupeng Li,Avery St. Dizier*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出了一个Schubitope的Ehrhart正性猜想的反例，即构造了一个Ehrhart多项式在单项式展开中出现负系数的Schubitope，该反例由GPT-5.6 Sol Pro在ChatGPT中发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Monical、Tokcan和Yong猜想Schubitope（一类由Schubert多项式和Demazure特征导出的广义排列多胞体）具有Ehrhart正性。验证或反驳该猜想的动机在于理解这类组合多胞体的计数性质。

Method: 作者通过具体构造一个Schubitope实例，计算其Ehrhart多项式并在单项式展开中检查系数符号，从而寻找并证明一个包含负系数的反例。该反例借助GPT-5.6 Sol Pro在ChatGPT中发现。

Result: 成功找到了一个Schubitope，其Ehrhart多项式的单项式展开中包含负系数，从而否定了Monical-Tokcan-Yong关于Schubitope Ehrhart正性的猜想。

Conclusion: Schubitope类并非整体具有Ehrhart正性，原猜想不成立。

Abstract: Schubitopes are a family of generalized permutahedra arising as the Newton polytopes of both Schubert polynomials and Demazure characters of $\mathrm{GL}_n$. Monical, Tokcan, and Yong conjectured that Schubitopes are Ehrhart positive. We exhibit a counterexample to this conjecture, a Schubitope whose Ehrhart polynomial contains a negative coefficient in its monomial expansion. This example was found using GPT-5.6 Sol Pro in ChatGPT.

</details>


### [127] [Fixed Perimeter Analogues of Several Partition Results Related to Parity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00421)
*Gabriel Gray,Emily Payne,Holly Swisher,Ren Watson*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将奇偶偏差不等式的研究从经典整数分拆推广到固定周长（perimeter）分拆的设定中，通过组合方法证明了固定周长版本的经典奇偶偏差结果及其推广，并扩展这些方法到PED和POD分拆的固定周长类比中，同时发展了递归公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2016年Straub证明Euler经典分拆恒等式对最大钩长（周长）为n的分拆仍然成立，激发了经典分拆与固定周长分拆之间关系的研究。作者希望将2020年Kim、Kim和Lovejoy提出的奇偶偏差不等式研究拓展到固定周长设定中，探索经典奇偶偏差结果的固定周长类比是否成立并可被推广。

Method: 采用组合方法（combinatorial methods）证明固定周长分拆的奇偶偏差结果，将经典奇偶偏差不等式推广到固定周长设定；同时将这些方法扩展到固定周长版本的PED（奇部不同且偶部无限制）和POD（偶部不同且奇部无限制）分拆中，并开发针对这些分拆的递归计数公式。

Result: 证明了固定周长设定下许多经典奇偶偏差结果的类比可以被证明并进一步推广；将方法成功扩展到PED和POD分拆的固定周长类比；为周长n且奇部不同/偶部无限制，以及偶部不同/奇部无限制的分拆计数建立了递归公式。

Conclusion: 固定周长分拆不仅是经典分拆的有趣变体，还能承载并推广奇偶偏差不等式等经典结果，且通过组合方法可系统性地建立此类结果，同时递归公式为后续计算和研究提供了基础工具。

Abstract: In 2016, Straub proved that Euler's classic partition identity holds true for partitions with largest hook (perimeter) $n$. This inspired further study of the relationship between classical partitions and fixed perimeter partitions. We extend the study of parity bias inequalities, first introduced by Kim, Kim, and Lovejoy in 2020, to the fixed perimeter setting and show using combinatorial methods that fixed perimeter analogues of many classical parity bias results can be proven and generalized. We also extend these methods to prove similar inequalities for the fixed perimeter analogues of PED and POD partitions. We additionally develop recursive formulas for the number of perimeter $n$ partitions with odd parts distinct and even parts unrestricted and with even parts distinct and odd parts unrestricted.

</details>


### [128] [Mirror Variations on Catalan Multijections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00431)
*Muhammad Adam Dombrowski*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文针对Catalan多重jection（多射）问题，提出了若干新的多重jection，这些新的多重jection产生的划分与先前发现的多重jection产生的划分不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Isaak和Langley在未发表的稿件中研究了两个多重jection何时产生相同的划分，本文旨在发现更多不同的多重jection，以丰富Catalan对象划分的研究。

Method: 提出若干新的Catalan多重jection构造方法，并证明这些新的多重jection产生的等价类划分与之前已知的多重jection产生的划分不同。

Result: 构造出了多个全新的Catalan多重jection，且所产生的划分与先前发现的所有多重jection的划分均不相同。

Conclusion: 本文提供了新的Catalan多重jection构造，扩展了Catalan多重jection的已知族，为后续研究多重jection的等价分类提供了更多素材。

Abstract: A Catalan multijection is a way to partition a set of $\binom{2n}{n}$ objects into equivalence classes of size $n+1$, where each equivalence class has exactly one distinct Catalan object. Isaak and Langley investigated when two multijections produced the same partitions in an unpublished manuscript. We propose some new multijections for which we get different partitions from previously discovered multijections.

</details>


### [129] [A note on the saturation number for unions of three cliques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00459)
*Hanlai Lin,Zhen He,Yiduo Xu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文确定了三个团的不交并 K_p ∪ K_q ∪ K_r 的饱和数及唯一极值图，适用于 2≤p≤q<r<p+q 且 n 足够大的情况，从而与已知结果一起完整解决了所有三个团不交并的饱和数问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究受 Faudree、Ferrara、Gould 和 Jacobson 提出的关于 K_p ∪ K_q ∪ K_{q+1} 的问题启发，旨在确定三个团不交并的饱和数，填补此前 r≥p+q 和 r=q 情况已知但 p<q<r<p+q 中间情况未解决的空白。

Method: 本文通过构造下界和上界来证明最优饱和图的结构。对于 K_p ∪ K_q ∪ K_r 的饱和图，在条件 2≤p≤q<r<p+q 下，构造特定的图并证明其是唯一的极值饱和图，从而确定饱和数。

Result: 确定了在 2≤p≤q<r<p+q 条件下 K_p ∪ K_q ∪ K_r 的饱和数及其唯一极值图（当 n 足够大时）。结合已有的 r≥p+q 和 r=q 的结果，完整解决了所有三个团不交并的饱和数与极值图问题。

Conclusion: 对于足够大的 n，任意三个团的不交并的饱和数和极值图已被完全确定，完满回答了 Faudree 等人提出的相关问题，并彻底解决了该领域的一个长期开放问题。

Abstract: A graph $G$ is $F$-saturated if $G$ contains no copy of $F$ but $G+e$ contains a copy of $F$ for every missing edge $e$ of $G$. The saturation number $\sat(n,F)$ is the minimum number of edges in an $n$-vertex $F$-saturated graph. Motivated by a problem posed by Faudree, Ferrara, Gould, and Jacobson concerning $K_p\cup K_q\cup K_{q+1}$, we determine the saturation number and the unique extremal graph for $K_p\cup K_q\cup K_r$ whenever $2\le p\le q<r<p+q$ and $n$ is sufficiently large.
  Together with the previously known results for $r\ge p+q$ and for $r=q$, this completes the determination of the saturation number and the extremal graphs for unions of three cliques, for all sufficiently large $n$.

</details>


### [130] [An optimal refinement-compatible bijection between singleton-free partitions and partitions without cyclic adjacencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00479)
*Vuong Bui*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出了无单点块的分划与无循环相邻元素同块的分划之间的一种直接"一轮"双射，解决了Bernhart关于不存在简单双射的猜想性评论。该双射与精化偏序高度兼容（正方向只分解块，逆方向只合并块），仅当n>2为偶数时存在一个不可避免的例外对，且这一例外被证明是最优的。该构造还能直接限制到非交叉分划上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bernhart指出：无单点的[n]分划数与无循环相邻元素(i,i+1 mod n)同块的分划数相等，但可能不存在简单双射。虽然Callan构造了算法双射证明更强的等分布性质，但其构造需经过多轮交换，因此Bernhart的评论仍可能成立（Chen和Wang也持此观点）。本文旨在构造一种直接的一轮双射来回应这一评论。

Method: 构造一个"一轮"直接双射。该映射与分划的精化偏序紧密兼容：正方向仅通过分解块将无单点分划变为无循环相邻分划（仅拆出单点块），逆方向仅通过合并块返回（仅将单点与其循环相邻元素所在的块合并）。当n>2为偶数时存在一个例外对，作者证明此例外是不可避免的，从而确立了该双射在精化序意义上的最优性。由于局部操作形式特殊（只拆出单点块、只将单点与含循环邻居的块合并），构造无需修改即可限制到非交叉分划类的对应双射。

Result: 成功构造了无单点分划与无循环相邻分划之间的直接一轮双射，回答了Bernhart的评论。该双射在精化序上具有最优性：除n>2为偶数时一对不可避免的例外外，正方向只分解块、逆方向只合并块；并证明了这一例外无法消除。同时证明双射可以自然地限制到非交叉分划上。

Conclusion: 本文首次给出了一种真正直接的"一轮"双射，证明了对Bernhart评论的正面回应：尽管Callan已构造了算法双射，但确实存在更简单、更结构化的双射。该双射的精化序兼容性及其非交叉限制性进一步体现了其内在的组合优美性。

Abstract: It is well known that the number of partitions of $[n]$ without singletons equals the number of partitions of $[n]$ in which no block contains two cyclically adjacent elements $i,i+1\pmod{n}$. Bernhart remarked that there might be no simple bijection between these two classes. Although Callan later constructed an algorithmic bijection proving the stronger equidistribution of singletons and adjacencies, his construction proceeds through multiple rounds of exchanges. Therefore, Bernhart's remark may still retain some validity, as suggested by Chen and Wang. In this article, we address this remark by giving a direct ``one-round'' bijection between the two classes. Unlike Callan's bijection, our map is closely compatible with the refinement order on partitions: in one direction it only decomposes blocks, while its inverse only merges blocks, with a single exceptional pair when $n>2$ is even. We further observe that this exception is unavoidable, establishing the optimality of the bijection with respect to the refinement order. The specific local form of these operations --- splitting off only singleton blocks and merging a singleton only with the block containing its cyclic neighbor --- also ensures that the construction restricts, without modification, to a bijection between the corresponding classes of noncrossing partitions.

</details>


### [131] [Extremal cross $t$-intersecting families under $t$-covering number constraints for vector spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00505)
*Yu Zhu,Benjian Lv,Kaishun Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域上向量空间中交叉t-相交族对的极值结构问题，在维数条件约束下确定了乘积最大时的极值结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉t-相交族问题是有限几何与极值组合中的经典问题，此前已有关于乘积最大化的部分结果，但涉及交集维数小于t的约束条件时，其极值结构尚未被完全刻画，本文旨在补全这一空白。

Method: 通过分析两个子空间族F和G在交叉t-相交条件下的乘积大小，结合条件dim(∩F)<t和dim(∩G)<t，运用极值组合与有限几何的技巧确定极值结构。

Result: 在给定约束条件下，确定了使得|F||G|取最大值时的极值结构。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了交叉t-相交族对在交集维数限制下的极值结构，推广了相关已有结果。

Abstract: Let $V$ be an $n$-dimensional vector space over the finite field $\mathbb{F}_q$, and ${V\brack k}$ denote the family of all $k$-dimensional subspaces of $V$. The families $\mathcal{F}\subseteq {V\brack k}$ and $\mathcal{G}\subseteq {V\brack \ell}$ are said to be cross $t$-intersecting if $\dim(F\cap G)\geq t$ for all $F\in\mathcal{F}$ and $G\in \mathcal{G}$. In this paper, we determine the extremal structures when $|\mathcal{F}||\mathcal{G}|$ attains the maximum value under the conditions $\dim\left(\cap_{F\in \mathcal{F}}F\right)<t$ and $\dim\left(\cap_{G\in \mathcal{G}}G\right)<t$.

</details>


### [132] [Two $q$-Supercongruences Related to Double Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00509)
*Wei-Wei Qi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用q恒等式和微分算子技术，建立了与截断双重基本超几何q级数相关的两个q-超同余式，模为某个分圆多项式的平方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究q-超同余式是超几何级数与数论交叉领域的重要课题，特别是与截断双重基本超几何q级数相关的同余式此前研究较少，本文旨在补充该方向的研究。

Method: 采用q-恒等式与微分算子技术，建立模分圆多项式平方的两个q-超同余式，研究对象为截断的双重基本超几何q级数。

Result: 成功建立两个新的q-超同余式，其模为分圆多项式的平方，涉及截断双重基本超几何q级数。

Conclusion: 本文通过组合q-恒等式和微分算子方法，获得了与截断双重基本超几何q级数相关的q-超同余式，拓展了该领域已知结果。

Abstract: In this paper, by using $q$-identities and differential operator techniques, we establish two $q$-supercongruences modulo the square of a cyclotomic polynomial, which are associated with truncated double basic hypergeometric $q$-series.

</details>


### [133] [Basics of DTS quasigroups: algebra, geometry and enumeration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00532)
*Aleš Drápal,Terry S. Griggs,Andrew R. Kozlik*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文从代数角度刻画了由有向三元系分解完全有向图所得到的拟群，将其与三角化伪曲面相关联，并报告了枚举结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有向三元系统（directed triple system）通过特定运算规则可以诱导出拟群结构，但此前缺乏对这类拟群的代数描述，也尚未将其与三角化伪曲面建立联系并系统枚举。

Method: 定义有向三元系为完全有向图的分解为有向三元组⟨x,y,z⟩；通过设定xy=z, yz=x, xz=y且uu=u构造二元运算，进而产生拟群；给出该类拟群的代数刻画，建立其与三角化伪曲面的关联，并进行枚举计算。

Result: 给出了诱导拟群的代数描述，说明了其与三角化伪曲面的对应关系，并报告了相关的枚举结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为有向三元系诱导的拟群提供了系统的代数理论框架，揭示了它们与三角化伪曲面的深层联系，并提供了新的枚举数据。

Abstract: A directed triple system can be defined as a decomposition of a complete digraph to directed triples $\langle x,y,z\rangle$. By setting $xy =z$, $yz =x$, $xz =y$ and $uu =u$ we get a binary operation that can be a quasigroup. We give an algebraic description of such quasigroups, explain how they can be associated with triangulated pseudosurfaces and report enumeration results.

</details>


### [134] [Proper conflict-free 7-coloring of planar graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00555)
*A. Jiménez,C. N. Lintzmayer,M. Sambinelli*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了适当的冲突自由染色问题（proper conflict-free coloring），证明了不含K_5-minor和Q_6-minor的图可以用至多7种颜色进行染色，其中Q_6=K_3∨K̄_3。该结果特别改进了平面图此前8种颜色的上界。证明结合了此前开发的迭代距离三选择器构造和anchor-contraction提升原理。论文还建立了第一机制的奇偶性类比，证明了当Hadwiger猜想对K_{k+1}成立时，不含K_{k+1}-minor的图可以用2k-1种颜色染色，使得每个非孤立顶点的开邻域中某种颜色出现奇数次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 适当的冲突自由染色是图论中一个重要的染色问题，要求每个非孤立顶点的开邻域中存在唯一出现的颜色。此前对于平面图的通用上界为8种颜色，本文希望改进这个上界，并将其推广到更一般的minor-free图类。

Method: 证明结合了两种核心技术：(1) 此前开发的迭代距离三选择器构造（iterated distance-three selector），用于在闭邻域中提供独立染色的见证顶点（witnesses）；(2) 新开发的anchor-contraction提升原理，将这些见证与合适minor的适当染色相结合。此外，论文还开发了第一种机制的奇偶性类比（parity analogue）。

Result: 证明了不含K_5-minor和Q_6-minor的图可被适当的冲突自由染色至多用7种颜色完成，改进了平面图此前的8色上界。同时证明了在Hadwiger猜想对K_{k+1}情形成立的假设下，不含K_{k+1}-minor的图可以用2k-1种颜色染色，使得每个非孤立顶点开邻域中某种颜色出现奇数次。

Conclusion: 本文通过创新性地结合迭代距离三选择器构造与anchor-contraction提升原理，成功将冲突自由染色的上界从8色改进到7色，并建立了奇偶性版本的对应结果，为后续研究minor-free图的冲突自由染色问题提供了新的方法论框架。

Abstract: A proper conflict-free coloring is a proper vertex coloring in which every nonisolated vertex has a color occurring uniquely in its open neighborhood. We prove that every graph with neither a $K_5$-minor nor a $Q_6$-minor admits such a coloring with at most seven colors, where $Q_6=K_3\vee\overline{K_3}$. In particular, this improves the previous general upper bound of eight for planar graphs. The proof combines a previously developed iterated distance-three selector construction with a general anchor-contraction lifting principle. The first supplies independently colored witnesses in closed neighborhoods, while the second combines those witnesses with a proper coloring of a suitable minor. We also develop the parity analogue of the first mechanism and show that, whenever the $K_{k+1}$ case of Hadwiger's conjecture holds, every $K_{k+1}$-minor-free graph can be proper vertex colored with $2k-1$ colors such that every nonisolated vertex has a color occurring an odd number of times in its open neighborhood.

</details>


### [135] [The finitude of the fibers of the complementary Bell numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00575)
*John M. Campbell*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文解决了Subbarao和Verma于1999年提出的关于互补贝尔数序列(f(n))_{n≥0}的三个开放问题中的第三个。该问题的第一部分询问f(n)是否对每个给定值仅取有限次，作者通过结合有限差分方法、部分Motzkin路径、Tate代数关于Gauss范数的完备性以及Strassmann定理，给出了肯定性解答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Subbarao和Verma在1999年提出了关于互补贝尔数序列的若干开放问题，Yang在2001年解决了前两个问题，但第三个问题至今悬而未决。填补这一长期空白是本文的核心动机。

Method: 采用多种数学工具的综合应用：有限差分方法、部分Motzkin路径、Tate代数在Gauss范数下的完备性，以及Strassmann定理，共同构建对第三个问题第一部分的证明。

Result: 成功证明互补贝尔数序列f(n)对任意给定值仅取有限次，即第三个问题的第一部分得到肯定解答。

Conclusion: 通过结合组合学、p-adic分析和代数工具，彻底解决了Subbarao-Verma第三个开放问题的第一部分，推进了对互补贝尔数性质的理解。

Abstract: Subbarao and Verma introduced, in 1999, a number of open problems concerning the sequence $(f(n))_{n \geq 0}$ of complementary Bell numbers, which may be defined via Bell polynomials $B_{n}(x) = \sum_{k=0}^{n} \left\{ \begin{smallmatrix} n \\ k \end{smallmatrix} \right\} x^k$ so that $f(n) = B_{n}(-1)$. Yang [Electron. J. Combin., 2001] subsequently solved the first two problems from Subbarao and Verma, but the third such problem has remained open, to the best of our knowledge. The first part of this third problem asks whether or not $f(n)$ takes any given value only a finite number of times. We solve this problem in the affirmative, through a combined application of finite difference-based methods, partial Motzkin paths, the completeness of the Tate algebra with respect to the Gauss norm, and Strassmann's theorem.

</details>


### [136] [Odd-Cycle Span Defect: A Polynomial Lower Bound and a Square-Root Upper Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00691)
*Shuyan Chen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究图G的色数χ(G)与奇圈诱导子图最大色数ψ(G)之间的加法间隙，定义函数F(N)度量这一间隙在有限阶数下的最大值，证明N^{1/6-o(1)} ≤ F(N) < √(6N)，将Erdős-Hajnal开放问题相关的有限阶尺度从log N/log log N提升到N的固定幂次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这一研究源于Erdős和Hajnal提出的开放问题，关注色数与奇圈结构色数之间的加法间隙如何在有限图上增长。此前Cameron-Clow路径染色构造仅提供log N/log log N量级的界，本文旨在突破这一对数尺度，建立更强大的多项式下界。

Method: 下界通过构造"palette-code graph"实现：从二元覆盖码C⊆{0,1}^p出发构造图，并精确证明χ(G)=2p+ℓ-ρ(C)（ρ为覆盖半径）和ψ(G)=2p；利用近中间Hamming覆盖码取得1/6指数。上界综合运用Polavarapu连通性定理、Chvátal-Erdős Hamiltonicity定理以及最大独立集剥离技术。

Result: 证明了N^{1/6-o(1)} ≤ F(N) < √(6N)，其中下界将有限阶尺度从先前的log N/log log N显著提升至N的固定幂次1/6。

Conclusion: 本文建立的上下界大幅推进了对Erdős-Hajnal开放问题加性间隙的理解，展示了覆盖码与图染色之间的深刻联系，为后续研究留下进一步优化指数的空间。

Abstract: For a graph $G$, let $ψ(G)=\max\{χ(G[V(C)]):C$ is an odd cycle of $G\}$, with $ψ(G)=0$ when $G$ is bipartite. For positive integers $N$, set $F(N)=\max\{χ(G)-ψ(G):|V(G)|\le N\}$. The function $F$ measures the finite-order additive gap arising from an open problem of Erdos and Hajnal. We prove $N^{1/6-o(1)}\le F(N)<\sqrt{6N}$. The lower bound raises the finite-order scale supplied by the Cameron-Clow path-colour construction from $\log N/\log\log N$ to a fixed power of $N$. Its proof constructs a palette-code graph from a binary covering code $\mathcal{C}\subseteq\{0,1\}^p$ and establishes the exact identities $χ(G)=2p+\ell-ρ(\mathcal{C})$ and $ψ(G)=2p$. Near-middle Hamming coverings yield the exponent $1/6$. The upper bound combines Polavarapu's connectivity theorem, the Chvatal-Erdos Hamiltonicity theorem, and maximum-independent-set stripping.

</details>


### [137] [Poisson approximations of the number of fixed points in random multiset permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00710)
*Dudley Stark*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文使用Stein方法研究随机多重集合排列中不动点数目的分布，得到了与经典泊松分布类似的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结论是n个字母的随机排列中不动点数趋近于Poisson(1)分布，但这一结果尚未推广到多重集合排列的情形，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用Stein方法对随机多重集合排列中的不动点数目进行分布逼近分析。

Result: 给出了随机多重集合排列中不动点数目的分布逼近结果，推广了经典置换场景下不动点数趋近Poisson(1)分布的结论。

Conclusion: 通过Stein方法，成功将经典随机排列不动点数的泊松逼近结果推广到多重集合排列的情形。

Abstract: For ordinary permutations on $n$ letters, the distribution of the number of fixed points of a random permutation is well known to approach the Poisson$(1)$ distribution in total variation distance as $n\to\infty$. We use Stein's method to get related results for the number of fixed points of random permutations of multisets.

</details>


### [138] [Parking Cascades: From the Simplest Sequence to Motzkin and Catalan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00735)
*Ben Adenbaum,Néstor F. Díaz Morera,Jennifer Elder,Pamela E. Harris,Molly Lynch,J. Carlos Martínez Mori*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入k级联停车函数（k-cascading parking functions），是经典停车函数的参数化变体，其中车辆可形成最多k辆车的碰撞级联。k=0时恢复经典停车函数，k=1时恢复MVP停车函数。虽然作为集合等价，但作为映射通常不同，因此论文研究其输出纤维的枚举。主要结果是一个基于置换模式的递归公式，用于计算任意置换在任意k≥0下的纤维大小。该公式在最长词处退化为插值于最简单序列（k=0）、Motzkin数（k=1）和Catalan数（k≥n-1）之间的整数序列族；在层叠置换集合处给出某些卷积三角形（包括Motzkin和Catalan卷积三角形）行和的新组合解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典停车函数已被广泛研究，但近年来引入的MVP停车函数与经典停车函数虽作为集合等价、作为映射却不同，这引出了如何系统化理解这类变体的问题。作者希望通过引入k级联停车函数这一统一参数化框架，将经典停车函数和MVP停车函数作为特例纳入同一理论体系，并通过研究输出纤维的枚举来揭示不同k值下的组合结构差异。

Method: 论文定义k级联停车函数作为停车函数的参数化变体，其中车辆碰撞级联最多涉及k辆车。作者建立了基于置换模式（permutation pattern）的递归公式来计算每个置换在给定k下的纤维大小。该公式通过置换的下降集或结构特征进行递归分解，并可特化到特定置换族（如最长词、层叠置换）以导出具体的整数序列和组合恒等式。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 任意k≥0下任意置换纤维大小的递归置换模式公式；(2) 在最长词上，公式退化为一系列插值于简单序列（k=0）、Motzkin数（k=1）和Catalan数（k≥n-1）之间的整数序列；(3) 在层叠置换上，公式为Motzkin和Catalan卷积三角形的行和提供了新的组合解释。

Conclusion: k级联停车函数提供了一个将经典停车函数与MVP停车函数统一起来的自然参数化框架。揭示的纤维枚举公式不仅统一了已知结果，还在最长词和层叠置换等特殊情形下与已知组合序列（Motzkin数、Catalan数、卷积三角形）建立了新的联系，表明这类变体具有丰富的组合结构。

Abstract: We introduce $k$-cascading parking functions, a parametrized variant of parking functions in which cars form bumping cascades of up to $k \geq 0$ cars. Setting $k = 0$ recovers classical parking functions, whereas $k = 1$ recovers MVP parking functions. Although parking functions and cascading parking functions are equivalent as sets, they are generally distinct as maps. Therefore, in this paper we consider the enumeration of the fibers of their outcomes. Our main result is a recursive, permutation pattern-based formula for the size of the fiber of any given permutation, for any given $k \geq 0$. When specialized to the longest word, the formula reduces to a family of integer sequences that interpolate between the simplest sequence ($k=0$), the Motzkin numbers ($k = 1$), and the Catalan numbers ($k\geq n-1$). When specialized to the set of layered permutations, the formula gives new combinatorial interpretations for the row sums of certain convolution triangles, including Motzkin and Catalan convolution triangles.

</details>


### [139] [Odd-Girth Bounds for Defective Edge Coloring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00791)
*Guantao Chen,Alireza Fiujlaali*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了环状无圈多重图G的(k,d)-边染色问题，即用最多k种颜色对边进行染色，使得每种颜色类构成的子图最大度不超过d。对于有奇围长g₀(G)的非二分多重图G，当d为奇数时，证明了χ'_d(G)≤⌈(g₀(G)Δ(G)-1)/(dg₀(G)-1)⌉。当d=1时退化为Goldberg关于Shannon定理的奇围长改良；当g₀(G)=3时退化为Aboulker等人关于Shannon的缺陷着色界。通过环多重图R(Δ,g₀)构造了达到等号的例子，并在一定范围内证明了缺陷版Goldberg–Seymour猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shannon定理给出了边染色数的上界⌈3Δ/2⌉，Goldberg进一步利用奇围长改进了这一结果。自然地，研究者希望将这些经典结果推广到缺陷边着色（即允许每种颜色类子图具有一定最大度d）的情形。已有工作（如Aboulker等人在g₀=3时的工作）给出了部分结果，本文旨在建立统一的一般性上界，并探讨等号取到的极值构造与缺陷版Goldberg–Seymour猜想的有效性范围。

Method: 本文采用组合证明方法，对环状无圈多重图建立(k,d)-边染色的上界估计。证明中利用了图的奇围长结构、边的重数分布以及缺陷染色的技术手段。通过构造"几乎满环多重图"R(Δ,g₀)（即奇圈上边重数交替为⌊Δ/2⌋和⌈Δ/2⌉、仅两条相邻边重数为⌊Δ/2⌋）来证明上界的最优性，并在特定参数范围内验证了缺陷版Goldberg–Seymour猜想。

Result: 证明了当d为奇数时，χ'_d(G)≤⌈(g₀(G)Δ(G)-1)/(dg₀(G)-1)⌉，该结果统一了Goldberg的奇围长改良和Aboulker等人的缺陷Shannon界。对所有奇数d>1、所有奇围长g₀≥3以及所有Δ>d，几乎满环多重图R(Δ,g₀)达到等号，表明该上界是紧的。此外，还推导出了缺陷版Goldberg–Seymour猜想成立的一个参数范围。

Conclusion: 本文给出了缺陷边着色下结合奇围长的统一上界，并通过环多重图构造证明了其紧性，同时将缺陷版Goldberg–Seymour猜想的成立范围进一步拓展，为缺陷边着色理论提供了新的重要结果。

Abstract: A $(k,d)$-edge coloring of a loopless multigraph $G$ is an edge coloring using at most $k$ colors such that the subgraph formed by each color class has maximum degree at most $d$. The least such $k$ is denoted by $χ'_d(G)$. Let $G$ be a loopless non-bipartite multigraph with maximum degree $Δ(G)$ and odd girth $g_0(G)$, and let $d\ge1$ be odd. We prove that \[ χ'_d(G)\le\left\lceil\frac{g_0(G)Δ(G)-1}{dg_0(G)-1}\right\rceil. \] For $d=1$, this is Goldberg's odd-girth refinement of Shannon's theorem, while for $g_0(G)=3$ it is the defective Shannon bound of Aboulker, Aubian, and Huang. For every odd $d>1$, every odd $g_0\ge3$, and every $Δ>d$, an almost full ring multigraph $R(Δ,g_0)$, an odd cycle with edge multiplicities alternating between $\lfloorΔ/2\rfloor$ and $\lceilΔ/2\rceil$, except that two consecutive edges have multiplicity $\lfloorΔ/2\rfloor$, attains equality. We also derive a range in which the defective Goldberg--Seymour conjecture holds.

</details>


### [140] [Small $q$-kernels in digraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00825)
*Irena Penev,Maya Stein,Ana Trujillo-Negrete*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究Spiro在2026年论文中提出的关于有向图中最小q-核存在的三个问题，其中一个问题得到肯定回答，另外两个得到否定回答，并进一步在二分有向图背景下深入研究后两个问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Spiro提出了关于有向图中"小"q-核存在性的三个开放问题，本文旨在解决这些开放问题并深化对二分有向图中q-核性质的理解。

Method: 通过构造性证明和反例来分别回答三个问题：对一个问题的肯定回答通过构造证明实现，对另外两个问题通过给出反例进行否定；随后将否定回答的两个问题专门放到二分有向图背景下重新考察，利用二分图的结构特性进行分析。

Result: 三个问题中一个得到肯定回答，两个得到否定回答。在二分有向图背景下，两个被否定问题中一个的答案是：当且仅当q为奇数时为肯定；另一个问题的答案是：当q为奇数时二分图情形下为肯定，而q为偶数时二分图情形仍为开放问题。

Conclusion: 本研究部分解决了Spiro提出的三个问题，揭示了q-核的奇偶性在二分有向图中起着关键作用，同时指出仍有开放问题值得进一步探索。

Abstract: We address three questions of Spiro [Europ. J. Combin., 133:Paper No. 104307, 2026] on smallest $q$-kernels in digraphs. All three questions are on the existence of small $q$-kernels in digraphs, where 'smallness' is measured in different ways. We answer one of these questions in the affirmative, and the other two in the negative. We further study the latter two questions in the context of bipartite digraphs. In particular, we show that in the bipartite setting, the answer to one of the two questions is affirmative if and only if $q$ is odd. For the other question, we show that the answer is positive when the digraph is bipartite and $q$ is odd (the case when the digraph is bipartite and $q$ is even remains open).

</details>


### [141] [Intersecting families of sets are usually trivial for $n\ge 2k+3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00861)
*Jiabao Yang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明：当 n ≥ 2k+3 时，几乎所有 k-均匀相交族都是平凡的（即所有成员含共同元素）。这验证了 Balogh、Garcia、Li 和 Wagner 的猜想结论，并将之前需要 n ≥ 2k+100ln k 的范围大幅改进到最优可能的 n ≥ 2k+3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极值组合学中，相交族（intersecting families）的结构问题是经典课题。Frankl和Kupavskii以及Balogh等人独立证明了在某些n的范围下几乎所有的k-均匀相交族都是平凡的，随后Balogh等人将范围改进到n ≥ 2k+100ln k，并猜想此结论在n ≥ 2k+3（即交叉集对不存在的临界范围）都应成立。本文旨在验证这一猜想。

Method: 作者采用组合计数与概率方法相结合的技术，通过对非平凡相交族的精细结构分析和计数估计，证明在 n ≥ 2k+3 的整个范围内，非平凡 k-均匀相交族的数量相对于平凡相交族（即星族，star families）的数量可以忽略不计。

Result: 成功证明了对于所有 n ≥ 2k+3，几乎所有的 k-均匀相交族都是平凡的，即绝大多数相交族的成员共享一个公共元素。这验证了 Balogh、Garcia、Li 和 Wagner 所猜想的最优结论，将已知的适用范围从 n ≥ 2k+100ln k 改进到 n ≥ 2k+3。

Conclusion: 本文在最优可能的参数范围 n ≥ 2k+3 内完全解决了一个已知猜想，证明几乎所有的 k-均匀相交族都是平凡的。这一结果在n等于下界2k+3时也成立，标志着该问题的彻底解决。

Abstract: A family of subsets of $[n]$ is called intersecting if it contains no pair of disjoint sets. It is called trivial if all its members contain a common element. Frankl and Kupavskii, and independently Balogh, Das, Liu, Sharifzadeh, and Tran, proved that there is a constant $c>0$ such that, whenever $n \geq 2k+2+c\sqrt{k\ln k}$, almost all $k$-uniform intersecting families are trivial. Balogh, Garcia, Li, and Wagner later improved this range to $n \geq 2k+100\ln k$. In this paper, we prove that the same conclusion holds for every $n\geq 2k+3$. This verifies the conjectured conclusion of Balogh, Garcia, Li, and Wagner throughout this range.

</details>


### [142] [Collinear Interior Lattice Points in Triangles Satisfying $B(T)\in\{4,5\}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00889)
*Jonathan Sakunkoo,Annabella Sakunkoo,Dana Paquin*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用阿德勒广义欧拉函数g(k)的算术性质及格点三角形的标准分类，完整刻画了B4-和B5-共线整数：仅k∈{1,2,5}是B4-共线整数，而任何整数都不是B5-共线整数，揭示了边界格点数从4到5时共线性约束消失的结构性对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已有B(T)=3研究的基础上，作者希望进一步扩展对格点三角形内点共线性质的理解，完整分类边界点数分别为4和5时的共线整数，以揭示不同边界点数下内点共线性约束的规律与差异。

Method: 结合格点三角形的标准分类（canonical lattice classifications）以及Alder广义欧拉函数g(k)的算术性质，对所有可能的B4-和B5-情形进行系统分类与逐一验证，从而确定所有符合条件的k值。

Result: 证明了B4-共线整数仅有k∈{1,2,5}；证明了不存在任何B5-共线整数。

Conclusion: 边界格点数为3和4时存在内点共线的约束，但边界格点数增至5时该约束被打破，表明共线性质随边界点数的增多而消失，呈现出结构性断裂。

Abstract: A positive integer $k$ is called $Bn$-collinear if at least one lattice triangle with $n$ boundary points ($B(T)=n$) and $k$ interior lattice points exists, and every such triangle has all of its interior points collinear. Building on prior work on $B(T)=3$, we completely classify the $B4$- and $B5$-collinear integers. Using canonical lattice classifications together with arithmetic properties of Alder's generalized totient function $g(k)$, we prove that the only $B4$-collinear integers are $k\in\{1,2,5\}$. Furthermore, we show that no integer is $B5$-collinear. This establishes a structural contrast: while three and four boundary lattice points exhibit some collinearity constraints, five boundary points disrupt the pattern.

</details>


### [143] [Asymptotically optimal bracketing covers for anchored boxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00899)
*Kosuke Suzuki*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了锚定盒子（anchored boxes）在星形偏差（star discrepancy）中的有限离散化问题，即括号覆盖（bracketing covers）和δ-覆盖（δ-covers）的数量下界与上界。证明了下界N[]（d,δ）≥⌈δ^{-d}⌉和N(d,δ)≥⌈(d!/d^d)δ^{-d}⌉，并构造了针对固定维数d的括号覆盖，结合下界表明N[]（d,δ）=(1+o_d(1))δ^{-d}（当δ→0时），同时给出了上界的显式估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星形偏差是拟蒙特卡洛（quasi-Monte Carlo）方法中的重要度量，其覆盖数（covering numbers）和括号覆盖数（bracketing numbers）的精确渐近行为对于理解偏差的离散化复杂度至关重要。此前缺乏对这些数量紧的下界和上界估计，特别是对括号覆盖数在维数固定时的精确渐近行为尚不明了，因此需要系统性地刻画这些离散化工具的数量级。

Method: 本文采用两种主要技术手段：(1) 通过合适的计数论证建立下界估计；(2) 构造性方法——将粗划分（coarse partition）与依赖于盒子的各向异性局部网格（box-dependent anisotropic local grids）相结合，构造出具有共享顶点的括号覆盖，从而同时得到δ-覆盖的渐近最优上界系数。

Result: 证明了括号覆盖数满足N[]（d,δ）≥⌈δ^{-d}⌉，δ-覆盖数满足N(d,δ)≥⌈(d!/d^d)δ^{-d}⌉。对于任意固定维数d，所构造的括号覆盖与下界相结合，证明了N[]（d,δ）=(1+o_d(1))δ^{-d}（δ→0时），且构造的共享顶点结构使得δ-覆盖的上界渐近系数为1，同时获得了两个量的显式上界。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了星形偏差离散化中括号覆盖数和δ-覆盖数的渐近行为：括号覆盖数在固定维数下趋近于δ^{-d}，δ-覆盖数下界系数为d!/d^d且构造方法达到渐近最优的上界系数1。这些结果深化了对覆盖数与括号覆盖数之间差距的理解，为星形偏差的有效计算提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Bracketing covers and $δ$-covers provide finite discretizations of the anchored boxes that define the star discrepancy. Let $N_{[]}(d,δ)$ and $N(d,δ)$ denote the corresponding bracketing and covering numbers. We prove the lower bounds \[
  N_{[]}(d,δ)\ge \lceil δ^{-d}\rceil,
  \qquad
  N(d,δ)\ge
  \left\lceil \frac{d!}{d^d}\,δ^{-d}\right\rceil. \] We also construct, for every fixed $d$, bracketing covers which, together with the lower bound, show that $N_{[]}(d,δ)=(1+o_d(1))δ^{-d}$ as $δ\downarrow0$.
