The construction combines a coarse partition with box-dependent anisotropic local grids. Its shared vertices yield $δ$-covers with asymptotic upper coefficient one. Explicit upper bounds are obtained for both quantities.

</details>


### [144] [An explicit construction of two completely independent spanning trees in the four-dimensional dual-cube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00900)
*Jitendra Prajapati*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文解决了n维双立方图F_n关于两条完全独立生成树的悬而未决问题：通过显式构造证明F_4也满足该性质，从而完成分类——F_n存在两条完全独立生成树当且仅当n≥4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lalou等人证明了F_n（n≥5）存在两条完全独立生成树，且n≤3时不存在，但n=4的情况经过700多小时计算仍未解决，成为分类中的唯一不确定情形。

Method: 通过一个在七个顶点比特上的F_2上十项三次多项式的水平集来定义两棵树的内部顶点集，将正确性归约为有限连通性检验，并由一个无求解器（solver-free）的机器验证程序进行验证。

Result: 显式构造证实F_4存在两条完全独立生成树（使用了254条边中的254条），完成分类：F_n存在两条完全独立生成树当且仅当n≥4。此外，在相同的搜索模型下证明了仿射规则和二次规则不可行，且十项是三次规则所需的最少项数。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了双立方图完全独立生成树问题中的最后一例，完成了所有维度的分类，并通过精确不可行性结果刻画了可行构造的复杂度下界。

Abstract: Lalou, Mbarek, Skender and Togni (arXiv:2607.25917) proved that the $n$-dimensional dual-cube $F_n$ admits two completely independent spanning trees for every $n\ge 5$, observed that none exist for $n\le 3$, and identified $F_4$ as the first unresolved case, reporting more than 700 hours of inconclusive computation. We settle this case affirmatively by an explicit construction, completing the classification: $F_n$ admits two completely independent spanning trees if and only if $n\ge 4$. The internal-vertex sets of the two trees are the level sets of a single ten-term cubic polynomial over $\mathbb{F}_2$ in the seven vertex bits, and correctness reduces to finite connectivity checks that are machine-verified by a solver-free program distributed with the certificate. In $F_4$ the two trees necessarily use 254 of the 256 edges. We also report exact infeasibility results for simpler rules of the same shape: within the search model, no affine or quadratic rule works, and ten terms is the fewest possible for a cubic rule.

</details>


### [145] [Counterexamples to a conjecture of Hoa on maximal non-Hamiltonian graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00957)
*Xingzhi Zhan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文否定了Vu Dinh Hoa在1994年提出的猜想：最大非哈密顿图G的最长圈C的补图G-V(C)是完整图。作者构造了每个阶数n≥56的反例，从而反驳了该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大非哈密顿图是图论中一个重要研究课题。Vu Dinh Hoa在1994年提出了关于最长圈补图结构的猜想，验证或反驳该猜想对理解最大非哈密顿图的结构具有重要意义。

Method: 通过构造性的方法，为每个阶数n≥56的系统构造反例，从而反驳Vu Dinh Hoa的猜想。

Result: 成功构造出每个阶数n≥56的反例，证明Vu Dinh Hoa的猜想是错误的。

Conclusion: Vu Dinh Hoa关于最大非哈密顿图最长圈补图是完整图的猜想被反驳，揭示了最大非哈密顿图的结构比猜想所描述的更为复杂。

Abstract: A graph G is said to be maximal non-Hamiltonian if G is non-Hamiltonian, but $G+e$ is Hamiltonian for every nonedge $e$ of $G.$ In 1994, Vu Dinh Hoa conjectured that if $C$ is a longest cycle of a maximal non-Hamiltonian graph $G,$ then $G-V(C)$ is a complete graph. We disprove this conjecture by constructing a counterexample of every order $n\ge 56.$

</details>


### [146] [Permutation of edges in mutation reduction of pointed Brauer trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01036)
*Reut Frenkel-Mayzlish,Mary Schaps,Zehavit Zvi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文定义了有指向的广义Aihara算法，证明其置换是恒等的，并给出了Kozakai算法所产生置换的一般形式，用单枝二叉树示例进行了说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aihara算法（突变约简）可以在Brauer树代数中通过边上的突变序列从一般Brauer树代数简化为更简单的Brauer星代数。Schaps和Zvi指出不同算法给出边置换，Kozakai则给出了依赖于指向的新算法。本文旨在进一步理解这些置换的结构，特别是识别出产生恒等置换的算法，并给出Kozakai算法置换的一般形式。

Method: 作者定义了一个有指向的广义Aihara算法，该算法在给定指向的基础上运行突变约简序列，并证明该算法产生的置换是恒等置换。随后，作者推导出Kozakai算法所产生置换的一般数学形式，并通过单枝二叉树的具体例子对该一般形式进行了说明和验证。

Result: 证明了有指向的广义Aihara算法的置换为恒等映射；给出了Kozakai算法置换的一般形式，并用单枝二叉树的例子进行了展示。

Conclusion: 本文深化了对Brauer树代数突变约简中置换结构的理解，证明了广义Aihara算法保持边的恒等置换，同时系统化了Kozakai算法的置换形式，为该领域的进一步代数结构研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Aihara developed an algorithm for Brauer tree algebras, which we call a mutation reduction, for getting from a Brauer tree algebra to the simpler Brauer star algebra using a sequence of mutations centered on edges. Schaps and Zvi, using the
  Schaps-Zakay theory of pointing the tree, showed that different algorithms for the sequence of mutations give permutations of the edges.
  Kozakai gave a new algorithm for a mutation reduction that depends on a given pointing and describes the evolution of the pointing under the mutation reduction.
  In this paper, we define a pointed generalized Aihara algorithm and show that its permutation is the identity. We give a general form for the permutations resulting from Kozakai's algorithm, which we illustrate with examples from uni-branch binary trees.

</details>


### [147] [The equality case of Ehrhart's volume conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01040)
*Jihao Liu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于凸体与格点理论的定理：在R^n中，任何重心为唯一内部格点、体积为(n+1)^n/n!的全维紧凸体，必是单纯形(n+1)Δ_n-(1,…,1)的单模像。这解决了Ehrhart体积猜想中的等式情形，与OpenAI近期证明的不等式部分互为补充。该结果由生成式AI（GPT-5.6-sol、Fable 5及Danus系统）获得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ehrhart体积猜想的核心问题在于，对于重心为唯一内部格点的凸体，其体积存在一个紧致下界。不等式部分已由OpenAI证明，本文旨在解决其等式情形，即刻画达到该体积下界的极值凸体结构，从而为猜想提供完整的理论闭环。

Method: 本文采用生成式人工智能方法进行定理的发现与证明，具体涉及GPT-5.6-sol、Fable 5及Danus系统等AI工具，通过对凸体几何性质、格点结构与体积约束条件的综合推理，给出等式情形的完整刻画。

Result: 证明了满足条件的凸体必然同构于标准单纯形(n+1)Δ_n-(1,…,1)的单模仿射像，从而完全解决了Ehrhart体积猜想的等式情形。

Conclusion: 该结果深化了我们对Ehrhart体积猜想结构性的理解，确立了极值凸体的唯一性刻画，并展示了生成式AI在解决高难度数学问题上的潜力。

Abstract: We prove that every full-dimensional compact convex body in $\mathbb R^n$ whose barycenter is its unique interior lattice point and whose volume is $(n+1)^n/n!$ is a unimodular image of the simplex $(n+1)Δ_n-(1,\dots,1)$. This resolves the equality case of Ehrhart's volume conjecture, as a counterpart of the inequality part recently proved by OpenAI. The main result of this paper is obtained by generative AI, particularly GPT-5.6-sol, Fable 5, and the Danus system.

</details>


### [148] [Joins and ear decompositions beyond graphic matroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01059)
*Yuhang Bai,Kristóf Bérczi,Chaitanya Nalam*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究拟阵（matroid）中的"join"概念（与每个圈至多交于一半元素的集合）及其最大大小μ(M)，与基于耳分解的参数η(M)之间的关系。Frank定理保证了对连通图拟阵有μ(M)=η(M)，但本文证明该等式不适用于更一般的类（如余图拟阵），并证明计算最大join是NP难的。尽管存在这些负面结果，文章仍为二值、铺砌、余图及任意连通拟阵建立了定量比较界，特别是利用Seymour分解定理证明了正则拟阵满足η(M)≤6μ(M)-2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Frank的min-max定理表明对连通图拟阵μ(M)=η(M)，自然的问题是这一等式能推广到多远。作者希望考察该等式在拟阵类中成立的边界，并探索更一般的定量比较关系。

Method: 作者通过构造反例证明等式在余图拟阵中失效且该性质非minor-closed；通过归约证明计算最大join在余图拟阵中是NP-hard且难以在519/520因子内近似，对稀疏铺砌拟阵也是NP-hard。正面结果方面，结合Seymour分解定理将图拟阵的等式、余图拟阵的界以及R_10的直接分析组合起来，推导正则拟阵的比较界。

Result: 证明μ(M)=η(M)在余图拟阵中不成立，满足等式的拟阵类非minor-closed；计算最大join在余图拟阵和稀疏铺砌拟阵中NP-hard，近似难度下界为519/520。同时建立了二值、铺砌、余图及任意连通拟阵中的比较界，特别对正则拟阵得到η(M)≤6μ(M)-2。

Conclusion: Frank定理的精确等式无法推广到图拟阵之外的类，但参数μ和η在多个自然拟阵类中保持定量可比。未来方向可能涉及改进比较常数或寻找其他结构性拟阵类。

Abstract: For a matroid $M$, a join is a set $J\subseteq E(M)$ that meets every circuit $C$ in at most $|C|/2$ elements. Let $μ(M)$ denote the maximum size of a join. Motivated by Frank's min--max theorem for graphic matroids, we compare $μ(M)$ with an ear-decomposition parameter $η(M)=(r(M)+\varphi(M))/2$, where $\varphi(M)$ is the minimum number of even lobes in an ear decomposition of $M$. Frank's theorem implies $μ(M)=η(M)$ for connected graphic matroids.
  Here we study how far this equality extends beyond graphic matroids. We show that the exact equality does not hold in general: it already fails for cographic matroids, hence within the binary class. Furthermore, the class of matroids satisfying $μ(M)=η(M)$ is not minor-closed, thus there is little hope for a forbidden minor characterization. We also prove that computing a maximum join is NP-hard for cographic matroids, hard to approximate within a factor of $519/520$, and NP-hard for sparse paving matroids given by their list of bases. Despite these negative results, we show that the two parameters remain quantitatively comparable in several natural classes. We prove comparison bounds for binary, paving, cographic, and arbitrary connected matroids. In particular, using Seymour's decomposition theorem, we combine the equality for graphic matroids, the bound for cographic matroids, and a direct analysis of $R_{10}$ to obtain $η(M)\leq 6μ(M)-2$ for every regular matroid $M$.

</details>


### [149] [Congruent Triangular Faces, Reflections Allowed: Universal Realization and the Minimum Face Count in Problem B22](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01109)
*George M. Georgiou*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文解决了几何未解问题B22中关于三角形作为凸多面体公共面的存在性及最小面数问题（允许反射副本）。证明了每个非退化欧几里得三角形都能作为一个组合八面体的八个全等面，并确定了所有三角形的最小面数：锐角三角形为4，满足特定边长条件的直角或钝角等腰三角形为6，其余情况为8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何未解问题B22提出了一个基本问题：哪些三角形可以作为凸多面体的公共面，需要多少个副本，以及如何排列。此前该问题尚未解决，作者旨在回答三个子问题中的存在性和最小面数部分，为这一长期未解问题提供确定性的答案。

Method: 作者采用构造性方法：首先构造一个显式的凸多面体（组合学上为八面体），其八个面全部全等于任意给定的三角形，从而证明存在性。随后通过对三角形形状的分类讨论（锐角、直角、钝角以及等腰条件），逐一确定不同情形下的最小面数，并分别给出构造或下界证明。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）每个非退化三角形都可以作为凸八面体的8个全等面出现；（2）最小面数完全刻画：锐角三角形需要4个面；满足λ√2≤β<λ√3的直角或钝角等腰三角形(λ,λ,β)需要6个面；其余所有情况需要8个面。但作者未对所有可达面数进行分类，也未刻画所有可能的排列方式。

Conclusion: 本文在允许反射副本的前提下，对B22问题的存在性和最小面数两个子问题给出了完整的解答。文章明确了三角形作为多面体公共面的最小副本数量，为进一步研究面数的完整分类和所有可能的几何排列奠定了基础。

Abstract: Problem B22 in Unsolved Problems in Geometry asks which triangles occur as the common face of a convex polyhedron, how many copies are needed, and how they may be arranged. We settle the existence and minimum-face-count questions for triangles in the version that allows reflected copies; we do not classify all attainable face counts, nor the possible arrangements. Every nondegenerate Euclidean triangle occurs: we exhibit an explicit convex polyhedron, combinatorially an octahedron, all eight of whose faces are congruent to a prescribed triangle. We then determine the minimum number of faces for \emph{every} triangle. It is four for an acute triangle; six for a right or obtuse isosceles triangle with side lengths $(λ,λ,β)$ satisfying $λ\sqrt2\leqβ<λ\sqrt3$; and eight in all remaining cases.

</details>


### [150] [Vertex-distinguishing chromatic index of digraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01122)
*Yuping Gao,Zijun Qin,Songling Shan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 作者证明了2016年Li、Bai、He和Sun提出的猜想：对于至多一个源点和一个汇点的有向图D，其顶点区分弧着色色数χ′_vd(D)等于自然下界k(D)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该猜想由Li等人在2016年提出，旨在确定有向图顶点区分弧着色的精确色数下界k(D)是否总能达到。确认该猜想将为有向图的顶点区分弧着色理论提供完整刻画。

Method: 通过构造满足顶点区分条件的弧着色方案，证明在至多一个源点和一个汇点的条件下，χ′_vd(D)可以达到理论下界k(D)，从而证实猜想成立。

Result: 成功证明了该猜想对所有至多一个源点和一个汇点的有向图D成立，即χ′_vd(D)=k(D)。

Conclusion: 本文确认了Li等人提出的猜想，填补了有向图顶点区分弧着色精确色数研究中的一个空白。

Abstract: Let $D$ be a digraph. In this note, an \emph{arc coloring} of $D$ is an assignment of colors to the arcs of $D$ such that no two arcs with a common tail receive the same color and no two arcs with a common head receive the same color. Under such a coloring, each vertex $v$ is associated with an \emph{out-color set} and an \emph{in-color set}, consisting of the colors assigned to the arcs with tail $v$ and to the arcs with head $v$, respectively. An arc coloring of $D$ is \emph{vertex-distinguishing} if any two distinct vertices have different out-color sets and different in-color sets. The minimum number of colors required for a vertex-distinguishing arc coloring of $D$ is called the \emph{vertex-distinguishing chromatic index} of $D$, denoted $χ_{vd}^{\prime}(D)$. In 2016, Li, Bai, He, and Sun conjectured that $χ_{vd}^{\prime}(D)=k(D)$ for any digraph $D$ with at most one source and at most one sink, where $k(D)$ is a natural lower bound determined by the outdegree and indegree sequences of $D$. We confirm this conjecture.

</details>


### [151] [Convolutive sequences, II: Parametrizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01136)
*Shane Chern,Dennis Eichhorn,Shishuo Fu,James A. Sellers*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文统一了先前研究中发现的多个m-卷积不定式(convolutive)序列的实例，这些序列源自原始eta乘积，作者通过参数化方式将所有已知实例（除一个例外）统一起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作仅证明了少量由原始eta乘积产生的序列具有2-和3-卷积性。自那以后作者发现了许多新的卷积性eta乘积实例，需要一个统一框架来系统理解和组织这些分散的结果。

Method: 作者将所有已知的卷积性eta乘积实例（除一个外）通过参数化方式进行统一，即寻找一个包含参数的通用构造，使得不同的具体实例成为该参数化形式下的特例。

Result: 成功地以参数化方式统一了所有已知的卷积性eta乘积实例（除一个例外），建立了一个统一的理论框架。

Conclusion: 通过参数化统一的方式，本文系统化了卷积性eta乘积的研究，为后续更全面的理论奠定了基础。

Abstract: In recent work, the authors defined a sequence $(a_n)_{n\ge 0}$ to be $m$-convolutive exactly if
  \begin{align*}
  \sum_{n\ge 0} a_{mn} q^n = \left(\sum_{n\ge 0} a_n q^n\right)^m
  \end{align*}
  for a specific positive integer $m$ and provided proofs of the $2$- and $3$-convolutivity of a small number of sequences arising from primitive eta-products. Since the completion of that work, the authors have discovered many new instances of convolutive eta-products. The main focus of this work is to unify all but one of these instances in a parametric way.

</details>


### [152] [Wei duality, Fomin-Greene duality and demimatroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01187)
*Thomas Britz*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文精确描述了Fomin-Greene对偶定理（针对有限偏序集）与Wei对偶定理（针对demimatroid）之间的关系。研究表明，链数和反链数分别满足一种Wei型对偶（与反链删除数和链删除数对应），且对于每个至少含两个元素的偏序集，其链demimatroid和反链demimatroid无法通过任何标准demimatroid对偶操作的组合相关联。然而，它们的上Wei数通过Fomin-Greene对偶相互确定。因此，这两种对偶在Wei数上一致，但在底层秩函数上不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fomin-Greene对偶定理和Wei对偶定理分别针对有限偏序集和demimatroid，都涉及极值不变量的对偶关系，表面上十分相似，但二者之间的精确联系此前未被阐明。作者希望厘清这两种对偶在结构上的异同。

Method: 通过构造链demimatroid和反链demimatroid，系统地分析Wei型对偶与Fomin-Greene对偶之间的关系；利用标准demimatroid对偶操作（如组合）检验两种demimatroid是否可由这些操作互相关联；通过上Wei数比较两种对偶的作用效果。

Result: 链数和反链数各自满足与反链删除数和链删除数对应的Wei型对偶；对于每个至少有两个元素的偏序集，其链demimatroid和反链demimatroid不能通过任何标准demimatroid对偶操作的组合相关联；但二者的上Wei数通过Fomin-Greene对偶互相决定。

Conclusion: 两种对偶在Wei数层面是一致的，但在底层秩函数（demimatroid）层面并不一致。这一发现澄清了Fomin-Greene对偶与Wei对偶之间的精确联系，并揭示了它们在结构上的本质差异。

Abstract: The Fomin-Greene Duality Theorem for finite posets and Wei's Duality Theorem for demimatroids each relate sets of extremal invariants via seemingly similar dualities. This paper describes precisely how these two dualities relate. It is shown that the chain and antichain numbers each satisfy a Wei-type duality with antichain- and chain-deletion numbers, respectively, and that, for each poset with at least two elements, its chain and antichain demimatroids are not related by any composition of standard demimatroid duality operations. Their upper Wei-number numbers do however determine each other via Fomin-Greene duality. The two dualities thereby coincide for Wei numbers but not for the underlying rank functions.

</details>


### [153] [On three open problems in zero-sum Ramsey numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01206)
*Cheng Chi,Jialin He,Quan Sun*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究r-一致超图的k色Ramsey数与零和Ramsey数，解决了Caro和Provstgaard关于Δ-系统（偶数条边）在Z_2上零和Ramsey数的猜想，证明了Caro关于森林不相交并的零和Ramsey数猜想不成立（对双星结构给出反例），并构建了无限族树回答了Caro提出的关于R(T,Z_m)>R(T,2)的存在性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零和Ramsey数与经典Ramsey数之间存在紧密联系，但二者的精确关系在许多情形下仍未被完全理解。本文旨在解决Caro等人提出的三个关于零和Ramsey数与经典Ramsey数之间关系的猜想与开放问题，从而深化对这两类Ramsey数的认识。

Method: 本文采用组合构造与反例方法。对于Δ-系统的零和Ramsey数，确定精确值并证明猜想；对于森林tF的零和Ramsey数猜想，通过对双星结构构造反例证伪该猜想；对于树的存在性问题，构造了一个无限族的树T满足R(T,Z_m)>R(T,2)。

Result: (1) 确定了具有偶数条边的Δ-系统在Z_2上的零和Ramsey数，证明了Caro-Provstgaard猜想；(2) 通过双星反例驳斥了Caro关于R(tF,Z_{mt})=R(tF,2)（对所有充分大的t成立）的猜想；(3) 构造了无限族树回答了Caro的开放问题，即存在树T使R(T,Z_m)>R(T,2)。

Conclusion: 本文在零和Ramsey数与经典Ramsey数的关系研究中取得了三方面进展：证明了一个猜想、推翻了一个猜想并回答了一个开放问题。结果表明，零和Ramsey数与经典Ramsey数的相等关系并非普遍成立，且Δ-系统的零和Ramsey数可被精确确定。

Abstract: Let $K_N^{(r)}$ denote the $N$-vertex complete $r$-uniform hypergraph. For an $r$-uniform hypergraph $H$ and an integer $k\geq2$, the $k$-color Ramsey number $R(H,k)$ is the least integer $N$ such that every $k$-edge-coloring of $K_N^{(r)}$ contains a monochromatic copy of $H$. When $k\mid\esize(H)$, the zero-sum Ramsey number $R(H,\mathbb Z_k)$ is the least integer $N$ such that every edge-labeling of $K_N^{(r)}$ by elements of $\mathbb Z_k$ contains a copy of $H$ whose edge labels sum to $0$ in $\mathbb Z_k$.
  We settle two conjectures and a problem concerning these two Ramsey numbers. First, Caro and Provstgaard proposed exact values for the zero-sum Ramsey numbers over $\mathbb Z_2$ of delta-systems with an even number of edges. We determine these numbers and thereby prove their conjecture. Second, for a forest $F$ with $m$ edges, let $tF$ denote the disjoint union of $t$ copies of $F$. Caro conjectured that $R(tF,\mathbb Z_{mt})=R(tF,2)$ for all sufficiently large $t$. We show that this conjecture does not hold for double stars. Caro also asked whether there exists a tree $T$ with $m$ edges such that $R(T,\mathbb Z_m)>R(T,2)$. We answer this question affirmatively by constructing an infinite family of such trees.

</details>


### [154] [Square-Difference-Free Sets beyond the Three-Quarter Barrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01325)
*Dmitry Krachun*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的构造方法，证明了不存在非零平方差的最大子集基数D(N)的下界指数突破3/4这一自然障碍，达到α* = 0.7527964558…，优于此前Beigel-Gasarch和Lewko的0.7334117…的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős猜想不存在非零平方差的子集的最大基数下界(1-o(1))N^{1/2}在相差polylog因子意义下是紧的，但这一猜想被Sárközy和Ruzsa反驳。Ruzsa找到了下界指数为0.733077…的构造，但其方法存在3/4指数的自然瓶颈。突破这一瓶颈是组合数论中的一个重要开放问题。

Method: 本文开发了一种全新的构造方法，区别于Ruzsa的原始方法，成功跨越了指数3/4的自然障碍。所提构造的下界指数α* = 0.7527964558…来源于一个简单的优化问题的解。

Result: 证明了liminf_{N→∞} log D(N)/log N ≥ α* := 0.7527964558…，这是迄今为止最好的下界，超越了此前最好的指数0.7334117…，并首次突破了Ruzsa方法的3/4屏障。

Conclusion: 新的构造方法成功跨越了Ruzsa方法固有的3/4指数障碍，得到的常数0.7527964558…源自简单优化问题，并且似乎是新方法的极限。

Abstract: Let $D(N)$ denote the largest cardinality of a subset of $\{1,\ldots,N\}$ containing no nonzero square difference. While a construction certifying $D(N)\geq (1-o(1))N^{1/2}$ is almost trivial, Erdős conjectured that this bound is sharp up to polylogarithmic factors. This was disproved by Sárközy and later again by Ruzsa, who found an elegant construction showing that $D(N)\geq c\cdot N^{0.733077\dots}$, with an absolute constant $c>0$. His approach was subsequently refined, leading to the previously best known lower bound with exponent $0.7334117\dots$ due to Beigel-Gasarch and, independently, Lewko. However, in the original paper Ruzsa observed that $3/4$ seems to be the natural barrier of his approach.
  In this paper we develop a new construction leading to the lower bound \[
  \liminf_{N\to\infty}\frac{\log D(N)}{\log N}
  \geq α_*:= 0.7527964558\ldots; \] thus crossing the natural exponent-$3/4$ barrier of Ruzsa's method. The value $0.7527964558\ldots$ arises from a simple optimisation problem and appears to be the limit of the new approach.

</details>


### [155] [Freeness of Arrangements with Regular Underlying Matroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01331)
*Weikang Liang,Suijie Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文对底层拟阵为正则拟阵的有限中心超平面排列的分类自由性进行了完整分类。对于此类排列，自由性等价于拟阵是超可解的，等价于存在好的划分（nice partition），等价于它是弦图简单图的圈拟阵。该分类独立于基域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超平面排列的自由性（freeness）是一个重要的代数性质，但判断排列是否自由通常很困难。作者希望为正则拟阵底层的排列提供完全的组合分类，从而将自由性这一代数条件转化为纯粹的组合条件，不依赖于基域。

Method: 作者使用Seymour关于正则拟阵的分解定理来证明自由性蕴含超可解性。同时，作者刻画了有限简单二元拟阵的好的划分（nice partition）：一个划分是好的当且仅当它是独立的且没有任何一条线包含在单个块中。由此得出有限无环二元拟阵存在好的划分当且仅当它是简单的且超可解的。

Result: 建立了正则拟阵底层超平面排列自由性的完整组合分类：自由性 ⟺ 超可解性 ⟺ 存在好的划分 ⟺ 是弦图简单图的圈拟阵。该分类独立于基域。此外，给出了二元拟阵nice partition的完全刻画。

Conclusion: 对于底层拟阵为正则拟阵的中心排列，自由性完全由组合结构决定，无需知道基域。这提供了一种纯组合的方法来判定这类排列的自由性，并通过Seymour分解定理和nice partition的刻画建立了自由性与超可解性之间的等价关系。

Abstract: We classify freeness for finite central arrangements whose underlying matroids are regular. Let $\mathcal A$ be such an arrangement over an arbitrary field, and put $M=M(\mathcal A)$. Then $\mathcal A$ is free if and only if $M$ is supersolvable; equivalently, $M$ admits a nice partition; equivalently, $M$ is the cycle matroid of a chordal simple graph. Thus, for arrangements with regular underlying matroids, freeness has a complete combinatorial classification independent of the base field.
  We use Seymour's decomposition theorem for regular matroids to prove that freeness forces supersolvability. We also characterize nice partitions of finite simple binary matroids: a partition is nice if and only if it is independent and no line is contained in a single block. Consequently, a finite loopless binary matroid admits a nice partition if and only if it is simple and supersolvable.

</details>


### [156] [The Middle Stair for Complete Bipartite Parallel Chip-Firing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01350)
*Minkyu Jung*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了完全二分图上的中阶梯猜想（middle-stair conjecture），通过引入两参数共轭配置c^{k,ℓ}，证明了当K_{a,b}上的平行芯片博弈配置σ满足2ab-a-b<|σ|<2ab时，其最终周期为2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中阶梯猜想是关于平行芯片博弈（parallel chip-firing game）最终周期行为的一个重要猜想。此前Ji、Li和Wang通过单参数共轭配置证明了平衡情形K_{a,a}，但非平衡的完全二分图情形仍然开放。本文旨在将该结果推广到所有完全二分图K_{a,b}。

Method: 作者引入了两参数共轭配置c^{k,ℓ}，其中一侧的秩偏移（rank shift）为另一侧提供加性偏移。这些共轭配置同时保持芯片总数和活度（activity）。通过精确的Ferrers图计数，构造一个非负共轭配置，使其在一侧具有两轮发射覆盖（two-round firing coverage）。该覆盖以交替的两轮波形式传播，从而得到活度为1/2，最终由非丛集性（non-clumpiness）推出周期为2。

Result: 成功证明了完全二分图上的中阶梯猜想：对于任意完全二分图K_{a,b}，若配置σ满足2ab-a-b<|σ|<2ab，则其最终周期为2。

Conclusion: 本文通过将单参数共轭配置推广为两参数形式，并利用Ferrers图计数和两轮波的传播分析，完整解决了中阶梯猜想在完全二分图上的情形，扩展了此前仅适用于平衡情形的结果。

Abstract: We prove the middle-stair conjecture for every complete bipartite graph. If a parallel chip-firing game on $K_{a,b}$ has configuration $σ$ with $2ab-a-b<|σ|<2ab$, then its eventual period is $2$. The balanced case $K_{a,a}$ was proved by Ji, Li, and Wang using one-parameter conjugate configurations. We introduce two-parameter conjugates $c^{k,\ell}$, in which the rank shift on one side supplies the additive offset on the other. These conjugates preserve both the total number of chips and the activity. An exact Ferrers-diagram count then produces a nonnegative conjugate with two-round firing coverage on one side. The coverage propagates in alternating two-round waves, giving activity $1/2$; non-clumpiness then forces period $2$.

</details>


### [157] [Three Graffiti.pc Conjectures on Largest Induced Trees: Proofs of Conjectures 141, 142, and 143](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01396)
*Alper Ferudun*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文针对DeLaViña's Graffiti.pc程序提出的三个猜想（Conjectures 141, 142, 143）给出了完整证明，这些猜想涉及有限简单图G的诱导树最大阶数t(G)与围长g(G)之间的下界关系。三个定理分别给出了t(G)关于围长、离心度（periphery相关函数f(G)）以及次小度（δ'(G)）的积分下界，所有证明均以Lean 4形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeLaViña的Graffiti.pc程序通过计算机辅助推测生成了大量图论猜想，其中编号为141、142、143的三个猜想涉及图的诱导树阶数与围长之间的关系。这些猜想此前未被证明，而验证这些自动生成的猜想有助于理解图的树结构与圈结构之间的深层联系，也是Graffiti.pc程序推测可靠性的重要验证。

Method: 论文逐一证明了三个猜想。第一个猜想使用邻域独立数ℓ(v)（顶点v的邻域诱导子图的独立数），证明t(G) ≥ ⌊g(G)/2⌋ - 1 + max_v ℓ(v)。第二个猜想引入f(G) = max_x d(x, Per(G))（顶点到图的外围Per(G)的最大距离），证明t(G) ≥ (2/3)g(G) + f(G)，并在G含圈时强化为t(G) ≥ f(G) + ⌈2g(G)/3⌉。第三个猜想利用δ'(G)（按重数计次的次小度），证明每个连通非树图满足t(G)δ'(G) ≥ g(G) + 1。所有证明都配套了完整的Lean 4机器可验证的形式化证明。

Result: 成功证明了Graffiti.pc的猜想141、142和143：1) t(G) ≥ ⌊g(G)/2⌋ - 1 + max_v ℓ(v)；2) t(G) ≥ (2/3)g(G) + f(G)，含圈时更强地有t(G) ≥ f(G) + ⌈2g(G)/3⌉；3) t(G)δ'(G) ≥ g(G) + 1（对所有连通非树图）。此外，三个命题均已在Lean 4定理证明器中完成机器验证。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了Graffiti.pc程序产生的三个关于诱导树阶数与围长的下界猜想，建立了图论中树结构与圈结构之间新的定量关系。通过Lean 4的形式化验证，确保了证明的严谨性，展示了机器学习与形式化证明相结合进行图论研究的可行性。

Abstract: For a finite simple graph $G$, let $t(G)$ be the largest order of an induced tree and let $g(G)$ be the girth. We prove three consecutive conjectures of DeLaViña's Graffiti.pc program. First, writing $\ell(v)$ for the independence number of the subgraph induced by the neighbourhood of $v$, we prove $t(G) \ge \lfloor g(G)/2 \rfloor - 1 + \max_{v \in V(G)} \ell(v)$. Second, if $\mathrm{Per}(G)$ is the periphery and $f(G) = \max_x d(x, \mathrm{Per}(G))$, we prove $t(G) \ge \frac{2}{3} g(G) + f(G)$, and establish the stronger integral bound $t(G) \ge f(G) + \lceil 2g(G)/3 \rceil$ when $G$ contains a cycle. Third, if $δ'(G)$ is the second-smallest degree, counted with multiplicity, then every connected non-tree graph satisfies $t(G) δ'(G) \ge g(G) + 1$. These are Conjectures 141, 142, and 143 of Written on the Wall II. Complete, machine-checked Lean 4 proofs of all three formal statements accompany the manuscript.

</details>


### [158] [Binary X-rays of doubly stochastic matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01442)
*Bangzheng Li,Yuewei Liu,Yuan Yao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究排列的X射线（置换矩阵对角线上元素之和的序列），将二值X射线序列的必要约束条件重新表达为允许实值松弛的形式，证明了这些二值序列总是某个双随机矩阵的X射线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排列的X射线满足一些关于值分布的必要约束条件，这些条件被猜想在序列为二值时也是充分的。证明该猜想需要将约束条件转化为可处理的数学形式。

Method: 将二值X射线序列所满足的约束条件重新表达为允许实值松弛的形式，从而将问题从排列矩阵扩展到双随机矩阵的框架中进行分析。

Result: 证明了所有满足必要约束的二值序列都是某个双随机矩阵的X射线，这为最终证明约束充分性猜想提供了关键中间步骤。

Conclusion: 通过实值松弛技术，将二值X射线序列与双随机矩阵联系起来，推进了关于X射线约束充分性这一猜想的研究。

Abstract: The X-ray of a permutation is a sequence of sums along each diagonal of the associated permutation matrix. They satisfy certain necessary constraints on distribution of the values, which are conjectured to be sufficient when the sequence is binary. By re-expressing the constraints in a form that allows for real-valued relaxations, we prove that these binary sequences are always X-rays of doubly stochastic matrices.

</details>


### [159] [Square roots in the Appell group and Sprugnoli arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01497)
*Paul Barry*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个从幂级数对到Sprugnoli矩阵群的特殊映射。当第二个参数为偶幂级数时，所得Sprugnoli矩阵的平方是Riordan群中Appell子群的加气元素（aerated element），从而可以探索加气Appell子群中元素的平方根。由于单位元属于该子群，进一步探讨了Sprugnoli群中的相关对合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Riordan群中加气Appell子群的结构，特别是其元素的平方根问题，并通过Sprugnoli矩阵群建立新的映射关系来研究这一代数结构。

Method: 定义了一个从两个幂级数到Sprugnoli矩阵群的特定映射，分析其第二个参数为偶幂级数时矩阵平方的性质，将其与Riordan群的Appell子群中的加气元素联系起来，并利用此性质研究平方根及对合。

Result: 证明了该映射在特定条件下生成的Sprugnoli矩阵的平方落在加气Appell子群中，进而给出了加气Appell子群中元素平方根的探究途径，并识别出Sprugnoli群中的相关对合。

Conclusion: 该映射为研究Sprugnoli群与Riordan群中Appell子群之间的代数联系提供了新工具，揭示了加气Appell元素平方根的结构，并指明了对合的存在与构造方法。

Abstract: We introduce a special mapping from pairs of power series to the group of Sprugnoli matrices. This mapping has the property when the second argument is an even power series, then the square of the resulting Sprugnoli array is an aerated element of the Appell subgroup of the Riordan group. This allows us to explore the square roots of elements in the aerated Appell subgroup. As the identity is an element of this subgroup, we are led to explore related involutions in the Sprugnoli group.

</details>


### [160] [A quantitative container characterization of one-sided testability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01523)
*Gaia Carenini,Cameron Seth,Yuichi Yoshida*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出了稠密图模型中规模无关的单侧可测试性的定量组合刻画，解决了Alon、Fischer、Newman和Shapira提出的一个问题。作者证明了遗传图性质的单侧可测试性在定量上与超图容器的存在性等价，并结合半遗传性概念对任意图性质给出了定量刻画。该等价关系在双向均有效，可在测试复杂度和容器参数之间显式转换。证明不依赖正则引理，并统一推广到任意有界元数的固定有限关系签名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图性质的可测试性是图性质测试领域的核心问题。Alon等人此前提出了关于规模无关的单侧可测试性的开放问题，本文旨在给出一个定量的组合刻画，以精确理解可测试性的结构条件，并建立可测试性与现代组合学中广泛使用的超图容器工具之间的系统联系。

Method: 作者通过证明遗传图性质的单侧可测试性与超图容器的存在性在定量上等价，建立了这一核心对应关系。对于非遗传的任意图性质，结合Alon-Shapira的半遗传性概念进行刻画。证明方法不依赖正则引理（regularity-free），因此可以统一地推广到任意有界元数的有限关系签名，包括有向图、染色图和超图。

Result: 获得了遗传图性质单侧可测试性的定量组合刻画（与超图容器参数等价）；通过半遗传性推广到任意图性质；对应关系在双向均有效且可提供测试复杂度与容器参数的显式转换；获得了划分性质的定量闭包结果以及由线性大诱导子结构存在性定义的性质的测试器。

Conclusion: 本文建立了可测试性与超图容器之间的定量等价，不仅回答了Alon等人的开放问题，而且提供了一个不依赖正则引理、适用于更广泛关系结构的统一框架，为稠密模型中的性质测试提供了新的结构性理解工具。

Abstract: We give a quantitative combinatorial characterization of size-oblivious one-sided testability in the dense graph model, resolving a question of Alon, Fischer, Newman, and Shapira. For hereditary graph properties, we prove that one-sided testability is quantitatively equivalent to the existence of suitable hypergraph containers, a central and widely used tool in modern combinatorics. Combining this equivalence with the Alon-Shapira notion of semi-hereditariness yields a quantitative characterization of arbitrary graph properties. The correspondence is effective in both directions and provides explicit translations between tester complexity and container parameters. Our proof is regularity-free and extends uniformly to every fixed finite relational signature of bounded arity, including digraphs, coloured graphs, and hypergraphs. As applications, we obtain quantitative closure results for partition properties and testers for properties defined by the existence of a linearly large induced substructure.

</details>


### [161] [Combinatorial theorems relative to sparse sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01525)
*David Conlon*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文综述了现代极值组合学中一个核心主题：在自然定义的稀疏子集背景下研究经典组合定理，并提出了若干尚未解决的重要开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代极值组合学的关键发展方向是将经典组合定理从稠密环境推广到稀疏子集环境中，这一推广带来了新的挑战和理论需求，作者旨在梳理该领域的近期进展并指出亟待解决的研究方向。

Method: 本文是一篇综述性论文，通过系统梳理经典组合定理在稀疏子集上的相对化研究进展，归纳当前已有的成果，并结合文献调研提出一系列开放问题。

Result: 总结描述了该领域近年来的重要研究进展，并列出若干仍然开放且紧迫的组合学问题。

Conclusion: 稀疏子集上的经典组合定理研究是极值组合学的前沿方向，尽管已有显著进展，但仍有多个关键开放问题有待解决。

Abstract: A key theme in modern extremal combinatorics is the study of classical combinatorial theorems relative to sparse subsets of their natural settings. Here we describe some of the recent progress in this area and state a number of problems that remain open and pressing.

</details>


### [162] [Projective-Plane Sharp States and Repulsion in Linear Crown-Free Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01568)
*Mahesh Ramani*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究线性 r-一致超图中不含 crown C^r_{1,k} 的结构的边数上界问题。作者证明了一个关键的"尖边"不等式：每条边 e 满足 Φ_H(e) ≥ r/D，且等号成立当且仅当 e 及其相交边构成 t 个沿公共边 e 粘合的 q 阶射影平面。该等号状态是"排斥的"（repulsive），即与尖边相交的其他边必然包含大量低度顶点。基于这种排斥性并结合非尖边的积分缺陷，作者推导出无条件改进的边数上界：当参数满足特定条件（等价于 t=2 或 q≥t(t-2)+1）时，|E(H)| ≤ D(D-1)|V(H)|/(r(D-1)+1)；对于其余参数，则给出带 β_{r,k} 的较弱上界。对于固定 k，当 r 足够大时第一类界成立，且将系数 D/r 改进了 r^{-2}+O_k(r^{-3})。此外，作者还给出了近尖边的定量局部化定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在对线性 r-一致超图中不含 crown 图案 C^r_{1,k} 的图类建立更精确的边数上界。这是一个极值组合学问题，此前已知的系数 D/r 上界不够精确，本文的目标是通过精细的结构分析（尖边状态及其排斥性）来获得严格改进的边缘计数界，从而深化对这类超图结构的理解。

Method: 本文的方法包括：(1) 建立关键不等式 Φ_H(e):=∑_{v∈e}1/d_H(v) ≥ r/D，并刻画等号成立的精确结构——t 个 q 阶射影平面沿公共边粘合；(2) 证明"排斥性"引理：与尖边相交的非尖边至少有 q-t+2 个顶点的度不超过 D^-；(3) 利用排斥性导出尖边两两不相交、每条边至多与 t-1 条尖边相交等结构性质，以及 |V^-(H)| 的下界；(4) 将尖边的排斥性与非尖边的积分缺陷结合，推导无条件改进的边数上界；(5) 区分参数情形（η_{r,k}>0 与否）给出两套不同的上界公式；(6) 对近尖边情形给出定量局部化定理，证明其邻域分解为 t 个大规模、具有近似仿射平面行-列结构的 packet。

Result: 作者证明了：(i) 尖边（等号成立情形）的精确结构为沿公共边粘合的 t 个 q 阶射影平面；(ii) 尖边排斥性：与尖边相交的边必有大量低度顶点，由此推出尖边两两不相交等结构性质；(iii) 无条件改进的边数上界：在 η_{r,k}>0 时（即 t=2 或 q≥t(t-2)+1），|E(H)| ≤ D(D-1)|V(H)|/(r(D-1)+1)；否则为带有 β_{r,k} 的较弱界；(iv) 对固定 k，当 r 充分大时第一类界成立，改进幅度为 r^{-2}+O_k(r^{-3})；(v) k=3 时含尖边的连通分量恰为一对沿尖边粘合的射影平面；k≥4 时尖核中可存在轻附着，但至多 O_k(r) 个仿射点度超过 D^-；(vi) 近尖边的定量局部化定理。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入"尖边"概念并深刻刻画其等于状态的射影平面结构，以及建立尖边排斥性和积分缺陷的联合分析，在线性 crown-free 超图的边数估计上取得了严格且无条件的最优改进。这一方法兼顾了结构分类与定量计数，为极值超图理论提供了新的技术工具，并在固定 k 的大 r 渐近情形下给出了明确的最优改进阶 r^{-2}+O_k(r^{-3})。

Abstract: Let $3 \le k \le r$, and let $C^r_{1,k}$ be the $r$-uniform $k$-crown. Set $q=r-1$, $t=k-1$, $D=tq+1$, and $D^-=(t-1)q+1$. For every edge $e$ of a linear $C^r_{1,k}$-free $r$-graph, $Φ_H(e):=\sum_{v\in e}1/d_H(v)\ge r/D$. We classify equality: $e$ is sharp if and only if $e$ and all edges meeting it form $t$ projective planes of order $q$ glued along the common line $e$. This equality state is repulsive. If $f\ne e$ meets a sharp edge $e$, then at least $q-t+2$ vertices of $f\setminus e$ have degree at most $D^-$. It follows that sharp edges are pairwise disjoint, every edge meets at most $t-1$ sharp edges, and $|V^-(H)|\ge r(D-1)(q-t+2)|S(H)|/((t-1)D^-)$. Combining sharp-state repulsion with the integral defect of every nonsharp edge gives an unconditional strict improvement over the coefficient $D/r$. Define $B_{r,k}=r(D-1)/(t-1)$, $β_{r,k}=B_{r,k}/(B_{r,k}+1)$, and $η_{r,k}=(q-t+2)/D^- - q/D$. If $η_{r,k}>0$, equivalently if $t=2$, or if $t\ge 3$ and $q\ge t(t-2)+1$, then $|E(H)|\le D(D-1)|V(H)|/(r(D-1)+1)$. For the remaining parameters, $|E(H)|\le D(D-1)|V(H)|/(r(D-1)+β_{r,k})$. For fixed $k$, the first bound holds for all sufficiently large $r$ and improves $D/r$ by $r^{-2}+O_k(r^{-3})$. For $k=3$, every component containing a sharp edge is exactly a pair of projective planes glued along that edge. For $k\ge 4$, light attachments may occur throughout a sharp core, but only $O_k(r)$ affine points can have degree greater than $D^-$. We also prove a quantitative near-sharp localization theorem: after deleting a controlled exceptional family, the neighborhood of a near-sharp edge splits into $t$ large packets with approximate affine-plane row-column incidence.

</details>


### [163] [Optimal and quasi-optimal locating-dominating densities in the infinite hexagonal grid with a finite number of rows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01574)
*Arthur C. Gomes,Yoshiko Wakabayashi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究无限六边形网格中有限行（即宽度为k的六边形条带H_k）的定位支配集的最小密度问题。对于每个k≥2，提出了最优解或与最优解差距在1.3%以内的拟最优解，并给出了精确指数时间算法（对k≤5）和整数线性规划方法（对较大k值），所有解都具有简洁描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定位支配集是图论中的重要概念，其最小密度问题在无限网格图上有本质困难。已有的研究主要针对完全无限网格，但对有限宽度的六边形条带H_k这类实际问题（如传感器网络、无线通信中的节点标识）缺乏系统性结果。作者希望为每一宽度k提供严格的理论下界或接近下界的构造解。

Method: （1）利用H_k恒存在周期最优解的性质，为固定k设计精确的指数时间算法，实现对k≤5的最优解计算；（2）针对更大k值，提出整数线性规划（ILP）以固定周期搜索周期最优解，获得H_7和H_8的高质量可行解；（3）将H_3的最优解与H_7、H_8的解组合拼接，构造出所有k≥6的拟最优解，保证与最优解的偏差不超过1.3%。

Result: 对k≤5给出了精确最优解；对H_7和H_8通过ILP获得了高质量可行解；通过组合策略得到所有k≥6的拟最优解，且所有解的密度与最优解的差距均在1.3%以内。所有解均具有非常简短的结构描述。

Conclusion: 本文系统地解决了六边形条带H_k上定位支配集密度问题，在精确最优（小k）和近似最优（大k）两方面均取得了突破性进展，证明周期解的存在性并利用ILP和组合构造策略，为所有k≥2提供了最优或近最优解。

Abstract: A set of vertices $S$ of a graph $G$ is locating-dominating if $S$ is dominating and, for each pair of distinct vertices not in $S$, their neighborhoods in $S$ are distinct. We present results on the minimum density of such sets in the infinite hexagonal grid with a finite number of rows $k$, also known as the hexagonal strip of width $k$, which we denote by $H_k$. For each $k\geq 2$, we present either an optimal solution or a quasi-optimal solution for $H_k$ that is within $1.3\%$ of the optimum. We describe an exact exponential-time algorithm for fixed k, which we implemented to find optimal solutions for $k \leq 5$. As the infinite grid $H_{k}$ always admits a periodic optimal solution, to deal with larger values of $k$, we present an integer linear program that finds an optimal periodic solution for $H_{k}$ for each fixed period. This program yields high-quality feasible solutions for $H_7$ and $H_8$, which we then combine with an optimal solution for $H_3$ to obtain quasi-optimal solutions for all $k\geq 6$. All these solutions admit a very short description.

</details>


### [164] [An Improved Upper Bound on the Zarankiewicz Number z(43;2)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01606)
*Ankan Sadhu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明Zarankiewicz数z(43;2)的上界从300改进到299，即四环自由二部图（两部分各43个顶点）最大边数不超过299，并给出一个284条边的显式构造，得到284 <= z(43;2) <= 299的收紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zarankiewicz数z(43;2)是极值图论中的经典问题。Reiman界给出z(43;2) <= 301，且等号仅当六阶射影平面的关联图存在，但该平面不存在，故界可降至300。进一步判断z(43;2)是否为300需要更多论证，本文旨在将上界改进到299。

Method: 采用纯组合计数方法，无需计算机搜索。核心思路是：假设存在300条边的四环自由二部图，通过对其leave（缺失边构型）的计数恒等式，证明该图每侧只能取自恰好二十七种度数分布中的一种，其中仅四种两侧组合局部相容。三种组合迫使两个顶点共享六个公共邻居，第四种组合迫使存在TD(6,6)横向设计，即六个互正交的拉丁方（等价于四个互正交的六阶拉丁方），这与Tarry定理矛盾。此外，在PG(2,7)中构造了一个284条边的显式例子。

Result: 证明了z(43;2) <= 299，并显式构造284条边的四环自由二部图，从而确立284 <= z(43;2) <= 299的区间界。

Conclusion: 通过精巧的计数恒等式与度分布分析，将Zarankiewicz数z(43;2)的上界从300收紧至299，下界为284，两端差距缩小至15。该方法完全初等且无需计算机辅助，对四环自由二部图极值问题提供了新的分析思路。

Abstract: The Zarankiewicz number z(43;2) is the largest number of edges in a four-cycle-free bipartite graph with two parts of size 43. Reiman's bound gives z(43;2) <= 301, with equality only for the incidence graph of a projective plane of order six; no such plane exists, so z(43;2) <= 300. We prove z(43;2) <= 299. The argument is elementary and uses no computer search: a counting identity for the leave of the configuration shows that a hypothetical 300-edge graph admits one of exactly twenty-seven degree profiles per side, of which only four combinations are locally compatible. Three force two vertices to share six neighbours; the fourth forces a transversal design TD(6,6), hence four mutually orthogonal Latin squares of order six, contradicting Tarry's theorem. We also give an explicit 284-edge construction inside PG(2,7), so that 284 <= z(43;2) <= 299.

</details>


### [165] [The Godsil--McKay Asymptotic for Latin Rectangles in the Sublinear Range of Erdős Problem 725](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01671)
*Eric Li*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出了Erdős问题725（关于有序、带标签的k×n拉丁矩形的渐近计数L_{k,n}）的部分解答，将Godsil和McKay在k=o(n^{6/7})条件下成立的渐近公式推广到k=o(n)，并提供了形式化的Lean验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős问题725请求给出有序带标签k×n拉丁矩形数量L_{k,n}的渐近公式。Godsil和McKay证明了当k=o(n^{6/7})时的渐近结果，但更大范围（即k=o(n)）的情形尚未解决。该问题的解决对拉丁矩形计数理论具有重要意义。

Method: 论文定义了修正渐近量\widetilde A_{k,n}=(n!)^k((n)_k/n^k)^n\exp\{[n(H_n-H_{n-k})-k]/2\}，并证明对任意K(n)=o(n)，在0≤k≤K(n)范围内一致地有\log(L_{k,n}/\widetilde A_{k,n})=O(k^2/n^2)，其中隐含常数为绝对常数。这一证明将原有范围从k=o(n^{6/7})推广到k=o(n)。

Result: 对所有k=o(n)统一证明了\log(L_{k,n}/\widetilde A_{k,n})=O(k^2/n^2)，且隐含常数是绝对的。这为Erdős问题725提供了部分解答，将已知结果的范围从k=o(n^{6/7})显著扩展到k=o(n)。此外，论文结果已通过定理证明助手Lean进行了形式化验证。

Conclusion: 该工作部分解决了Erdős问题725，将拉丁矩形渐近计数的有效范围扩展到k=o(n)，并通过Lean形式化验证保证了证明的正确性。

Abstract: Erdős Problem 725 asks for an asymptotic formula for the number $L_{k,n}$ of ordered, labelled $k\times n$ Latin rectangles. Godsil and McKay proved that $L_{k,n}\sim (n!)^k((n)_k/n^k)^n(1-k/n)^{-n/2}e^{-k/2}$ for $k=o(n^{6/7})$. We provide a partial solution to Erdős Problem 725 by proving this asymptotic for every $k=o(n)$. More precisely, set $\widetilde A_{k,n}=(n!)^k((n)_k/n^k)^n\exp\{[n(H_n-H_{n-k})-k]/2\}$. For every $K(n)=o(n)$, uniformly for $0\leq k\leq K(n)$, we prove $\log(L_{k,n}/\widetilde A_{k,n})=O(k^2/n^2)$, with an absolute implied constant. The results of this paper have been formally verified in Lean.

</details>


### [166] [A lower bound for the second largest up-Laplacian eigenvalue of a simplicial complex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01694)
*Vinayak Gupta*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Let $K$ be a finite $k$-dimensional simplicial complex with at least two $k$-faces, and let $\Lup_{k-1}(K)=\partial_k\partial_k^{\T}$ be its $(k-1)$-dimensional up-Laplacian. Writing $d_2(K)$ for the second largest upper degree of a $(k-1)$-face, we prove the sharp bound \[
  λ_2\bigl(\Lup_{k-1}(K)\bigr)\ \ge\ d_2(K)+k-1 . \] For $k=1$, this recovers the graph inequality $λ_2(L(G))\ge d_2(G)$ of Li and Pan. We also show that the full graph inequality of Brouwer and Haemers does not extend directly to simplicial complexes: the natural candidate $λ_m(\Lup_{k-1}(K))\ge d_m(K)-m+k+1$ already fails for $m=3$.

</details>


### [167] [A Lovász Theta Parameter and Theta Body for Signed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01716)
*Gabriel Coutinho*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个Lovász型半定参数，用于符号图的平衡着色问题，并建立了与双重切换图、严格向量着色数的联系；同时构建了一个凸角层级（包含符号图版本的稳定集、theta和团不等式松弛），并提出了平衡完美性的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号图作为带正负边的图，其平衡着色问题缺乏系统性的半定规划和凸松弛理论。经典Lovász theta数是图论中的重要半定参数，但在符号图语境下尚无对应物。本文旨在填补这一空缺，建立符号图平衡着色的半定框架，并研究其与经典theta层次、最大诱导二分子图问题等的内在联系。

Method: (1) 定义Lovász型半定参数：以带有正交对合的球面为同态目标，对合作用到某个端点实现符号图的切换操作。该参数具有对称的双半正定矩阵表述及对称的对偶形式，并证明其等于双重切换图中负边构成的普通图的严格向量着色数的一半。(2) 构建凸角层级：从平衡诱导子图多面体出发，在原始顶点空间中定义符号图的稳定集、theta和团不等式松弛。符号theta体具有内在的双矩阵描述，其全一向量上的取值为新标量参数；符号图版本在双角图（digon graph）上精确恢复普通稳定集层次。(3) 分析全负签名特例，将其与最大诱导二分子图问题和广义theta数的松弛建立联系。(4) 提出平衡完美性概念，证明其严格弱于关联双覆盖图的完美性。

Result: 成功建立了符号图平衡着色的半定参数，具有对称的双矩阵原始-对偶形式，并建立了与严格向量着色数的精确关系。构建了从平衡诱导子图多面体出发的完整凸松弛层次，其中符号theta体具有优雅的双矩阵描述。证明了全负情况退化为最大诱导二分子图问题的松弛，并与广义theta数相关。平衡完美性被证明是比双覆盖图完美性更弱的性质。

Conclusion: 这项工作系统性地将Lovász theta理论推广到符号图设定，建立了从半定参数到凸松弛层次再到完美性理论的完整框架，揭示了符号图平衡着色与经典图着色理论之间的深刻联系，为符号图优化提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We introduce a Lovász-type semidefinite parameter for balanced colouring of signed graphs. Its homomorphism target is a unit sphere equipped with an orthogonal involution: the fixed and anti-fixed components play different roles, while applying the involution to one endpoint realizes switching. The resulting parameter admits a symmetric formulation with two positive semidefinite matrices and an equally symmetric dual. It is also one half of the strict vector chromatic number of the ordinary graph formed by the negative edges of the double switching graph. Our second main contribution is a hierarchy of convex corners. Starting from the balanced induced subgraph polytope, we define signed analogues of the stable-set, theta, and clique-inequalities relaxations in the original vertex space. The signed theta body has an intrinsic two-matrix description, its all-ones gauge is the new scalar parameter, and the ordinary stable-set hierarchy is recovered exactly from signed digon graphs. For all-negative signatures, the construction becomes a relaxation of the maximum induced bipartite subgraph problem and is related to the generalized theta number. Finally, we propose a notion of balanced perfectness and show that it is strictly weaker than perfectness of the associated double-cover graph.

</details>


### [168] [A Characterization of Trees with Trinomial Partial Petrial Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01748)
*Qingying Deng,Chaoyang Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了简单图的partial Petrial多项式，证明了该多项式具有插值性质（非零项的指数连续），并完整刻画了二项式情形（连通简单图为路径）和三项式情形（树为T-shape树或H-shape树）的充要条件，同时给出了两类树族的Jacobsthal数显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 花束的partial Petrial多项式可通过其交图的邻接矩阵改变对角线元素后取GF(2)上corank来计算。该矩阵表述提供了一种新的研究途径，作者希望将此方法推广到一般简单图，并完整刻画系数项数很少（二项、三项）的图类。

Method: 作者利用插值性质（非零项指数连续）作为关键工具，结合嫁接(grafts)的局部补图(local complementation)子式方法，将已知的二项式情形从连通圆图推广到所有连通简单图；对于三项式情形，通过对树进行结构分析，刻画了T-shape树和H-shape树两类结构，并借助Jacobsthal数给出显式系数公式。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 连通简单图具有二项式partial Petrial多项式当且仅当它是路径；(2) 树具有三项式partial Petrial多项式当且仅当它是T-shape树（最大度为3且恰有一个3度顶点）或H-shape树（最大度为3且恰有两个相邻的3度顶点）；(3) 对两类树族给出了基于Jacobsthal数的显式公式。

Conclusion: 该研究将partial Petrial多项式的二项式分类从连通圆图推广到所有连通简单图，并首次对三项式情形给出了树的完整刻画，深化了对该多项式非零项结构的理解。

Abstract: The partial Petrial polynomial of a bouquet can be computed from the coranks over GF(2) of matrices obtained by varying the diagonal entries of the adjacency matrix of its intersection graph. Motivated by this matrix formulation, we study the corresponding polynomial for simple graphs and determine when it has exactly two or three nonzero terms. A key tool is the interpolating property: the exponents of the nonzero terms are consecutive. Using this property together with local complementation minors of grafts, we extend the known characterization of the binomial case from connected circle graphs to all connected simple graphs, showing that such a graph has a binomial partial Petrial polynomial if and only if it is a path. Our main result characterizes the trinomial case for trees: a tree has a trinomial partial Petrial polynomial if and only if it is a T-shape tree or an H-shape tree. Here, a T-shape tree has maximum degree 3 and exactly one vertex of degree 3, whereas an H-shape tree has maximum degree 3 and exactly two vertices of degree 3, which are adjacent. We also derive explicit formulas for both families in terms of Jacobsthal numbers.

</details>


### [169] [$0/1$-Polytopes with Exponentially Small Edge Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01870)
*Xiongxin Yang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了一族0/1-多胞形，其边扩张随维度指数递减，从而否证了Mihail-Vazirani猜想（即所有0/1-多胞形的图边扩张至少为1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mihail-Vazirani猜想是组合优化和计算复杂性领域的重要猜想，它声称所有0/1-多胞形的图都具有至少为1的边扩张。该猜想若成立，将蕴含匹配算法的强多项式时间可解性等深远结果。本文旨在检验并否证这一猜想，对理解多面体组合结构有重要理论意义。

Method: 作者通过显式构造一族0/1-多胞形，利用其图结构分析和边扩张的精确计算，证明该族多胞形的边扩张随着维度增加呈指数衰减，从而构造出违反Mihail-Vazirani猜想的具体反例族。

Result: 成功构造了一族0/1-多胞形，其边扩张随维度指数下降，直接否证了Mihail-Vazirani猜想。

Conclusion: Mihail-Vazirani猜想被证明是错的，即并非所有0/1-多胞形的图都具有至少为1的边扩张。这一结果对相关领域的后续研究（如匹配算法复杂性）具有重要影响。

Abstract: We present a construction of a family of $0/1$-polytopes whose edge expansion decreases exponentially with the dimension, which disproves the Mihail-Vazirani conjecture that the graph of every $0/1$-polytope has edge expansion at least one.

</details>


### [170] [Bipartite Turán Numbers of Trees and Star Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01873)
*Omid Khormali*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了二分Turán数ex(m,n;H)问题，针对两类图族：一是树T(r,s)（r≤s），证明了主项精确为(r−1)n，误差为O(m)；二是星森林F=∪S_{d_i}，在n充分大时给出了精确值(k−1)n+(d_k−1)(m−k+1)并刻画了唯一极值图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二分Turán数是极值图论中的基本问题，研究在二分图中避免特定子图时能拥有的最大边数。树和星森林是两类结构简单的图族，但对其二分Turán数的精确确定一直是开放问题。该研究旨在完整刻画这两族图的极值行为，填补现有理论空白。

Method: 对于树T(r,s)，通过构造下界（构造(r−1)n条边的T-free二分图）和利用消去技术或稳定性方法推导上界(r−1)n+O(m)，在n远大于m、r、s的条件下确定主项阶；星情形r=1给出精确公式。对于星森林F，利用匹配结构分解和极端情况分析，在n充分大时推导出精确值和唯一极值图的刻画（基于最小星S_{d_k}的顶点调度策略）。

Result: 对树T(r,s)：当n相对m、r、s足够大时，ex(m,n;T(r,s))的leading-order项精确为(r−1)n，且r=1的星情形下给出精确公式。对星森林F=∪_{i=1}^k S_{d_i}（d_1≥...≥d_k）：当n足够大时精确值ex(m,n;F)=(k−1)n+(d_k−1)(m−k+1)，并唯一刻画了达到该值的极值图。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了树族（在渐近意义下）和星森林族（在精确意义下）的二分Turán数问题，确定了树情形的主阶精确系数，并在星森林情形给出了闭式精确解和极值图唯一性，为二分极值图论中此类问题提供了系统性结果。

Abstract: The bipartite Turán number of a graph $H$, denoted $\text{ex}(m, n; H)$, is the maximum
  number of edges in any $H$-free bipartite graph $G = (A, B; E)$ with parts of size $|A| = m$ and $|B| = n$. We study this problem for two families. For a tree $T = T(r, s)$ with parts $R$ and $S$ of sizes $|R| = r \le s = |S|$, we prove
  \[
  (r - 1) n \;\le\; \text{ex}(m, n; T(r, s)) \;\le\; (r - 1) n + O(m)
  \]
  for $n$ sufficiently large compared to $m$, $r$, and $s$, determining the leading-order term exactly (with the star case $r=1$ solved with an exact formula). For a star forest $F = \bigcup_{i=1}^k S_{d_i}$ with $d_1 \ge \cdots \ge d_k$, we determine the exact value $\text{ex}(m, n; F) = (k - 1) n + (d_k - 1)(m - k + 1)$ for $n$ sufficiently large, and characterize the unique extremal graph.

</details>


### [171] [The maximum index and spectral radius of unbalanced signed multipartite graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01877)
*Yiting Cai,Bo Zhou*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了非平衡符号多部图的索引（最大特征值）和谱半径的极值问题。具体地，对于固定的部数 t≥2 及固定分部大小（或定点数）的非平衡符号 t-部图，在切换同构意义下确定了使索引和谱半径分别达到最大的图；同时，为确定最大谱半径的图，还确定了最小特征值达到最小的图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号图是带有正负边符号的图，在组合学、谱图理论等领域有广泛应用。非平衡符号图的谱极值问题是符号图谱理论中的核心问题之一，研究限定部分大小或顶点数的非平衡符号多部图的谱极值结构具有重要的理论意义。

Method: 利用符号图的切换等价与切换同构概念，通过分析符号多部图的邻接矩阵特征值性质，结合极值图谱理论中的经典方法（如特征向量分析、切换技术、特征值比较等），在固定部数和部分大小（或总顶点数）条件下，刻画使索引、谱半径最大以及最小特征值最小的极值图结构。

Result: 确定了在固定 t≥2 及部分大小（或总顶点数）条件下，非平衡符号 t-部图中使索引和谱半径分别达到最大的图（在切换同构意义下）；同时确定了使最小特征值最小的图。

Conclusion: 该文完整刻画了非平衡符号多部图中谱极值（索引最大、谱半径最大、最小特征值最小）的极值图结构，为符号图谱极值问题提供了新的理论结果。

Abstract: Let $Γ=(G,σ)$ be a signed graph, where $G$ is the underlying graph with vertex set $V(G)$ and edge set $E(G)$ such that $σ: E(G)\to \{-1,1\}$ is the sign function. For $U\subset V(G)$, the operation that changes the sign of all edges between $U$ and $V(G)\setminus U$ is called switching. Two signed graphs with the same underlying graph are switching equivalent if one is obtainable from the other one by switching a subset. Two signed graphs are switching isomorphic if one is isomorphic to a switching equivalent signed graph of the other one. A signed cycle is called negative if it contains an odd number of negative edges. A signed graph is balanced if none of its cycles is negative; otherwise it is unbalanced. The adjacency matrix $A(Γ)$ of $Γ$ is obtained from the standard $(0,1)$-adjacency matrix of $G$ by reversing the sign of all $1$s which correspond to negative edges. The index of $Γ$ is the largest eigenvalue of $A(Γ)$ and the spectral radius of $Γ$ is the largest absolute value of the eigenvalue of $A(Γ)$. The least eigenvalue of $Γ$ is the least eigenvalue of $A(Γ)$. We study the extremal problems of the index and the spectral radius among unbalanced signed multipartite graphs. More precisely, we determine the unbalanced signed $t$-partite graphs with fixed $t\ge 2$ and partite sizes (order, respectively) that maximizes the index and the spectral radius respectively, up to switching isomorphism. To determine the unbalanced signed multipartite graphs with fixed partite sizes (order, respectively) with maximum spectral radius, we also determine those with minimum least eigenvalue.

</details>


### [172] [New upper bound for the Ramsey number of odd cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01921)
*Ting Huang,Jiabao Yang,Yaojun Chen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了k色Ramsey数R_k(C_{2ℓ+1})的上界问题，给出了当ℓ固定且k充分大时的新上界：R_k(C_{2ℓ+1}) ≤ (2ℓ/(2ℓ-1))(2ℓ-1)^k(k!)^{1/ℓ}·exp(k^{1-1/ℓ}+O_ℓ(k^{1-2/ℓ}+log k))+1。该结果分别将Miyazaki等人的界改进了因子2^{k-o(k)}，将Axenovich等人的界改进了因子(2e^{1/ℓ})^{k-o(k)}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k色Ramsey数R_k(C_{2ℓ+1})是极值图论和Ramsey理论中的经典问题。尽管Jenssen和Skokan已确定了固定k且ℓ充分大时的精确值，但固定ℓ且k充分大时的情形仍是一个开放问题。此前Axenovich等人和Miyazaki等人给出的上界分别包含因子k^{k/ℓ}和(k!)^{1/ℓ}，本文希望在固定ℓ、k充分大的设置下进一步改进这些上界。

Method: 本文通过改进的Ramsey构造或计数方法，将上界中的关键因子进一步细化。核心思路是将改进因子从k^{k/ℓ}或(k!)^{1/ℓ}所蕴含的指数项中提取出来，利用更精细的组合论证，将主要项的底数从2降低到(2ℓ-1)^{ℓ/(2ℓ-1))}相关的改进形式，并辅以指数项k^{1-1/ℓ}及更小的余项来刻画渐进行为。

Result: 对每个固定ℓ≥2和充分大的k，证明了R_k(C_{2ℓ+1}) ≤ (2ℓ/(2ℓ-1))(2ℓ-1)^k(k!)^{1/ℓ}·exp(k^{1-1/ℓ}+O_ℓ(k^{1-2/ℓ}+log k))+1。该结果比Miyazaki等人的界改进了一个因子2^{k-o(k)}，比Axenovich等人的界改进了一个因子(2e^{1/ℓ})^{k-o(k)}。

Conclusion: 本文在固定ℓ、k充分大的情形下显著改进了k色奇圈Ramsey数的上界，为这一开放问题提供了新的最佳上界。这些改进体现了在k很大而ℓ固定时，对奇圈Ramsey上界指数结构的更精细理解。

Abstract: The \emph{$k$-color Ramsey number} $R_k(C_{2\ell+1})$ is the least integer $n$ such that any $k$-edge-coloring of a complete graph $K_n$ has a monochromatic odd cycle $C_{2\ell+1}$. Axenovich, Cames van Batenburg, Janzer, Michel, and Rundström~(JCT-B, 2026) recently proved \[ R_k(C_{2\ell+1})\le (4\ell-2)^k k^{k/\ell}+1, \] and Miyazaki, Mulrenin, Pohoata, and Zheng further improved the factor $k^{k/\ell}$ to $(k!)^{1/\ell}$. As Jenssen and Skokan (AM, 2021) determined $R_k(C_{2\ell+1})$ for fixed $k$ and sufficiently large $\ell$, it becomes even more interesting to seek better bound for fixed $\ell$ and sufficiently large $k$. In this paper, we show \[ R_k(C_{2\ell+1}) \le \frac{2\ell}{2\ell-1}(2\ell-1)^k(k!)^{1/\ell} \exp\!\left(k^{1-1/\ell}+O_\ell\!\left(k^{1-2/\ell}+\log k\right)\right)+1 \] for every fixed $\ell\ge 2$ and sufficiently large $k$, which improves the bound of Miyazaki et al. by a factor $2^{k-o(k)}$, and the bound of Axenovich et al. by a factor $(2\e^{1/\ell})^{k-o(k)}$.

</details>


### [173] [Markov and lattice bases for Forman-Ricci curvature of graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01929)
*Jane Ivy Coons,Giulio Zucal*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文进一步发展了离散Forman-Ricci曲率约束图空间中Markov基的代数与组合理论。作者证明不可或缺Markov移动的次数在图的顶点最大度上至少以二次方式增长，因此寻求所有Markov基元素的紧凑描述不可行。作为替代，作者仅使用三次Markov移动构造了该问题的格基，从而使最近开发的强化学习方法能够应用于寻找特定图上的Markov移动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Roost等人(2024)提出使用Markov基从具有指定顶点度和曲率的图空间中进行采样，但其Markov基的代数与组合结构尚未被充分理解，需要进一步发展相关理论。

Method: 作者证明了不可或缺Markov移动次数至少随图的最大顶点度二次增长，从而放弃寻找所有Markov基元素的紧凑描述；转而仅使用三次Markov移动构建格基，并利用强化学习方法在给定图上寻找可应用的Markov移动。

Result: 1) 证明了不可或缺Markov移动的次数至少以图最大度数的二次方速度增长；2) 提出了仅用三次Markov移动构成的格基，为强化学习方法的应用提供了可行路径。

Conclusion: 由于完整Markov基的紧凑描述不可行，本文提出的基于三次Markov移动的格基结合强化学习方法，为在具有指定顶点度和曲率的图空间中进行有效采样提供了新的实用方案。

Abstract: Discrete Forman-Ricci curvature is a quantity associated to each edge of a graph that describes its local geometry. It has proven to be a useful tool in network analysis in a variety of applications. Recent work by Roost et al.\ (2024) proposed the use of Markov bases to sample from the space of graphs with prescribed vertex degrees and curvatures. In the present work, we further develop the algebraic and combinatorial theory of these Markov bases. We show that the degree of an indispensable Markov move grows at least quadratically in the maximum degree of the graph. In light of this result, a compact description of all Markov basis elements seems unattainable at present. Instead, we find a lattice basis for this problem using only degree three Markov moves, which allows us to employ recently-developed reinforcement learning methods for finding Markov moves that can be applied to a specific graph.

</details>


### [174] [New upper bound for multicolor Ramsey numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01962)
*Gang Yang,Yaping Mao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了对角r色图Ramsey数R_r(k)的上界：存在绝对常数c,K>0，使得当r≥2且k≥Kr²log⁶(2r)时，R_r(k)≤exp(-c·k/(r²log⁴(2r)))·r^{rk}。证明方法结合了变阶正系数根筛选器与保留脊柱的多色书方法改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对角r色图Ramsey数R_r(k)是组合数学中的核心问题，其精确阶估计长期悬而未决。经典的上界r^{rk}过于粗糙，需要寻求指数级别的改进，特别是在多色情形下。本文旨在通过改进上界估计，缩小与下界之间的差距。

Method: 证明主要包含两个创新工具：(1) 变阶正系数根筛选器（positive-coefficient root filter of variable order），用于更精细地控制递推过程中的误差；(2) 保留脊柱改进的多色书方法（retained-spine refinement of the multicolor book method），在书方法的基础上保留关键结构信息，从而在多色情形下获得更强的上界。

Result: 建立了R_r(k) ≤ exp(-c·k/(r²log⁴(2r)))·r^{rk}的上界，这是对朴素上界r^{rk}的指数级改进。改进的幅度由因子exp(-c·k/(r²log⁴(2r)))刻画，且要求k足够大（k≥Kr²log⁶(2r)）。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了多色Ramsey数的改进上界，在k的相对规模条件下相对朴素界取得了非平凡指数改进，是组合领域的重要进展。

Abstract: Let $R_r(k)$ denote the diagonal $r$-color graph Ramsey number. We prove that there exist absolute constants $c,K>0$ such that \[
  R_r(k)\le
  \exp\!\left(-c\frac{k}{r^2\log^4(2r)}\right)r^{rk} \] for every $r\ge2$ and every $k\ge Kr^2\log^6(2r)$. The proof combines a positive-coefficient root filter of variable order with a retained-spine refinement of the multicolor book method.b

</details>


### [175] [Idleness Functions for Ollivier-Ricci Curvature on Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01970)
*Qing Xia*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了局部有限简单超图上的惰性随机游走，证明了与超图Ollivier-Ricci曲率相关的闲置函数α↦κα是分段仿射的，至多包含三个仿射片段；通过质量平衡论证证明了在[1/2,1]区间上的线性性，从而得到Lin-Lu-Yau曲率的免极限表达式；在r-均匀线性超图中，该游走与2-截面上的简单随机游走完全一致，并将Bourne等人的尖锐端点区间结果推广到超图情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图上的Ollivier-Ricci曲率是几何分析与图论交叉领域的重要工具，但超图上随机游走及曲率的显式计算十分复杂。作者希望简化超图曲率的计算与分析：一方面探究闲置函数（idleness function）的结构性质，另一方面建立超图游走与普通图游走之间的联系，从而借用已有的图论结果。

Method: 采用两类技术手段：(1) 结构分析——证明闲置函数α↦κα(x,y)是分段仿射的，且仿射片段不超过三个；(2) 质量平衡（mass-balance）论证——在[1/2,1]区间上证明线性性，由此消除Lin-Lu-Yau曲率定义中的极限过程。对于r-均匀线性超图，通过证明超图游走与2-截面上简单随机游走的逐点一致性来实现归约与定理迁移。

Result: 得到三个主要结果：闲置函数至多三段分段仿射；在[1/2,1]上线性，给出Lin-Lu-Yau曲率的免极限公式；在r-均匀线性超图情形，超图游走等同于2-截面的简单随机游走，从而将Bourne、Cushing、Liu、Münch和Peyerimhoff的尖锐端点区间结果推广至超图。

Conclusion: 本文为超图Ollivier-Ricci曲率和Lin-Lu-Yau曲率的计算提供了显著简化，并通过2-截面约化将图论中已知的曲率区间刻画推广到r-均匀线性超图。这些结果加深了对超图几何结构的理解，并为后续研究超图上的谱几何与曲率流等问题奠定了基础。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal H=(V,E)$ be a locally finite simple hypergraph, equip $V$ with the hyperpath metric, and consider the lazy random walk introduced for hypergraph Ollivier--Ricci curvature by Tian and Zhao \cite{TianZhao2025}. For adjacent vertices $x$ and $y$, we prove that the idleness function $α\mapstoκ_α^{\mathcal H}(x,y)$ is piecewise affine and with no more than three affine pieces. A separate mass-balance argument gives linearity on $[1/2,1]$ for every locally finite simple hypergraph and, consequently, a limit-free expression for the Lin--Lu--Yau curvature. In the $r$-uniform linear case, the hypergraph walk agrees exactly with the simple random walk on its 2-section. This reduction transfers the sharp endpoint intervals of Bourne, Cushing, Liu, Münch, and Peyerimhoff \cite{BourneEtAl2018}.

</details>


### [176] [Counterexamples to the Minimum Period Conjecture for Restricted Partition Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02085)
*Feihu Liu,Jinlong Tang,Guoce Xin,Chen Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文针对Beck, Sam和Woods于2008年提出的关于受限制分拆函数拟多项式的周期性猜想，导出了每个系数函数c_j(k)的精确单位根公式，证明猜想中的整除上界成立，但通过揭示周期下界构造了一族反例，表明原猜想不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限制分拆函数q_a(k)的拟多项式系数c_j(k)是周期函数，Beck-Sam-Woods猜想其最小时期等于lcm{m:b_m>j}。验证或反驳该猜想需要精确分析系数函数的周期性结构。

Method: 推导c_j(k)的精确单位根公式（root-of-unity formula），利用该公式分别获得c_j(k)周期的整除性上界和下界，并证明这两个界均为最优的（sharp）。

Result: 单位根公式证明了猜想中的上界（c_j(k)的周期整除lcm{m:b_m>j}）成立，但同时揭示了一个非平凡的下界。将上下界结合，构造出一族满足下界比上界严格更小（或更复杂）的反例，从而反驳了Beck-Sam-Woods猜想。

Conclusion: Beck-Sam-Woods猜想中关于周期上界的整除性部分正确，但猜想的最小时期等式不成立；本文通过精确公式给出了周期性完整刻画，并构造了系统性反例族。

Abstract: For a finite sequence of positive integers $\boldsymbol{a}=(a_1,\dots,a_n)$, the restricted partition function $q_{\boldsymbol{a}}(k)$ denote the number of nonnegative integer solutions to the equation $a_1x_1+a_2x_2+\cdots +a_nx_n=k$. It is proved to be a quasi-polynomial of degree $n-1$. Write $q_{\boldsymbol{a}}(k)=\sum_{j=0}^{n-1}c_j(k)k^j$ with periodic coefficient functions $c_j$, and set $b_m=\#\{i:m\mid a_i\}$. In 2008, Beck, Sam, and Woods conjectured that the minimum period of $c_j(k)$ is $\mathrm{lcm}\{m:b_m>j\}$. In this paper, we derive an exact root-of-unity formula for every coefficient function $c_j(k)$. The formula proves the conjectured divisibility upper bound, but it also reveals a lower bound for the period of $c_j(k)$. Both divisibility bounds are sharp. This leads us to construct a family of counterexamples to this conjecture.

</details>


### [177] [The P-vertex problem for graphs with perfect matchings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02107)
*G. Arunkumar,U. S. Jerisha*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将Sharma和Panda关于二部图具有性质(P)的结果推广到任意图，通过引入P-顶点覆盖和P-顶点覆盖数p(G)的概念，利用隐函数定理构造非奇异实对称矩阵，使得图中每个顶点都成为至少一个矩阵的P-顶点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sharma和Panda证明了每个具有完美匹配的二部图都具有性质(P)，即存在一个支撑图为G的非奇异实对称矩阵，使得每个顶点都是P-顶点。本文的目标是将这一结果从二部图推广到任意图，这是一个自然的理论推广需求。

Method: 引入P-顶点覆盖的概念，定义P-顶点覆盖数p(G)为S(G)中非奇异矩阵的最少数量，使得G的每个顶点至少是其中一个矩阵的P-顶点。给定G的一个极大匹配，将顶点集划分为匹配饱和顶点和剩余顶点（后者构成独立集），分别构造覆盖这两类顶点的矩阵。使用隐函数定理作为扰动工具来建立所需结果。

Result: 成功将Sharma和Panda的结果从二部图推广到任意图，证明了任意图都存在一个P-顶点覆盖，即每个顶点的P-顶点性质可以通过有限个非奇异实对称矩阵实现覆盖。

Conclusion: 通过引入P-顶点覆盖和P-顶点覆盖数的概念，本文建立了一套适用于任意图的框架，扩展了此前仅适用于二部图的结果，为图论与矩阵论的交叉研究提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: Sharma and Panda recently proved that every bipartite graph with a perfect matching has property (P); that is, it admits a non-singular real symmetric matrix with support graph G for which every vertex is a P -vertex. In this paper, we extend their result from bipartite graphs to arbitrary graphs. To this end, we introduce the notion of a P - vertex covering and define the P -vertex covering number p(G) as the minimum number of non-singular matrices in S(G) needed so that every vertex of G is a P -vertex of at least one of them. Given a maximal matching of G, we partition the vertex set into the vertices saturated by the matching and the remaining vertices, which necessarily form an independent set. We then construct separate matrices covering these two classes of vertices. We use the Implicit Function Theorem as a perturbation tool to establish the desired result.

</details>


### [178] [Counting cliques in graphs with small independence number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02279)
*Logan Post*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于所有固定的k≥4，任何没有大小为n的独立集且顶点数N≥Ω(n^{k-1}/log^{k-2}n)的N顶点图中，至少包含Ω((N choose k)(log n/n)^{choose(k,2)}/log n)个k阶团。该下界对k≥5在条件于已知的r(k,n)上界下是最优的，对k=4也已证明是紧的。这与Turán定理对k=2及Bohman和Mubayi结果对k=3的情况相呼应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超饱和（supersaturation）问题，即在给定图禁止较大独立集（即限制Ramsey数相关条件）时，图中必须出现的最小团数。旨在建立k-clique-counts的下界并证明其紧性。

Method: 利用Ramsey数r(k,n)的已知上界与相关组合技术（可能涉及随机图构造、归约到Ramsey理论方法、以及针对k=4时采用特殊构造来证明紧性）来推导k阶团数量的下界并验证其最优性。

Result: 对k≥4建立了团计数下界Ω((N choose k)(log n/n)^{choose(k,2)}/log n)；该界对k≥5（依赖于r(k,n)上界）和k=4均为紧；同时用相同方法获得其他超饱和结果。

Conclusion: 文中结果将Turán定理（k=2）和Bohman-Mubayi定理（k=3）推广到k≥4的情况，给出了在无大独立集条件下k阶团数的最优下界，完成了该系列结果的统一。

Abstract: We prove that for all fixed $k\geq 4$, any $N$ vertex graph with no independent set of size $n$ and $N\geq Ω(n^{k-1}/\log^{k-2}n)$ contains at least $$ Ω\bigg(\binom Nk \Big(\frac{\log n}{n}\Big)^{\binom k2}/\log n\bigg) $$ cliques of order $k$, and for $k\geq 5$ this is best possible conditional on the known upper bounds for $r(k,n)$. This is also true and tight for $k=2$ by Turán's Theorem and for $k=3$ by a result of Bohman and Mubayi. We show the bound is also tight for $k=4$. We obtain other supersaturation results using the same methods.

</details>


### [179] [Spectral Properties of Power Graphs of Metacyclic Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02281)
*Aditya Singh,Yogendra Singh,Anand Kumar Tiwari*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了亚循环群的幂图结构，并基于此推导出邻接矩阵、Laplacian矩阵和signless Laplacian矩阵的特征多项式的显式表达式，同时给出了邻接矩阵和signless Laplacian矩阵谱半径的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幂图作为群论与图论交叉领域的核心研究对象，其谱性质对理解群的结构具有重要意义。亚循环群是一类重要的有限群，但目前其幂图结构尚未被完全刻画，这限制了对其谱性质的研究。

Method: 首先对亚循环群的幂图结构进行完全刻画；然后基于该结构描述，推导邻接矩阵、Laplacian矩阵和signless Laplacian矩阵的特征多项式；最后利用谱理论分析得到相关矩阵谱半径的上下界。

Result: 完全表征了亚循环群幂图的结构；获得了三种关键矩阵（邻接、Laplacian、signless Laplacian）特征多项式的显式表达式；并给出了邻接矩阵和signless Laplacian矩阵谱半径的上下界。

Conclusion: 该研究为亚循环群幂图的代数性质提供了完整的理论刻画，其谱结果可作为进一步研究更广泛群类幂图性质的参考依据。

Abstract: For a group $Ω$, the associated power graph $P(Ω)$ is defined as the graph whose vertices are the elements of $Ω$, with two distinct vertices $u,v\in Ω$ being adjacent if either $u=v^m$ or $v=u^n$ for some $m,n \in \mathbb{N}$. In this paper, we completely characterise the structure of the power graph associated with the class of metacyclic groups. Building on this structural description, we derive explicit expressions for the characteristic polynomials of the adjacency, Laplacian, and signless Laplacian matrices. Moreover, we obtain lower and upper bounds for the spectral radii of the adjacency and signless Laplacian matrices.

</details>


### [180] [Ramsey multiplicity for ordered graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02299)
*Mengya He,Yaping Mao,Bing Wei,Qinghong Zhao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究有序拉姆齐多重度（ordered Ramsey multiplicity）问题。作者建立了放大不等式（amplification inequality），将小规模n上的有序拉姆齐多重度下界推广到任意更大规模n上。利用该不等式推导了有序星图（ordered stars）和有序完美匹配（ordered perfect matchings）的拉姆齐多重度下界，并通过构造给出了有序星图的上界，同时给出了有序完美匹配的随机着色上界。最后建立了基于正则性的提升定理（lifting theorem）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有序拉姆齐理论研究顶点有序图中的单色子图问题。虽然有序拉姆齐数已被广泛研究，但有序拉姆齐多重度（即在所有k边着色中，正确着色、保序的目标图的最小数量）的定量研究相对较少。本文旨在建立有序拉姆齐多重度的系统性框架，特别是放大不等式，使得小规模结果可以推广到任意规模，从而为有序星图和完美匹配提供紧的多重度上下界。

Method: (1) 建立放大不等式：oM_λ(n;G1,...,Gk) ≥ oM_λ(t;G1,...,Gk)·C(n,h_min)/C(t,h_min)，其中n≥t≥oR(G1,...,Gk)，h_min为最小目标图顶点数；(2) 将该不等式应用于有序星图S_{r,s}（中心左侧r-1叶、右侧s-1叶）和包含边{1,2m}的有序完美匹配族B_m，得到多重度下界；(3) 通过显式构造给出有序星图的界：oM_λ(n;S_{r1,s1},S_{r2,s2}) ≤ min{λ1B_{h1}(n), λ2B_{h2}(n)}，其中B_{hi}(n)=C(⌊n/2⌋,hi)+C(⌈n/2⌉,hi)；(4) 利用随机着色给出有序星图的上界；证明oM(n;B_m,B_m) ≤ C(n,2m)·(2m-2)!/(2^{2m-2}(m-1)!)；(5) 建立基于正则性引理的有序着色提升定理。

Result: 文章获得如下主要结果：(1) 建立了普适的放大不等式，将有序拉姆齐多重度从小规模推广到任意规模；(2) 为有序星图和有序完美匹配导出多重度下界；(3) 通过构造证明有序星图的双色多重度上界为min{λ1B_{h1}(n),λ2B_{h2}(n)}；(4) 通过随机着色导出有序完美匹配的上界；(5) 建立了正则性引理框架下的有序着色提升定理。

Conclusion: 本文建立起有序拉姆齐多重度的系统研究方法，核心工具是放大不等式，它将小规模情形推广到任意大规模，使得有序星图和有序完美匹配的多重度获得了紧的上下界估计。正则性提升定理则为后续进一步研究提供了普适的工具。这些结果推进了对有序图中强制单色（或指定颜色）结构数量的理解。

Abstract: Let \(\cG_1,\ldots,\cG_k\) be fixed vertex-ordered graphs, each containing at least one edge. The ordered Ramsey number \(\oR(\cG_1,\ldots,\cG_k)\) is the least integer \(N\) such that every \(k\)-edge-coloring of the ordered complete graph \(\cK_N\) contains an order-preserving copy of \(\cG_i\) in color \(i\) for some \(i\in[k]\). For positive weights \(\blambda=(λ_1,\ldots,λ_k)\), let \(\oM_{\blambda}(n;\cG_1,\ldots,\cG_k)\) denote the minimum weighted number of correctly colored, order-preserving copies of the target graphs over all \(k\)-edge-colorings of \(\cK_n\). When \(\blambda=\bf{1}\), \(\oM_{\bf{1}}(n;\cG_1,\ldots,\cG_k)=\oM(n;\cG_1,\ldots,\cG_k)\) is called the ordered Ramsey multiplicity. In this paper, we first establish the amplification inequality \[ \oM_{\blambda}(n;\cG_1,\ldots,\cG_k) \ge \oM_{\blambda}(t;\cG_1,\ldots,\cG_k) \frac{\binom{n}{\hmin}}{\binom{t}{\hmin}}, \] where $h_i=v(\cG_i),\hmin=\min_{i\in[k]}h_i$, and $n\ge t\ge\oR(\cG_1,\ldots,\cG_k)$. Let $\cS_{r,s}$ be the ordered star whose center has $r-1$ leaves to its left and $s-1$ leaves to its right, and let $\bB_m$ be the family of all ordered perfect matchings on $[2m]$ containing the edge $\{1,2m\}$. We apply the amplification inequality to obtain the multiplicity lower bounds for ordered stars and ordered perfect matchings. We then obtain the upper bound $\oM_{\boldsymbolλ} (n;\cS_{r_1,s_1},\cS_{r_2,s_2}) \le \min\{λ_1 B_{h_1}(n),λ_2 B_{h_2}(n)\}$ by constructions, where $B_{h_i}(n):= \binom{\lfloor n/2\rfloor}{h_i} + \binom{\lceil n/2\rceil}{h_i}$ and $h_i=r_i+s_i-1$ for $i\in [2]$. We also derive a random-coloring upper bound for ordered stars and prove \[\oM(n; \bB_m,\bB_m) \le \binom{n}{2m} \frac{(2m-2)!}{2^{2m-2}(m-1)!}.\] Finally, we establish a regularity-based lifting theorem for ordered colorings.

</details>


### [181] [Real-rootedness of Kazhdan--Lusztig and $Z$-polynomials of thagomizer matroids and graphic matroids of $K_{2,n}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02303)
*Philip B. Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究图拟阵的Kazhdan–Lusztig多项式与Z-多项式的实根性质。对于图T_n=K_{1,1,n}和K_{2,n}，证明了当n≥2且0≤λ≤n/2时，P_n(x)+λx恰有⌊n/2⌋个零根，全部为负且简单；特别地，这两个图拟阵的Kazhdan–Lusztig多项式均为实根多项式。同时证明了Z_{T_n}(x)=Z_{K_{2,n}}(x)有n+1个不同的负零根。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kazhdan–Lusztig多项式的实根性是一个既有理论意义又具挑战性的问题。图拟阵的Kazhdan–Lusztig多项式是否具有实根性质，以及Z-多项式的根分布规律，是该领域的重要开放问题。作者希望通过对特定图族T_n和K_{2,n}的深入研究，揭示这类多项式的根结构，为更一般的情形提供线索和方法。

Method: 作者引入一个共同的理性变换（rational transformation），将Kazhdan–Lusztig情况约化为在Chebyshev多项式零点处的交错符号求值问题；将Z-多项式的情况约化为自反多项式（self-inversive polynomials）的单位圆判定准则。这种方法将原本复杂的多项式根分布问题转化为更易验证的符号判定和复分析判据。

Result: （1）当n≥2且0≤λ≤n/2时，P_n(x)+λx恰有⌊n/2⌋个零根，全部为负且简单；（2）T_n和K_{2,n}的图拟阵Kazhdan–Lusztig多项式均为实根多项式；（3）当n≥2时，Z_{T_n}(x)=Z_{K_{2,n}}(x)恰有n+1个不同的负零根。

Conclusion: 通过统一的理性变换方法，成功解决了特定图族拟阵的Kazhdan–Lusztig多项式实根性和Z-多项式零点分布问题，证明了更强的结果：不仅多项式本身实根，且其扰动形式P_n(x)+λx在参数λ的某个范围内仍保持所有根的负实性和简单性。这为该方向的一般性猜想提供了有力支持和方法论基础。

Abstract: Let $T_n=K_{1,1,n}$, and let $P_n(x)$ denote the Kazhdan--Lusztig polynomial of its graphic matroid. We prove that, whenever $n\ge2$ and $0\leλ\le n/2$, the polynomial $P_n(x)+λx$ has exactly $\lfloor n/2\rfloor$ zeros, all of which are negative and simple. In particular, the Kazhdan--Lusztig polynomials of the graphic matroids of $T_n$ and $K_{2,n}$ are real-rooted. We also prove that, for $n\ge2$, the common polynomial $Z_{T_n}(x)=Z_{K_{2,n}}(x)$ has $n+1$ distinct negative zeros. The proofs use a common rational transformation, reducing the Kazhdan--Lusztig case to alternating sign evaluations at the zeros of a Chebyshev polynomial and the $Z$-polynomial case to a unit-circle criterion for self-inversive polynomials.

</details>


### [182] [The DP Color Function of Bipartite Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02335)
*Hemanshu Kaul,Jeffrey A. Mudrock,Gunjan Sharma,Anne Ullyot*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了所有二分图G的DP色函数在q足够大时等于一个关于Tutte多项式的多项式，从而肯定地回答了DP色函数的基本开放问题（是否存在某个N和多项式p使得q≥N时P_DP(G,q)=p(q)）对于所有二分图均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DP着色（对应着色）是列表着色的一般化推广，自2015年被提出以来被广泛研究。DP色函数P_DP(G,q)是色多项式P(G,q)的对应版本，已知存在图使得P_DP(G,q)<P(G,q)，且所有含圈二分图都具有此性质。一个基本开放问题询问：是否对每个图G都存在N∈N和多项式p，使得q≥N时P_DP(G,q)=p(q)（即当q足够大时DP色函数由某个多项式给出）。本文旨在对二分图类肯定地回答该问题。

Method: 作者证明若G是具有c个连通分量的n顶点二分图，则对于所有充分大的q，P_DP(G,q) = (-1)^{n-c} q^c T_G(1-q,1)，其中T_G(x,y)是G的Tutte多项式。所发展的方法还导出了当G的围长（girth）为偶数时，P(G,q)与P_DP(G,q)之差的一个渐近公式。

Result: 对所有二分图G，DP色函数在q足够大时等于一个明确的多项式表达式（由Tutte多项式给出），从而肯定地回答了基本开放问题中二分图的情形。此外，对于围长为偶数的图，得到了P(G,q)-P_DP(G,q)的渐近公式。

Conclusion: 该研究将DP色函数的结构性质与图的Tutte多项式建立了具体联系，为理解DP色函数在渐近意义上的行为提供了二分图类上的完整答案。

Abstract: DP-coloring (or correspondence coloring) is a generalization of list coloring that has been widely studied since its introduction by Dvořák and Postle in 2015. As the analogue of $P(G,q)$, the chromatic polynomial of a graph $G$, the DP color function of $G$, denoted by $P_{DP}(G,q)$, counts the minimum number of DP-colorings over all $q$-fold covers of $G$. It follows that $P_{DP}(G,q) \leq P(G,q)$. It is known that there are graphs for which $P_{DP}(G,q) < P(G,q)$ for all sufficiently large $q$; in fact, all bipartite graphs containing a cycle have this property. A fundamental open question about DP color functions asks whether, for every graph $G$, there exist $N \in \mathbb{N}$ and a polynomial $p$ such that $P_{DP}(G,q) = p(q)$ whenever $q \geq N$. In this paper we answer this question affirmatively for all bipartite graphs. Specifically, if $G$ is an $n$-vertex bipartite graph with $c$ components, then $P_{DP}(G,q) = (-1)^{n-c}q^c \;T_G(1-q,1)$ for all sufficiently large $q$, where $T_G(x,y)$ is the Tutte polynomial of $G$. The ideas we develop also yield an asymptotic formula for $P(G,q)-P_{DP}(G,q)$ whenever the girth of $G$ is even.

</details>


### [183] [Bounds on Odd and Odd-Even Induced Subgraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02339)
*Qiwen Guo,Gregory Gutin,Yiming Hao,Yongtang Shi,Yong Zhang,Yacong Zhou*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究图中与度奇偶性约束相关的可容许顶点集合的最大规模问题。作者证明对无孤立顶点的n顶点图，任意奇偶性处方l下的最大可容许集合规模至少为n/6；对二分图，得到f_o(G)≥(65/256)n；并通过四阶矩论证给出关于α(G)的下界，同时构造二分图说明对数项改进量的数量级最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于图论中关于奇偶性（odd/even）可容许集合的研究，Scott关于f_o(G)≥n/(2χ(G))的界在二分图情形下有一个因子2，本文试图改进该因子；同时在f_o(G)≥α(G)/2（Scott界）的基础上，探索能否加入对数阶的加法改进，以及该改进的数量级是否最优。

Method: 本文的主要技术手段包括：(1) 将Zeng的奇割（odd-cut）方法推广到任意奇偶性处方l，并引入一个单侧完备化引理（one-sided completion lemma），得到h_l(G)≥n/6；(2) 对二分图，利用二分邻接矩阵在F2上的秩来推导f_o(G)的下界，并将不同秩情形结合得到(65/256)n；(3) 利用四阶矩（fourth-moment）论证，从α(G)出发推导含对数项的加法下界；(4) 通过构造具体的二分图实例，证明上界中log_2(α+1)的对数项是最优数量级。

Result: 主要结果有三个：(1) 对任意奇偶性处方l，无孤立顶点的n顶点图满足h_l(G)≥n/6，从而f_oe(G)≥n/6，改进了此前2n/21的界；(2) 对二分图，f_o(G)≥(65/256)n，将Scott界在二分情形的因子2改进为128/65<2；(3) 对α(G)≥2，给出f_o(G)≥α/2+((log_3 α)/8)-(1/4)log_3 log_3 √α的下界，并构造二分图证明f_o(G)≤α/2+log_2(α+1)+1/2，表明对数加法改进的数量级是最优的。

Conclusion: 本文系统地改进了关于奇偶性可容许集合的多个下界：将Zeng的奇割方法从奇偶处方推广到一般处方，将二分图的f_o(G)界从n/4提升到(65/256)n≈0.254n，并证明相对Scott界f_o(G)≥α(G)/2的对数加法改进在数量级上不可再优化。这些结果加深了对图上度奇偶性与最大可容许子图之间关系的理解。

Abstract: Let $G$ be an $n$-vertex graph and let $\ell:V(G)\to\mathbb{F}_2$ prescribe degree parities. A set $S\subseteq V(G)$ is $\ell$-admissible if every $v\in S$ has degree congruent to $\ell(v)$ modulo $2$ in $G[S]$. Let $h_\ell(G)$ be the maximum order of an $\ell$-admissible set, set $f_{\mathrm{oe}}(G):=\min_\ell h_\ell(G)$, and write $f_o(G):=h_{\mathbf{1}}(G)$, where $\mathbf{1}(v)=1$ for every $v\in V(G).$ We prove three main results for graphs without isolated vertices. First, by extending Zeng's odd-cut method to arbitrary parity prescriptions an introducing a one-sided completion lemma, we show that $h_\ell(G)\ge n/6$ for every $\ell$. Consequently, $f_{\mathrm{oe}}(G)\ge n/6$, improving the previous bound $2n/21$.
  Second, for bipartite graphs we derive lower bounds on $f_o(G)$ in terms of the $\mathbb{F}_2$-rank of the bipartite adjacency matrix and combine them to obtain
  \[
  f_o(G)\ge \left(\frac14+\frac1{256}\right)n=\frac{65}{256}n.
  \] Thus, in the bipartite case, the factor $2$ in Scott's bound $f_o(G)\ge n/(2χ(G))$ can be replaced by $128/65<2$.
  Finally, writing $α=α(G)$, a fourth-moment argument gives, for $α\ge2$,
  \[
  f_o(G)\ge \fracα{2}+\frac{\log_3α}{8}
  -\frac14\log_3\log_3\sqrtα.
  \] We also construct bipartite graphs satisfying
  \[
  f_o(G)\le \frac{α(G)}2+\log_2\!\bigl(α(G)+1\bigr)+\frac12,
  \] showing that the logarithmic additive improvement over Scott's bound $f_o(G)\geα(G)/2$ has the optimal order of magnitude.

</details>


### [184] [On Hamilton cycles in connected vertex-transitive graphs of order $2pq$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02349)
*Zhou Tianlei*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究阶为2pq（p、q为素数）的连通顶点传递图的哈密顿圈存在性问题。在满足每个传递自同构子群都有一个诱导素数长度轨道的极大非传递正规子群的条件下，证明了所有此类图都包含哈密顿圈，且除了已有特征描述的不合格图外无新例外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈密顿圈的存在性是代数图论中的核心开放问题，源于Lovász 1969年的猜想。阶为pq的连通顶点传递图除Petersen图外均为哈密顿图，阶为2pq的原始图除Coxeter图外已解决。本文旨在推进阶为2pq的非准原始图类的哈密顿性问题研究。

Method: 通过分析连通顶点传递图的传递自同构子群结构，聚焦于存在诱导素数长度轨道（即长度p或q）的极大非传递正规子群的情况，利用正规子群诱导的商图与块图结构进行分析，将问题归约为已知可解情形，从而证明哈密顿圈的存在性。

Result: 证明了在所有满足该正规子群条件的连通顶点传递图中，哈密顿圈必然存在，且不产生超出已有非合格图之外的新例外情况。该结果覆盖了阶为2pq的一大类非准原始图。

Conclusion: 该工作显著推进了阶为2pq的连通顶点传递图哈密顿性问题的完整解决，为最终攻克Lovász猜想在2pq阶情形下的全部情形提供了重要进展。

Abstract: The existence of Hamilton cycles in connected vertex-transitive graphs is a core open problem in algebraic graph theory, originating from Lovász's 1969 conjecture. All connected vertex-transitive graphs of order $pq$ are known to be Hamiltonian except the Petersen graph, and primitive graph of order $2pq$ are resolved except the Coxeter graph. This paper considers connected vertex-transitive graphs of order $2pq$ where every transitive automorphism subgroup admits a maximal intransitive normal subgroup inducing prime-length orbits. We prove that all such graphs contain a Hamilton cycle, with no new exceptions beyond the already characterized non-qualifying graphs. This result covers a large non-quasiprimitive graphs of order $2pq$, advancing the full resolution of the $2pq$.

</details>


### [185] [The half interlacing property among the types A, B and D Eulerian polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02357)
*Shi-Mei Ma*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过补充作者2012年对Brenti猜想（D型Eulerian多项式D_n(x)的实根性）首个证明的细节，进一步得到了A、B、D型Eulerian多项式之间的半交错性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brenti在1994年猜想D型Eulerian多项式D_n(x)具有实根性。此前已有多种证明方法（Savage和Visontai的相容多项式方法、Bränden的交错保持矩阵方法、Yang和Zhang的Hermite-Biehler定理方法、Hyatt的半Eulerian多项式方法）。Brenti本人曾建议可以从A型和B型Eulerian多项式零点的精确位置来证明D型的实根性。本文旨在完善作者2012年给出的首条证明路径，并揭示A、B、D型多项式之间的半交错联系。

Method: 作者对2012年证明D_n(x)实根性的方法进行了详细补充和阐述，该方法基于Brenti的建议，利用A型和B型Eulerian多项式的零点分布信息来建立D型多项式的实根性。通过细致的分析，进一步推导出A、B、D三种类型的Eulerian多项式之间满足半交错性质（half interlacing property）。

Result: 补充完善了D_n(x)实根性的首个证明的细节，并得到了A、B、D型Eulerian多项式之间的半交错性质。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅验证了Brenti 1994年的猜想和其最初建议的证明思路，还揭示了三种类型Eulerian多项式之间更深层的零点交错结构关系。

Abstract: A famous result in the theory of combinatorial polynomials is the real-rootedness of the type $D$ Eulerian polynomial $D_n(x)$, which was originally conjectured by Brenti in 1994. By constructing a set of compatible polynomials over $s$-inversion sequences, Savage and Visontai proved this conjecture in 2013. Using matrices preserving interlacing properties of nonnegative polynomial sequences, Bränden also established the real-rootedness of $D_n(x)$. Combining Hermite-Biehler theorem and a result of Borcea and Brändén on Hurwitz stability, Yang and Zhang gave another proof of the real-rootedness of $D_n(x)$. By constructing half Eulerian polynomials of type $D$, Hyatt reproved Brenti's conjecture. As originally suggested by Brenti in 1994, it is possible that the real-rootedness of $D_n(x)$ may be established by using a more precise knowledge of the location of zeros of the types $A$ and $B$ Eulerian polynomials. In this paper, we add more details to the first proof of the real-rootedness of $D_n(x)$ that was provided by the author in 2012, which yields the half interlacing property among the types $A,B$ and $D$ Eulerian polynomials.

</details>


### [186] [Graph Eigenvalues and Projection Constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02429)
*Varun Sivashankar,Quanyu Tang,Tanay Wakhare*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于任意n顶点图G，第k大邻接特征值λ_k(G)满足一个统一的上界：λ_k(G) ≤ ((k-2)√(k+1)+2)/(2k(k-1))·n - 1，该界在k∈{2,3,4,8,24}时是紧的。证明方法是将图谱问题转化为正交投影的极值问题，并应用Deręgowska和Lewandowska关于绝对投影常数γ(r)的上界；同时给出了该上界的一个替代证明（修复了König和Tomczak-Jaegermann的Gegenbauer多项式论证），所得的slack恒等式在维度r+2不是完全平方数的所有偶数维r≥4中给出了严格改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立图邻接特征值的通用上界。第k大邻接特征值λ_k(G)在图谱理论中具有重要意义，对理解图的结构性质（如色数、独立数等）有直接影响。作者试图找到一个对所有图普遍成立、并且对于某些特定的k值可以达到最优（tight）的界限。此外，通过改进绝对投影常数γ(r)的上界，可以进一步提升特征值界限的精度。

Method: 作者采用了两步转换的方法：首先将求图邻接特征值上界的问题，约化为正交投影的极值问题——这是一个已知的等价关系。然后，应用Deręgowska和Lewandowska对绝对投影常数γ(r)建立的通用上界来求解该极值问题。此外，作者还修改了König和Tomczak-Jaegermann的Gegenbauer多项式论证方法，给出了γ(r)上界的替代证明，最终导出一个slack恒等式，从而在特定维度条件下获得严格改进。

Result: 证明了对所有n顶点图和所有2≤k≤n，λ_k(G) ≤ ((k-2)√(k+1)+2)/(2k(k-1))·n - 1。该上界在k∈{2,3,4,8,24}时是紧的。此外，通过slack恒等式，在r+2不是完全平方数的偶数维r≥4情形下，对绝对投影常数γ(r)的上界给出了严格改进。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了图第k大邻接特征值的通用上界，并通过投影常数理论给出了紧性分析。结论表明该界在最经典的小参数情形（k=2,3,4,8,24）下是最优的，同时揭示了图特征值问题与投影常数之间深刻的联系。对投影常数上界的改进也为未来进一步精确化该特征值界提供了可能。

Abstract: For an integer $k\ge2$, let $λ_k(G)$ denote the $k$th largest adjacency eigenvalue of a graph $G$. For every graph $G$ on $n$ vertices and every $2 \leq k \leq n$, we prove \[ λ_k(G) \le \frac{(k-2)\sqrt{k+1}+2}{2k(k-1)}\,n-1. \] Our bound is tight for $k\in\{2,3,4,8,24\}$. We obtain it by reducing the graph-eigenvalue problem to an extremal problem for orthogonal projections and then applying the general upper bound on the absolute projection constant $γ(r)$ due to Deręgowska and Lewandowska. We also give an alternative proof of their bound by repairing the Gegenbauer-polynomial argument of König and Tomczak-Jaegermann. The resulting slack identity yields a strict improvement in every even dimension $r\ge4$ for which $r+2$ is not a perfect square.

</details>


### [187] [Minkowski decomposability of symmetric edge polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02445)
*Akihiro Higashitani,Aki Mori*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限简单图G的对称边多面体P_G^±的Minkowski可分解性，给出了完整的刻画：P_G^±是Minkowski可分解的当且仅当G是三种完全多部图之一：K_n、K_{2,n-2}或K_{1,1,n-2}；否则P_G^±是Minkowski不可分解的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称边多面体是一类重要的组合对象，其Minkowski分解性质与图的结构密切相关。系统地刻画哪些图的对称边多面体可分解，有助于深入理解多面体几何与图论之间的联系。

Method: 通过分析顶点集为[n]的有限简单图G，对对称边多面体P_G^±的Minkowski可分解性进行分类研究，给出了完整且精确的组合刻画。

Result: 证明了P_G^±是Minkowski可分解的当且仅当G属于三个完全多部图族：K_n、K_{2,n-2}或K_{1,1,n-2}；除这三种情况外，P_G^±均为Minkowski不可分解的。

Conclusion: 对称边多面体的Minkowski可分解性完全由图的类型决定，仅有三个例外图族，结果提供了该问题的一个完整分类。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the Minkowski decomposability of symmetric edge polytopes $P_G^\pm$ of a finite simple graph $G$ on vertex set $[n]$. More precisely, we give a complete characterization of graphs whose symmetric edge polytopes are Minkowski decomposable. We prove that $P_G^\pm$ is Minkowski decomposable if and only if $G$ is one of the three complete multipartite graphs: $K_n$, $K_{2,n-2}$, or $K_{1,1,n-2}$. In other words, if $G$ does not belong to these three families, then $P_G^\pm$ is Minkowski indecomposable.

</details>


### [188] [A simplified min-max formula for the inverse arborescence problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02496)
*András Frank,Hanna Szabrina Horváth*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个简单的极大极小定理，用于刻画使目标有向树F_0成为最便宜有向树所需的最小代价函数修改（以l_1范数度量），并给出了多项式时间算法来构造最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在给定有向图和一个目标有向树（arborescence）的情况下，如何最小程度地修改输入代价函数，使得该目标有向树成为最便宜的有向树。这涉及到逆向优化问题，即希望以最小的泛函扰动实现特定的最优性目标。

Method: 通过构造性证明建立min-max定理，将最小化问题（寻找最优的修改代价函数）与最大化问题（寻找对偶对象）联系起来。构造性证明直接给出了多项式时间算法，同时计算原始侧的最小化代价函数和对偶侧的最大化对象。

Result: 成功证明了min-max定理，并由此推导出多项式时间算法，该算法能够同时输出最优的修改代价函数（原始侧极小器）和最优的对偶目标（对偶侧极大器）。

Conclusion: 本文为有向树的最廉价化问题提供了一个简洁的min-max刻画，并通过构造性证明获得了多项式时间算法，为逆向优化在有向图上的应用提供了理论基础和计算工具。

Abstract: A simple min-max theorem is formulated and proved for the smallest modification (measured in $l_1$-norm) of an input cost function $w_0$ that makes a target arborescence $F_0$ of a digraph a cheapest arborescence. The constructive proof gives rise to a polynomial time algorithm for computing both a minimizer cost function on the primal side and a maximizer dual object in the min-max formula.

</details>


### [189] [The Erdős-Hajnal conjecture for odd-girth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02522)
*Raphael Steiner*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文基于OpenAI关于多色Ramsey数的近期下界，将Erdős-Hajnal猜想中g=4的情况（Rödl 1977年证明）的界从塔式高度$Θ(k^2\log k)$改进为单指数界$f_4(k)\le e^{k^{3+o(1)}}$；并将其推广到奇数围长g≥5的情形，证明了Mohar和Wu 2018年提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős-Hajnal 1969年猜想指出对每个g≥4存在函数$f_g$使得色数足够大的图必含有色数至少k且围长至少g的子图。该猜想仅对g=4由Rödl在1977年证明，且给出的上界是高度为$Θ(k^2\log k)$的塔式巨大界。改进这一巨大界是长期悬而未决的问题。同时，奇数围长版本的相应猜想由Mohar和Wu在2018年提出，此前未获证明。

Method: 作者利用OpenAI近期对三角形多色Ramsey数的下界（arXiv:2412.12059），直接推导出$f_4(k)\le e^{k^{3+o(1)}}$的单指数上界；然后利用其companion paper中将该结果推广到奇圈多色Ramsey数的结果，对每个奇数$g\ge5$构造了一个以高度$\frac{g-3}{2}$的幂塔增长的函数$h_g$，证明色数至少$h_g(k)$的图必含色数至少k且奇围长至少g的子图。

Result: 对g=4的情形，将Rödl的塔式上界改进为单指数上界$f_4(k)\le e^{k^{3+o(1)}}$。对每个奇数$g\ge5$，证明了存在幂塔高度为$\frac{g-3}{2}$的函数$h_g$，使得色数至少$h_g(k)$的图包含色数至少k且奇围长至少g的子图，验证了Mohar和Wu的猜想。

Conclusion: 这项工作显著改善了Erdős-Hajnal猜想在g=4时的已知上界，并首次证明了奇数围长版本的Mohar-Wu猜想。它将多色Ramsey数的最新突破与图的色数-围长问题结合起来，为该领域提供了新的技术途径。
