Abstract: A famous conjecture of Erdős and Hajnal from 1969 states that for every integer $g\ge 4$ there exists a (smallest) function $f_g:\mathbb{N}\rightarrow \mathbb{N}$ such that every graph of chromatic number at least $f_g(k)$ contains a subgraph with chromatic number at least $k$ and girth at least $g$.
  So far, this has only been proved for $g=4$ by Rödl in 1977 and remains open for every $g\ge 5$. Rödl's elegant proof yields an upper bound on $f_4(k)$ which is a tower of $k$-s of height $Θ(k^2\log k)$, suggesting the problem of improving this enormous bound. We deduce a single-exponential bound $$f_4(k)\le e^{k^{3+o(1)}}$$ from OpenAI's recent lower bound on multicolor Ramsey numbers of triangles.
  Using a generalization of the latter result to multi-color Ramsey numbers of odd cycles from a companion paper, we show that for every odd $g\ge 5$ there is a function $h_g:\mathbb{N}\rightarrow \mathbb{N}$ growing at most as a power tower of height $\frac{g-3}{2}$ such that every graph of chromatic number at least $h_g(k)$ has a subgraph of chromatic number at least $k$ and odd-girth at least $g$. This proves a conjecture of Mohar and Wu from 2018.

</details>


### [190] [Cyclic Sources of Strong Domination in Graph Norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02526)
*Shuyan Chen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个双参数族的两连通强支配图，它们不是半规范图，从而超出了此前已识别的两类（规范图和偶路径）示例。该构造基于双根块的循环合并，并利用转移算子的局部偶Schatten不等式刻画了根可逆块生成所有偶循环合并的判据。文中确定了对K_{2,m}（根在大小为m的部分）该判据恰在m为偶数时成立，并分类了连通外平面强支配图及满足普适循环判据的连通根可逆外平面块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conlon和Lee提出了在规范图和偶路径之外寻找强支配图的问题。此前已知的强支配图示例仅限于规范图和偶路径两类，需要构造新的示例以丰富该领域，并理解强支配图的结构特征。

Method: 通过双根块的循环合并来构造双参数族的两连通强支配图。利用转移算子的局部偶Schatten不等式，刻画根可逆块生成所有偶循环合并的条件；对K_{2,m}具体计算该判据；并对连通外平面图进行分类。

Result: 构造了一族非半规范的强支配图；确定了K_{2,m}满足普适循环判据当且仅当m为偶数；完全分类了连通外平面强支配图以及满足普适循环判据的连通根可逆外平面块。

Conclusion: 本文通过循环合并构造了超越已知类别的新强支配图族，并给出了相关图类的完整分类，回答了Conlon和Lee的开放问题。

Abstract: Conlon and Lee asked for strongly dominating graphs beyond norming graphs and even paths. We construct a two-parameter family of pairwise non-isomorphic $2$-connected strongly dominating graphs that are not seminorming, and hence lie outside the two classes of examples previously identified for signed strong domination. The construction uses cyclic amalgamation of two-rooted blocks. For root-reversible blocks, we characterize the generation of all even cyclic amalgams by local even-Schatten inequalities for transfer operators. We determine this criterion for $K_{2,m}$, with the roots in the part of size $m$: it holds exactly when $m$ is even. We also classify the connected outerplanar strongly dominating graphs and the connected root-reversible outerplanar blocks satisfying the universal cyclic criterion.

</details>


### [191] [Multicolor Ramsey numbers of odd cycles are superexponential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02537)
*Raphael Steiner*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文改进了OpenAI关于三角形k色Ramsey数的递归构造，将其推广到固定奇圈的multicolor Ramsey数，证明了R_k(𝒪_p) ≥ (log^{(p-1)}k)^{k/3-o(k)}，从而说明任意固定奇圈的multicolor Ramsey数关于颜色数都是超指数的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenAI最近证明了三角k色Ramsey数以超指数方式增长（R_k(C_3) ≥ k^{k/3-o(k)}）。本文的动机是将这一突破性结果从三角形推广到更一般的固定奇圈，回答关于奇圈multicolor Ramsey数下界的问题。

Method: 作者修改了OpenAI的递归构造方法，将其应用于固定奇圈集合𝒪_p={C_3,C_5,...,C_{2p+1}}。通过引入(p-1)重迭代对数，构造了满足所需性质的颜色图，并利用递归过程建立了下界R_k(𝒪_p) ≥ (log^{(p-1)}k)^{k/3-o(k)}。

Result: 对于任意固定p，证明了R_k(𝒪_p) ≥ (log^{(p-1)}k)^{k/3-o(k)}。这一结果立即推出对任意固定奇圈，multicolor Ramsey数是关于颜色数的超指数函数。

Conclusion: 本文成功将三角multicolor Ramsey数的超指数下界推广至所有固定奇圈情形，填补了奇圈情形与三角形情形的推广空白。值得注意的额外信息是，该证明由ChatGPT 5.6 Pro/Sol自主发现。

Abstract: In a recent breakthrough, OpenAI proved that the $k$-color Ramsey number of the triangle $C_3$ grows super-exponentially, more precisely, they proved that $R_k(C_3)\ge k^{k/3-o(k)}$. In this short note, we present a modification of their recursive construction that works for multicolor Ramsey numbers of fixed odd cycles. More precisely, for $p\ge 1$, let $\mathcal{O}_p=\{C_3,C_5,\ldots,C_{2p+1}\}$. We show that \[
  R_k(\mathcal{O}_p)\ge (\log^{(p-1)}k)^{k/3-o(k)} \] for every fixed $p$, where $\log^{(p-1)}$ denotes the $(p-1)$-fold iterated logarithm. This immediately implies that for every fixed odd cycle, the multicolor Ramsey number is superexponential in the number of colors.
  The presented proof was found autonomously by ChatGPT 5.6 Pro/Sol.

</details>


### [192] [On (1,1,2,3)- and (1,1,3,3,3)-Packing Colorings of Claw-Free Subcubic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02566)
*Maidoun Mortada,Ayman El Zein,Sara Al Hajjar*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了每个无爪次立方图都是(1,1,2,3)-可填充着色的，且每个连通无爪次立方图除单一图H外都是(1,1,3,3,3)-可填充着色的，两个结果均为最优的，并证实了前两位作者的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gastineau和Togni提出了一个开放问题：除Petersen图外，每个次立方图是否都是(1,1,2,3)-可填充着色的。作者旨在解决该问题的无爪图情形，并验证前两位作者提出的关于(1,1,3,3,3)-填充着色的猜想。

Method: 作者建立了一个基于无爪次立方图的骨架（skeleton）图和核（core）图的结构框架，并结合一个Hall型匹配论证，将构造合适的3-填充（3-packings）问题转化为辅助二部图中的匹配问题。

Result: 证明了每个无爪次立方图都是(1,1,2,3)-可填充着色的；且每个连通无爪次立方图除单个图H外都是(1,1,3,3,3)-可填充着色的，从而确认了前两位作者的猜想。两个结果都被证明是最好可能的。

Conclusion: 该研究部分回答了Gastineau和Togni提出的问题，确认了无爪次立方图的(1,1,3,3,3)-填充着色猜想，并为次立方图填充着色问题提供了新的结构证明工具。

Abstract: For a non-decreasing sequence $S=(a_1,a_2,\ldots,a_r)$ of positive integers, an $S$-packing coloring of a graph $G$ is a partition of $V(G)$ into sets $A_1,\ldots,A_r$ such that any two distinct vertices in $A_i$ are at distance greater than $a_i$, for every $i\in\{1,\ldots,r\}$. Gastineau and Togni [\emph{Discrete Math.} 339 (2016), 2461--2470] asked whether every subcubic graph, except the Petersen graph, is $(1,1,2,3)$-packing colorable. In this paper, we prove that every claw-free subcubic graph is $(1,1,2,3)$-packing colorable. Moreover, we show that every connected claw-free subcubic graph, except a single graph $\mathcal{H}$, is $(1,1,3,3,3)$-packing colorable, thereby confirming a conjecture of the first two authors. Both results are best possible. Our proofs rely on a structural framework based on the skeleton and core graphs of a claw-free subcubic graph, together with a Hall-type matching argument that reduces the construction of suitable $3$-packings to a matching problem in an auxiliary bipartite graph.

</details>
